```rust
//! Colours. The terminal keeps its own background; only foregrounds are set. Brand: signal lime
//! `#C8F25A` on dark, `#4D7A00` on light (docs/BRAND.md). Meaning is never colour-only.
use klip_core::KlipPaths;
use ratatui::style::{Color, Modifier, Style};

/// What the TUI needs to know about its surroundings.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct TuiEnv {
    pub paths: KlipPaths,
    pub no_color: bool,
    pub truecolor: bool,
    pub colors256: bool,
    pub light: bool,
    pub mouse: bool,
}

impl TuiEnv {
    pub fn from_process(paths: KlipPaths) -> Self {
        Self::from_vars(paths, |k| std::env::var(k).ok())
    }

    pub fn from_vars(paths: KlipPaths, get: impl Fn(&str) -> Option<String>) -> Self {
        let no_color = get("NO_COLOR").map(|v| !v.is_empty()).unwrap_or(false);
        let colorterm = get("COLORTERM").unwrap_or_default().to_lowercase();
        let term = get("TERM").unwrap_or_default();
        let truecolor = colorterm.contains("truecolor") || colorterm.contains("24bit");
        let colors256 = truecolor || term.contains("256color");
        let light = get("KLIP_THEME")
            .map(|v| v.eq_ignore_ascii_case("light"))
            .unwrap_or(false);
        Self { paths, no_color, truecolor, colors256, light, mouse: true }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy)]
pub struct Theme {
    pub signal: Color,
    pub warn: Color,
    pub bad: Color,
    pub text: Color,
    pub dim: Color,
    pub faint: Color,
    /// `NO_COLOR`: no colours at all; emphasis comes from bold/dim/reverse only.
    pub plain: bool,
}

impl Theme {
    pub fn from_env(env: &TuiEnv) -> Self {
        if env.no_color {
            return Self {
                signal: Color::Reset,
                warn: Color::Reset,
                bad: Color::Reset,
                text: Color::Reset,
                dim: Color::Reset,
                faint: Color::Reset,
                plain: true,
            };
        }
        let (signal, warn, bad, text, dim, faint) = if env.truecolor {
            if env.light {
                (
                    Color::Rgb(77, 122, 0),
                    Color::Rgb(168, 95, 0),
                    Color::Rgb(179, 38, 30),
                    Color::Rgb(18, 22, 26),
                    Color::Rgb(90, 100, 107),
                    Color::Rgb(140, 151, 159),
                )
            } else {
                (
                    Color::Rgb(200, 242, 90),
                    Color::Rgb(255, 184, 77),
                    Color::Rgb(255, 107, 107),
                    Color::Rgb(232, 237, 240),
                    Color::Rgb(140, 151, 159),
                    Color::Rgb(90, 100, 107),
                )
            }
        } else if env.colors256 {
            if env.light {
                (
                    Color::Indexed(64),
                    Color::Indexed(130),
                    Color::Indexed(124),
                    Color::Indexed(234),
                    Color::Indexed(240),
                    Color::Indexed(245),
                )
            } else {
                (
                    Color::Indexed(192),
                    Color::Indexed(215),
                    Color::Indexed(203),
                    Color::Indexed(255),
                    Color::Indexed(245),
                    Color::Indexed(240),
                )
            }
        } else {
            (
                Color::Green,
                Color::Yellow,
                Color::Red,
                Color::Reset,
                Color::DarkGray,
                Color::DarkGray,
            )
        };
        Self { signal, warn, bad, text, dim, faint, plain: false }
    }

    fn fg(&self, c: Color, plain_mods: Modifier) -> Style {
        if self.plain {
            Style::default().add_modifier(plain_mods)
        } else {
            Style::default().fg(c)
        }
    }

    pub fn st_signal(&self) -> Style {
        self.fg(self.signal, Modifier::BOLD)
    }
    pub fn st_warn(&self) -> Style {
        self.fg(self.warn, Modifier::BOLD)
    }
    pub fn st_bad(&self) -> Style {
        self.fg(self.bad, Modifier::BOLD | Modifier::REVERSED)
    }
    pub fn st_text(&self) -> Style {
        self.fg(self.text, Modifier::empty())
    }
    pub fn st_dim(&self) -> Style {
        self.fg(self.dim, Modifier::DIM)
    }
    pub fn st_faint(&self) -> Style {
        self.fg(self.faint, Modifier::DIM)
    }
    /// The selected row of a list.
    pub fn st_selected(&self) -> Style {
        if self.plain {
            Style::default().add_modifier(Modifier::REVERSED)
        } else {
            Style::default().fg(self.signal).add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD | Modifier::REVERSED)
        }
    }
    pub fn st_title(&self) -> Style {
        self.st_signal().add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD)
    }
}
```

```rust
//! File-based helpers: the daemon's config and log live in the Klip directory and are read directly
//! (same user, private permissions), so these screens work even when the daemon is not running.
use klip_core::{DaemonConfig, KlipPaths};
use std::fs;
use std::io::{Read, Seek, SeekFrom, Write};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};

/// The log is read from this far back when the Log screen opens.
pub const LOG_INITIAL_BYTES: u64 = 256 * 1024;
/// A line longer than this without a newline is shown anyway instead of waiting forever.
const LOG_MAX_PARTIAL: usize = 64 * 1024;
/// Send's "load a file" refuses anything bigger.
pub const MAX_LOADED_FILE_BYTES: u64 = 64 * 1024 * 1024;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct LogChunk {
    /// Where the next read starts.
    pub new_offset: u64,
    /// Complete lines only (each ends with `\n`), lossily decoded.
    pub text: String,
    /// The file became shorter than the previous offset (truncated or rotated): start over.
    pub reset: bool,
    /// There is no log file (yet).
    pub missing: bool,
}

pub fn log_path(paths: &KlipPaths) -> PathBuf {
    paths.dir().join("daemon.log")
}

/// Read what was appended to the log since `offset`; `None` means "first read": the last 256 KiB,
/// starting at a line boundary. An unfinished last line is left for the next read.
pub fn read_log_chunk(path: &Path, offset: Option<u64>) -> Result<LogChunk, String> {
    let mut file = match fs::File::open(path) {
        Ok(f) => f,
        Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => {
            return Ok(LogChunk { new_offset: 0, text: String::new(), reset: offset.is_some(), missing: true })
        }
        Err(e) => return Err(format!("{}: {e}", path.display())),
    };
    let len = file.metadata().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?.len();
    let (start, reset) = match offset {
        None => (len.saturating_sub(LOG_INITIAL_BYTES), false),
        Some(o) if o > len => (0, true),
        Some(o) => (o, false),
    };
    file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(start)).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let mut buf = Vec::new();
    file.take(len - start).read_to_end(&mut buf).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;

    // First read in the middle of a line: drop the partial first line.
    let mut skip = 0;
    if offset.is_none() && start > 0 {
        skip = buf.iter().position(|b| *b == b'\n').map(|i| i + 1).unwrap_or(buf.len());
    }
    let data = &buf[skip..];
    let take = match data.iter().rposition(|b| *b == b'\n') {
        Some(i) => i + 1,
        None if data.len() >= LOG_MAX_PARTIAL => data.len(),
        None => 0,
    };
    Ok(LogChunk {
        new_offset: start + (skip + take) as u64,
        text: String::from_utf8_lossy(&data[..take]).into_owned(),
        reset,
        missing: false,
    })
}

/// Validate, then write `config.json`: temp file (mode 0600) → fsync → rename over the target.
/// The old file stays untouched when validation or any write step fails.
pub fn write_config_atomic(paths: &KlipPaths, cfg: &DaemonConfig) -> Result<(), String> {
    cfg.validate()?;
    let target = paths.config_file();
    let tmp = paths.dir().join(".config.json.tmp");
    let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(cfg).map_err(|e| e.to_string())? + "\n";
    let mut opts = fs::OpenOptions::new();
    opts.write(true).create(true).truncate(true);
    {
        use std::os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt;
        opts.mode(0o600);
    }
    let mut file = opts.open(&tmp).map_err(|e| format!("{}: {e}", tmp.display()))?;
    let written = file
        .write_all(json.as_bytes())
        .and_then(|_| file.sync_all())
        .map_err(|e| format!("{}: {e}", tmp.display()));
    if let Err(e) = written {
        let _ = fs::remove_file(&tmp);
        return Err(e);
    }
    fs::rename(&tmp, &target).map_err(|e| {
        let _ = fs::remove_file(&tmp);
        format!("{}: {e}", target.display())
    })
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum ServiceState {
    /// This OS has no unit file the CLI knows how to install.
    Unsupported,
    NotInstalled,
    Installed(PathBuf),
}

/// Where `klip install-service` writes its unit file on this OS.
pub fn service_unit_path(home: &Path) -> Option<PathBuf> {
    if cfg!(target_os = "linux") {
        Some(home.join(".config/systemd/user/klip.service"))
    } else if cfg!(target_os = "macos") {
        Some(home.join("Library/LaunchAgents/com.klip.daemon.plist"))
    } else {
        None
    }
}

pub fn service_state(home: Option<&Path>) -> ServiceState {
    match home.and_then(service_unit_path) {
        None => ServiceState::Unsupported,
        Some(p) if p.exists() => ServiceState::Installed(p),
        Some(_) => ServiceState::NotInstalled,
    }
}

/// Read a UTF-8 text file for the Send screen. `~/` is expanded; directories, files over `max_bytes`
/// and non-UTF-8 content are refused with a plain message.
pub fn load_text_file(path: &str, max_bytes: u64) -> Result<String, String> {
    let expanded = if let Some(rest) = path.strip_prefix("~/") {
        match std::env::var_os("HOME") {
            Some(home) => PathBuf::from(home).join(rest),
            None => PathBuf::from(path),
        }
    } else {
        PathBuf::from(path)
    };
    let meta = fs::metadata(&expanded).map_err(|e| format!("{}: {e}", expanded.display()))?;
    if !meta.is_file() {
        return Err(format!("{} is not a file", expanded.display()));
    }
    if meta.len() > max_bytes {
        return Err(format!("{} is too large ({} bytes; the limit is {max_bytes})", expanded.display(), meta.len()));
    }
    let bytes = fs::read(&expanded).map_err(|e| format!("{}: {e}", expanded.display()))?;
    String::from_utf8(bytes).map_err(|_| format!("{} is not valid UTF-8 text", expanded.display()))
}
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {

    use crate::api::*;

    async fn the_fake_records_calls_and_behaves_like_a_daemon() {
```

```rust
//! The boundary between the TUI and the daemon. `klip-cli` implements [`DaemonApi`] over the control
//! socket; tests use `testkit::FakeDaemon`.
use async_trait::async_trait;
pub use klip_proto::ipc::{
    ConfigInfo, DaemonStatusInfo, HelloInfo, HistoryItem, PairPollInfo, PeerStatusInfo, PendingPairInfo,
};

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum ApiError {
    /// No daemon answers on the control socket.
    DaemonDown,
    /// The daemon did not answer within this many seconds.
    Timeout(u64),
    /// The daemon is older than the call (it answered "unknown request").
    Unsupported,
    /// The request is bigger than the daemon accepts.
    TooLarge { size: usize, max: usize },
    /// The daemon understood and said no, with its own reason.
    Refused(String),
    Io(String),
}

impl ApiError {
    /// One short sentence for a toast or a notice.
    pub fn plain(&self) -> String {
        match self {
            Self::DaemonDown => "Klip is not running".to_string(),
            Self::Timeout(s) => format!("Klip did not answer within {s} s"),
            Self::Unsupported => "the running daemon is older: restart it after upgrading".to_string(),
            Self::TooLarge { size, max } => format!(
                "{} is too large; one request can carry at most {}",
                crate::fmt::bytes(*size),
                crate::fmt::bytes(*max)
            ),
            Self::Refused(m) | Self::Io(m) => m.clone(),
        }
    }
}

/// Result of `klip send`: which devices got the text and which did not (with the reason).
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct SendReport {
    pub bytes: usize,
    pub delivered: Vec<String>,
    pub failed: Vec<(String, String)>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct PairTicket {
    pub ticket: String,
    pub expires_in_secs: u64,
    pub host_fingerprint: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct Paired {
    pub name: String,
    pub fingerprint: String,
}

#[async_trait]
pub trait DaemonApi: Send + Sync {
    /// Protocol-2 handshake. `Err(Unsupported)` on a daemon that predates it.
    async fn hello(&self) -> Result<HelloInfo, ApiError>;
    async fn status(&self) -> Result<DaemonStatusInfo, ApiError>;
    async fn history(&self, limit: usize) -> Result<Vec<HistoryItem>, ApiError>;
    async fn send(&self, text: String) -> Result<SendReport, ApiError>;
    /// Returns the seconds the pause will last.
    async fn pause(&self, secs: u64) -> Result<u64, ApiError>;
    async fn resume(&self) -> Result<(), ApiError>;
    /// `selector` is a name or the start of a fingerprint. Returns the removed device's name.
    async fn unpair(&self, selector: String) -> Result<String, ApiError>;
    /// `(removed names, (name, reason) failures)`.
    async fn unpair_all(&self) -> Result<(Vec<String>, Vec<(String, String)>), ApiError>;
    async fn copy_to_clipboard(&self, id: [u8; 16]) -> Result<(), ApiError>;
    async fn clear_history(&self) -> Result<(), ApiError>;
    async fn pair_start(&self, ttl_secs: u64, lan: bool) -> Result<PairTicket, ApiError>;
    async fn pair_poll(&self) -> Result<PairPollInfo, ApiError>;
    async fn pair_decide(&self, accept: bool) -> Result<PairPollInfo, ApiError>;
    async fn pair_cancel(&self) -> Result<(), ApiError>;
    async fn pair_join(&self, ticket: String) -> Result<Paired, ApiError>;
    async fn stop_daemon(&self) -> Result<(), ApiError>;
    /// Start the daemon in the background; returns its process id.
    async fn start_daemon(&self) -> Result<u32, ApiError>;
    /// Does what `klip install-service` does; returns the message to show.
    async fn install_service(&self) -> Result<String, ApiError>;
}
```

```rust
//! Test support for this crate and for klip-cli's TUI tests (feature `testkit`).
use crate::api::*;
use async_trait::async_trait;
use klip_proto::hlc::NodeId;
use klip_proto::ipc::{
    max_request_bytes, CAP_CLEAR_HISTORY, CAP_COPY_TO_CLIPBOARD, CAP_UNPAIR_ALL, IPC_PROTOCOL_VERSION,
};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::Duration;

pub fn sample_peer(name: &str, online: bool, seed: u8) -> PeerStatusInfo {
    PeerStatusInfo {
        node_id: NodeId::new([seed; 32]),
        name: name.to_string(),
        is_online: online,
        last_error: None,
    }
}

pub fn sample_status(peers: Vec<PeerStatusInfo>) -> DaemonStatusInfo {
    DaemonStatusInfo {
        is_active: true,
        is_paused: false,
        paused_remaining_secs: 0,
        platform: "Linux (X11)".to_string(),
        memory_rss_bytes: 16_600_000,
        node_id: NodeId::new([9; 32]),
        relay_mode: "n0 public relays + discovery".to_string(),
        relay_state: "relay connected (https://aps1-1.relay.n0.iroh.link./)".to_string(),
        pairing_open: false,
        peers,
        total_synced_clips: 128,
        last_sync_time_secs_ago: Some(4),
        last_clip_preview: Some("https://github.com/…".to_string()),
        last_clip_size_bytes: 38,
    }
}

pub fn sample_hello() -> HelloInfo {
    let max_clip = 32 * 1024 * 1024;
    HelloInfo {
        protocol: IPC_PROTOCOL_VERSION,
        daemon_version: "0.4.0".to_string(),
        capabilities: vec![
            CAP_UNPAIR_ALL.to_string(),
            CAP_COPY_TO_CLIPBOARD.to_string(),
            CAP_CLEAR_HISTORY.to_string(),
        ],
        config: ConfigInfo {
            device_name: "Laptop".to_string(),
            relay: "n0".to_string(),
            max_history_items: 50,
            max_clip_bytes: max_clip,
            lan_discovery: true,
        },
        max_request_bytes: max_request_bytes(max_clip),
    }
}

pub fn sample_item(n: u8, text: &str, sensitive: bool) -> HistoryItem {
    HistoryItem {
        id: [n; 16],
        origin_node: NodeId::new([1; 32]),
        origin_name: "Vivo V2253".to_string(),
        timestamp_ms: 1_700_000_000_000 + u64::from(n) * 1000,
        content_preview: text.chars().take(80).collect(),
        full_content: text.as_bytes().to_vec(),
        size_bytes: text.len(),
        is_sensitive: sensitive,
    }
}

pub struct FakeState {
    pub up: bool,
    pub status: DaemonStatusInfo,
    /// `None` behaves like a v0.3.6 daemon: no Hello, no copy-back, no clear.
    pub hello: Option<HelloInfo>,
    pub history: Vec<HistoryItem>,
    pub calls: Vec<String>,
    /// Returned (once) by the next call instead of its normal result.
    pub fail_next: Option<ApiError>,
    pub send_reply: Result<SendReport, ApiError>,
    pub ticket: PairTicket,
    pub poll: PairPollInfo,
    pub join_reply: Result<Paired, ApiError>,
    pub delay_ms: u64,
}

impl FakeState {
    fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            up: true,
            status: sample_status(vec![
                sample_peer("Vivo V2253", true, 1),
                sample_peer("Pixel 8", false, 2),
            ]),
            hello: Some(sample_hello()),
            history: vec![],
            calls: vec![],
            fail_next: None,
            send_reply: Ok(SendReport { bytes: 0, delivered: vec!["Vivo V2253".into()], failed: vec![] }),
            ticket: PairTicket {
                ticket: "klip1TESTTICKETTESTTICKETTESTTICKET".to_string(),
                expires_in_secs: 120,
                host_fingerprint: "AAAA-BBBB-CCCC".to_string(),
            },
            poll: PairPollInfo { window_open: true, seconds_left: 100, pending: None, paired: None },
            join_reply: Ok(Paired { name: "Pixel 8".into(), fingerprint: "DDDD-EEEE-FFFF".into() }),
            delay_ms: 0,
        }
    }
}

pub struct FakeDaemon {
    state: Mutex<FakeState>,
}

impl FakeDaemon {
    pub fn new() -> Arc<Self> {
        Arc::new(Self { state: Mutex::new(FakeState::new()) })
    }

    pub fn old_daemon() -> Arc<Self> {
        let d = Self::new();
        d.with(|s| s.hello = None);
        d
    }

    pub fn with<R>(&self, f: impl FnOnce(&mut FakeState) -> R) -> R {
        f(&mut self.state.lock().unwrap())
    }

    pub fn calls(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        self.state.lock().unwrap().calls.clone()
    }

    async fn enter(&self, call: &str) -> Result<(), ApiError> {
        let delay = {
            let mut s = self.state.lock().unwrap();
            s.calls.push(call.to_string());
            if let Some(e) = s.fail_next.take() {
                return Err(e);
            }
            if !s.up {
                return Err(ApiError::DaemonDown);
            }
            s.delay_ms
        };
        if delay > 0 {
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(delay)).await;
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    fn need_protocol_2(&self) -> Result<(), ApiError> {
        if self.state.lock().unwrap().hello.is_none() {
            Err(ApiError::Unsupported)
        } else {
            Ok(())
        }
    }
}

#[async_trait]
impl DaemonApi for FakeDaemon {
    async fn hello(&self) -> Result<HelloInfo, ApiError> {
        self.enter("hello").await?;
        self.state.lock().unwrap().hello.clone().ok_or(ApiError::Unsupported)
    }

    async fn status(&self) -> Result<DaemonStatusInfo, ApiError> {
        self.enter("status").await?;
        Ok(self.state.lock().unwrap().status.clone())
    }

    async fn history(&self, limit: usize) -> Result<Vec<HistoryItem>, ApiError> {
        self.enter("history").await?;
        Ok(self.state.lock().unwrap().history.iter().take(limit).cloned().collect())
    }

    async fn send(&self, text: String) -> Result<SendReport, ApiError> {
        self.enter(&format!("send {}", text.len())).await?;
        let mut s = self.state.lock().unwrap();
        let mut reply = s.send_reply.clone();
        if let Ok(r) = &mut reply {
            r.bytes = text.len();
        }
        reply
    }

    async fn pause(&self, secs: u64) -> Result<u64, ApiError> {
        self.enter(&format!("pause {secs}")).await?;
        let mut s = self.state.lock().unwrap();
        s.status.is_paused = true;
        s.status.paused_remaining_secs = secs;
        Ok(secs)
    }

    async fn resume(&self) -> Result<(), ApiError> {
        self.enter("resume").await?;
        let mut s = self.state.lock().unwrap();
        s.status.is_paused = false;
        s.status.paused_remaining_secs = 0;
        Ok(())
    }

    async fn unpair(&self, selector: String) -> Result<String, ApiError> {
        self.enter(&format!("unpair {selector}")).await?;
        let mut s = self.state.lock().unwrap();
        let hit = s.status.peers.iter().position(|p| {
            p.name == selector || p.node_id.fingerprint().replace('-', "").starts_with(&selector.replace('-', "").to_uppercase())
        });
        match hit {
            Some(i) => Ok(s.status.peers.remove(i).name),
            None => Err(ApiError::Refused(format!("no paired device matches '{selector}'"))),
        }
    }

    async fn unpair_all(&self) -> Result<(Vec<String>, Vec<(String, String)>), ApiError> {
        self.enter("unpair_all").await?;
        let mut s = self.state.lock().unwrap();
        let removed = s.status.peers.drain(..).map(|p| p.name).collect();
        Ok((removed, vec![]))
    }

    async fn copy_to_clipboard(&self, id: [u8; 16]) -> Result<(), ApiError> {
        self.enter("copy").await?;
        self.need_protocol_2()?;
        let s = self.state.lock().unwrap();
        if s.history.iter().any(|i| i.id == id) {
            Ok(())
        } else {
            Err(ApiError::Refused("that clip is no longer in the history".to_string()))
        }
    }

    async fn clear_history(&self) -> Result<(), ApiError> {
        self.enter("clear_history").await?;
        self.need_protocol_2()?;
        self.state.lock().unwrap().history.clear();
        Ok(())
    }

    async fn pair_start(&self, ttl_secs: u64, lan: bool) -> Result<PairTicket, ApiError> {
        self.enter(&format!("pair_start {ttl_secs} {lan}")).await?;
        let mut s = self.state.lock().unwrap();
        s.status.pairing_open = true;
        Ok(s.ticket.clone())
    }

    async fn pair_poll(&self) -> Result<PairPollInfo, ApiError> {
        self.enter("pair_poll").await?;
        Ok(self.state.lock().unwrap().poll.clone())
    }

    async fn pair_decide(&self, accept: bool) -> Result<PairPollInfo, ApiError> {
        self.enter(&format!("pair_decide {accept}")).await?;
        let mut s = self.state.lock().unwrap();
        s.poll.pending = None;
        Ok(s.poll.clone())
    }

    async fn pair_cancel(&self) -> Result<(), ApiError> {
        self.enter("pair_cancel").await?;
        let mut s = self.state.lock().unwrap();
        s.status.pairing_open = false;
        s.poll.window_open = false;
        Ok(())
    }

    async fn pair_join(&self, ticket: String) -> Result<Paired, ApiError> {
        self.enter(&format!("pair_join {ticket}")).await?;
        self.state.lock().unwrap().join_reply.clone()
    }

    async fn stop_daemon(&self) -> Result<(), ApiError> {
        self.enter("stop_daemon").await?;
        self.state.lock().unwrap().up = false;
        Ok(())
    }

    async fn start_daemon(&self) -> Result<u32, ApiError> {
        let mut s = self.state.lock().unwrap();
        s.calls.push("start_daemon".to_string());
        s.up = true;
        Ok(4242)
    }

    async fn install_service(&self) -> Result<String, ApiError> {
        self.enter("install_service").await?;
        Ok("Installed systemd unit: /home/test/.config/systemd/user/klip.service".to_string())
    }
}
```

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `api::{ApiError, DaemonStatusInfo, HelloInfo}`, `fmt`, `textbuf::TextBuf`, `testkit::sample_status` (tests).
- Produces (used by every later task):

```rust
mod tests {
    use super::*;

        let mut a = app();

        assert_eq!(pause_choices(true).len(), 6);
        update(&mut a, ch('p'));

        assert!(matches!(fx.as_slice(), [Effect::Pause(3600)]), "{fx:?}");
        assert!(a.modal.is_none());

        }
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter)); // Custom…
        assert!(a.text_focus());
        update(&mut a, ch('0'));
        let fx = update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        assert!(fx.is_empty() && a.modal.is_some(), "zero seconds is refused");
        assert!(a.toasts.iter().any(|t| t.kind == ToastKind::Error));
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Backspace));
        for c in "9x0".chars() {
            update(&mut a, ch(c));

        assert!(matches!(tick(&mut a, 1000).as_slice(), [Effect::Status]));
        assert!(tick(&mut a, 1250).is_empty());
        assert!(tick(&mut a, 2500).is_empty(), "a request is still in flight");

            body: String::new(),
            action: ConfirmAction::StopDaemon,

        a.inflight.status = true;
        update(&mut a, Msg::Reply(Reply::Status(Err(ApiError::DaemonDown))));
        assert!(matches!(a.conn, Conn::Down { .. }));
        assert!(a.status.is_none());
        assert!(matches!(a.modal, Some(Modal::Notice { .. })), "the decision cannot be applied any more");

        a.inflight.status = true;
        let fx = update(&mut a, Msg::Reply(Reply::Status(Ok(sample_status(vec![])))));
        assert!(matches!(a.conn, Conn::Up));
        assert!(matches!(a.hello, HelloState::Unknown));
        assert!(fx.iter().any(|e| matches!(e, Effect::Hello)), "{fx:?}");

    #[test]
    fn hello_replies_decide_the_protocol_state() {
        let mut a = app();
        update(&mut a, Msg::Reply(Reply::Hello(Ok(crate::testkit::sample_hello()))));
        assert!(a.has_capability("copy_to_clipboard"));
        assert_eq!(a.clip_limit(), 32 * 1024 * 1024);
        let mut b = app();
        update(&mut b, Msg::Reply(Reply::Hello(Err(ApiError::Unsupported))));
        assert!(matches!(b.hello, HelloState::Old));
        assert!(!b.has_capability("copy_to_clipboard"));
        assert_eq!(b.request_cap(), OLD_REQUEST_CAP);
        let mut c = app();
        update(&mut c, Msg::Reply(Reply::Hello(Err(ApiError::Timeout(15)))));
        assert!(matches!(c.hello, HelloState::Unknown), "a timeout says nothing about the protocol");
    }

    #[test]
    fn toasts_expire() {
        let mut a = app();
        tick(&mut a, 0);
        a.toast(ToastKind::Info, "hello");
        tick(&mut a, 3_900);
        assert_eq!(a.toasts.len(), 1);
        tick(&mut a, 4_100);
        assert!(a.toasts.is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn tabs_are_clickable_and_compact_on_narrow_terminals() {
        let wide = tab_layout(120, Screen::Home);
        assert_eq!(wide[0], (Screen::Home, BRAND_COLS, BRAND_COLS + 8));
        let narrow = tab_layout(60, Screen::Devices);
        let total_end = narrow.last().unwrap().2;
        assert!(total_end <= 60, "the compact tab bar must fit in 60 columns, ends at {total_end}");

        let mut a = app();
        let (_, start, _) = wide[3]; // History
        update(
            &mut a,
            Msg::Mouse(MouseEvent {
                kind: MouseEventKind::Down(MouseButton::Left),
                column: start + 1,
                row: 0,
                modifiers: KeyModifiers::NONE,
            }),
        );
        assert_eq!(a.screen, Screen::History);
        a.mouse_on = false;
        update(
            &mut a,
            Msg::Mouse(MouseEvent {
                kind: MouseEventKind::Down(MouseButton::Left),
                column: BRAND_COLS + 1,
                row: 0,
                modifiers: KeyModifiers::NONE,
            }),
        );
        assert_eq!(a.screen, Screen::History, "mouse off: clicks are ignored");
    }

    #[test]
    fn replies_for_actions_become_toasts() {
        let mut a = app();
        update(&mut a, Msg::Reply(Reply::Unpaired(Ok("Pixel".into()))));
        assert!(a.toasts.iter().any(|t| t.text.contains("Pixel") && t.kind == ToastKind::Ok));
        update(&mut a, Msg::Reply(Reply::Paused(Err(ApiError::DaemonDown))));
        assert!(a.toasts.iter().any(|t| t.kind == ToastKind::Error));
        update(&mut a, Msg::Reply(Reply::DaemonStarted(Err(ApiError::Refused("boom".into())))));
        assert!(matches!(a.modal, Some(Modal::Notice { .. })));
    }
}
```

```rust
//! One module per screen; each has its own state, key handling and drawing. This module routes to
//! them. Until a screen exists its hook does nothing.
use crate::app::{AppState, Effect, Screen};
use crossterm::event::KeyEvent;

pub fn key(_app: &mut AppState, _k: KeyEvent, _fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {}
pub fn paste(_app: &mut AppState, _text: &str, _fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {}
pub fn scroll(_app: &mut AppState, _delta: i32) {}
pub fn text_focus(_app: &AppState) -> bool {
    false
}
pub fn on_enter(_app: &mut AppState, _to: Screen, _fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {}
pub fn on_leave(_app: &mut AppState, _from: Screen, _fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {}
pub fn on_tick(_app: &mut AppState, _fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {}
pub fn on_daemon_down(_app: &mut AppState) {}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `app.rs`** (above the test module)

```rust
//! The pure core of the TUI: state, events, effects and `update`. No I/O, no terminal, no clock
//! (time arrives as `Msg::Tick`). Everything here is unit-tested without a terminal.
use crate::api::{ApiError, DaemonStatusInfo, HelloInfo};
use crate::fmt;
use crate::textbuf::TextBuf;
use crossterm::event::{
    KeyCode, KeyEvent, KeyEventKind, KeyModifiers, MouseButton, MouseEvent, MouseEventKind,
};

pub const MIN_COLS: u16 = 60;
pub const MIN_ROWS: u16 = 16;
/// "Until I turn it back on": ten years, as on Android.
pub const PAUSE_FOREVER_SECS: u64 = 10 * 365 * 24 * 3600;
/// What a daemon older than the Hello call accepts in one request.
pub const OLD_REQUEST_CAP: usize = 8 * 1024 * 1024;
pub const DEFAULT_CLIP_LIMIT: usize = 32 * 1024 * 1024;
/// Width of the brand mark in front of the tabs ("●─● klip  ").
pub const BRAND_COLS: u16 = 10;
const STATUS_EVERY_MS: u64 = 1000;
const TOAST_MS: u64 = 4000;
const TOAST_ERROR_MS: u64 = 7000;
const MAX_TOASTS: usize = 3;

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ screens

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Screen {
    Home,
    Devices,
    Pair,
    History,
    Send,
    Log,
    Settings,
}

impl Screen {
    pub const ALL: [Screen; 7] = [
        Screen::Home,
        Screen::Devices,
        Screen::Pair,
        Screen::History,
        Screen::Send,
        Screen::Log,
        Screen::Settings,
    ];

    pub fn index(self) -> usize {
        Self::ALL.iter().position(|s| *s == self).unwrap_or(0)
    }
    pub fn from_index(i: usize) -> Option<Screen> {
        Self::ALL.get(i).copied()
    }
    pub fn title(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Screen::Home => "Home",
            Screen::Devices => "Devices",
            Screen::Pair => "Pair",
            Screen::History => "History",
            Screen::Send => "Send",
            Screen::Log => "Log",
            Screen::Settings => "Settings",
        }
    }
    pub fn next(self) -> Screen {
        Self::ALL[(self.index() + 1) % Self::ALL.len()]
    }
    pub fn prev(self) -> Screen {
        Self::ALL[(self.index() + Self::ALL.len() - 1) % Self::ALL.len()]
    }
}

/// Narrow terminals show only the number of the inactive tabs.
pub fn is_compact(width: u16) -> bool {
    width < 90
}

pub fn tab_label(s: Screen, compact: bool, active: Screen) -> String {
    if compact && s != active {
        format!(" {} ", s.index() + 1)
    } else {
        format!(" {} {} ", s.index() + 1, s.title())
    }
}

/// Where each tab label sits in the tab bar: `(screen, start column, end column exclusive)`.
/// Shared by drawing and mouse clicks so they can never disagree.
pub fn tab_layout(width: u16, active: Screen) -> Vec<(Screen, u16, u16)> {
    let compact = is_compact(width);
    let mut x = BRAND_COLS;
    Screen::ALL
        .iter()
        .map(|s| {
            let w = tab_label(*s, compact, active).chars().count() as u16;
            let r = (*s, x, x + w);
            x += w;
            r
        })
        .collect()
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- state

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Conn {
    Connecting,
    Up,
    Down { reason: String },
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum HelloState {
    Unknown,
    /// A daemon that predates the Hello call (v0.3.6): everything that existed then works.
    Old,
    New(HelloInfo),
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum ToastKind {
    Info,
    Ok,
    Warn,
    Error,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Toast {
    pub text: String,
    pub kind: ToastKind,
    pub until_ms: u64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum ConfirmAction {
    Unpair { selector: String, name: String },
    StopDaemon,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum Modal {
    Help,
    Confirm { title: String, body: String, action: ConfirmAction },
    Pause { selected: usize, custom: Option<TextBuf> },
    Notice { title: String, body: String },
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum PauseChoice {
    Resume,
    For(u64),
    Custom,
    Forever,
}

pub fn pause_choices(paused: bool) -> Vec<(PauseChoice, &'static str)> {
    let mut v = Vec::new();
    if paused {
        v.push((PauseChoice::Resume, "Resume now"));
    }
    v.push((PauseChoice::For(900), "15 minutes"));
    v.push((PauseChoice::For(3600), "1 hour"));
    v.push((PauseChoice::For(8 * 3600), "8 hours"));
    v.push((PauseChoice::Custom, "Custom (seconds)…"));
    v.push((PauseChoice::Forever, "Until I turn it back on"));
    v
}

/// One request per poll kind may be in flight; screens add their own flags here.
#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct Inflight {
    pub hello: bool,
    pub status: bool,
    pub history: bool,
    pub pair_poll: bool,
    pub log: bool,
}

/// When each poll was last started (monotonic milliseconds).
#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct Clock {
    pub status_ms: u64,
    pub history_ms: u64,
    pub pair_poll_ms: u64,
    pub log_ms: u64,
}

pub struct AppState {
    pub mono_ms: u64,
    pub wall_ms: u64,
    pub ticks: u64,
    pub screen: Screen,
    pub conn: Conn,
    pub status: Option<DaemonStatusInfo>,
    pub hello: HelloState,
    pub size: (u16, u16),
    pub toasts: Vec<Toast>,
    pub modal: Option<Modal>,
    pub mouse_on: bool,
    pub quit: bool,
    pub dirty: bool,
    /// Ctrl+L: the runtime clears the terminal before the next draw.
    pub force_clear: bool,
    pub inflight: Inflight,
    pub clock: Clock,
}

impl AppState {
    pub fn new(size: (u16, u16)) -> Self {
        Self {
            mono_ms: 0,
            wall_ms: 0,
            ticks: 0,
            screen: Screen::Home,
            conn: Conn::Connecting,
            status: None,
            hello: HelloState::Unknown,
            size,
            toasts: Vec::new(),
            modal: None,
            mouse_on: true,
            quit: false,
            dirty: true,
            force_clear: false,
            inflight: Inflight::default(),
            clock: Clock::default(),
        }
    }

    pub fn paused(&self) -> bool {
        self.status.as_ref().map(|s| s.is_paused).unwrap_or(false)
    }

    pub fn has_capability(&self, cap: &str) -> bool {
        matches!(&self.hello, HelloState::New(h) if h.capabilities.iter().any(|c| c == cap))
    }

    /// The daemon's clip limit (the default when the daemon has not said).
    pub fn clip_limit(&self) -> usize {
        match &self.hello {
            HelloState::New(h) => h.config.max_clip_bytes,
            _ => DEFAULT_CLIP_LIMIT,
        }
    }

    /// The largest request the daemon accepts.
    pub fn request_cap(&self) -> usize {
        match &self.hello {
            HelloState::New(h) => h.max_request_bytes,
            _ => OLD_REQUEST_CAP,
        }
    }

    pub fn toast(&mut self, kind: ToastKind, text: impl Into<String>) {
        let ms = if kind == ToastKind::Error { TOAST_ERROR_MS } else { TOAST_MS };
        self.toasts.push(Toast { text: text.into(), kind, until_ms: self.mono_ms + ms });
        while self.toasts.len() > MAX_TOASTS {
            self.toasts.remove(0);
        }
        self.dirty = true;
    }

    /// True while keystrokes go into a text field (then letter and digit shortcuts are inactive).
    pub fn text_focus(&self) -> bool {
        match &self.modal {
            Some(Modal::Pause { custom: Some(_), .. }) => true,
            Some(_) => false,
            None => crate::screens::text_focus(self),
        }
    }
}

// -------------------------------------------------------------------------------- events & effects

#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum Msg {
    Key(KeyEvent),
    Mouse(MouseEvent),
    Paste(String),
    Resize(u16, u16),
    Tick { mono_ms: u64, wall_ms: u64 },
    Reply(Reply),
}

/// Work for the runtime: a call to the daemon or a file operation. `update` only describes it.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum Effect {
    Hello,
    Status,
    Pause(u64),
    Resume,
    Unpair { selector: String },
    StopDaemon,
    StartDaemon,
}

/// The result of an effect, delivered back as `Msg::Reply`.
#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum Reply {
    Hello(Result<HelloInfo, ApiError>),
    Status(Result<DaemonStatusInfo, ApiError>),
    Paused(Result<u64, ApiError>),
    Resumed(Result<(), ApiError>),
    Unpaired(Result<String, ApiError>),
    DaemonStopped(Result<(), ApiError>),
    DaemonStarted(Result<u32, ApiError>),
}

/// Effects to run when the app starts.
pub fn boot(app: &mut AppState) -> Vec<Effect> {
    app.inflight.status = true;
    app.inflight.hello = true;
    vec![Effect::Status, Effect::Hello]
}

pub fn update(app: &mut AppState, msg: Msg) -> Vec<Effect> {
    let mut fx = Vec::new();
    match msg {
        Msg::Key(k) => {
            if k.kind != KeyEventKind::Release {
                app.dirty = true;
                on_key(app, k, &mut fx);
            }
        }
        Msg::Paste(text) => {
            app.dirty = true;
            on_paste(app, &text, &mut fx);
        }
        Msg::Mouse(m) => on_mouse(app, m, &mut fx),
        Msg::Resize(w, h) => {
            app.size = (w, h);
            app.dirty = true;
        }
        Msg::Tick { mono_ms, wall_ms } => on_tick(app, mono_ms, wall_ms, &mut fx),
        Msg::Reply(r) => {
            app.dirty = true;
            on_reply(app, r, &mut fx);
        }
    }
    fx
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ input

fn go(app: &mut AppState, to: Screen, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    if app.screen == to {
        return;
    }
    let from = app.screen;
    app.modal = None;
    app.screen = to;
    app.dirty = true;
    crate::screens::on_leave(app, from, fx);
    crate::screens::on_enter(app, to, fx);
}

fn on_key(app: &mut AppState, k: KeyEvent, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    let ctrl = k.modifiers.contains(KeyModifiers::CONTROL);
    let alt = k.modifiers.contains(KeyModifiers::ALT);
    if ctrl && k.code == KeyCode::Char('c') {
        app.quit = true;
        return;
    }
    if alt {
        if let KeyCode::Char(c @ '1'..='7') = k.code {
            if let Some(s) = Screen::from_index(c as usize - '1' as usize) {
                go(app, s, fx);
            }
            return;
        }
    }
    if ctrl {
        match k.code {
            KeyCode::Right => {
                let next = app.screen.next();
                go(app, next, fx);
                return;
            }
            KeyCode::Left => {
                let prev = app.screen.prev();
                go(app, prev, fx);
                return;
            }
            KeyCode::Char('l') => {
                app.force_clear = true;
                return;
            }
            _ => {}
        }
    }
    if app.modal.is_some() {
        on_modal_key(app, k, fx);
        return;
    }
    if crate::screens::text_focus(app) {
        crate::screens::key(app, k, fx);
        return;
    }
    match k.code {
        KeyCode::Char('q') => app.quit = true,
        KeyCode::Char('?') => app.modal = Some(Modal::Help),
        KeyCode::Char('p') => {
            app.modal = Some(Modal::Pause { selected: 0, custom: None });
        }
        KeyCode::Char('m') => {
            app.mouse_on = !app.mouse_on;
            let text = if app.mouse_on {
                "Mouse on"
            } else {
                "Mouse off: you can select text with the mouse"
            };
            app.toast(ToastKind::Info, text);
        }
        KeyCode::Char(c @ '1'..='7') => {
            if let Some(s) = Screen::from_index(c as usize - '1' as usize) {
                go(app, s, fx);
            }
        }
        KeyCode::Tab => {
            let next = app.screen.next();
            go(app, next, fx);
        }
        KeyCode::BackTab => {
            let prev = app.screen.prev();
            go(app, prev, fx);
        }
        _ => crate::screens::key(app, k, fx),
    }
}

fn on_paste(app: &mut AppState, text: &str, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    if let Some(Modal::Pause { custom: Some(buf), .. }) = &mut app.modal {
        let digits: String = text.chars().filter(|c| c.is_ascii_digit()).collect();
        buf.insert_str(&digits);
        return;
    }
    if app.modal.is_none() {
        crate::screens::paste(app, text, fx);
    }
}

fn on_mouse(app: &mut AppState, m: MouseEvent, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    if !app.mouse_on || app.modal.is_some() {
        return;
    }
    match m.kind {
        MouseEventKind::Down(MouseButton::Left) if m.row == 0 => {
            let hit = tab_layout(app.size.0, app.screen)
                .into_iter()
                .find(|(_, a, b)| m.column >= *a && m.column < *b);
            if let Some((s, _, _)) = hit {
                go(app, s, fx);
            }
        }
        MouseEventKind::ScrollUp => crate::screens::scroll(app, -3),
        MouseEventKind::ScrollDown => crate::screens::scroll(app, 3),
        _ => {}
    }
}

fn on_modal_key(app: &mut AppState, k: KeyEvent, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    let Some(modal) = app.modal.take() else { return };
    let closes = matches!(k.code, KeyCode::Esc | KeyCode::Enter | KeyCode::Char('q'));
    app.modal = match modal {
        Modal::Help => {
            if closes || k.code == KeyCode::Char('?') {
                None
            } else {
                Some(Modal::Help)
            }
        }
        Modal::Notice { .. } => {
            if closes {
                None
            } else {
                Some(modal)
            }
        }
        Modal::Confirm { action, .. } if matches!(k.code, KeyCode::Char('y') | KeyCode::Char('Y')) => {
            run_confirmed(action, fx);
            None
        }
        Modal::Confirm { .. } => {
            if closes || matches!(k.code, KeyCode::Char('n') | KeyCode::Char('N')) {
                None
            } else {
                Some(modal)
            }
        }
        Modal::Pause { selected, custom } => pause_key(app, selected, custom, k, fx),
    };
}

fn run_confirmed(action: ConfirmAction, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    match action {
        ConfirmAction::Unpair { selector, .. } => fx.push(Effect::Unpair { selector }),
        ConfirmAction::StopDaemon => fx.push(Effect::StopDaemon),
    }
}

/// Keys inside the pause menu. Returns the modal to keep open, or `None` to close it.
fn pause_key(
    app: &mut AppState,
    mut selected: usize,
    mut custom: Option<TextBuf>,
    k: KeyEvent,
    fx: &mut Vec<Effect>,
) -> Option<Modal> {
    let choices = pause_choices(app.paused());
    if let Some(buf) = custom.as_mut() {
        match k.code {
            KeyCode::Esc => custom = None,
            KeyCode::Backspace => buf.backspace(),
            KeyCode::Char(c) if c.is_ascii_digit() => {
                buf.insert_char(c);
            }
            KeyCode::Enter => {
                let secs: u64 = buf.text().parse().unwrap_or(0);
                if secs == 0 || secs > PAUSE_FOREVER_SECS {
                    app.toast(ToastKind::Error, "Enter a number of seconds from 1 to 315360000");
                } else {
                    fx.push(Effect::Pause(secs));
                    return None;
                }
            }
            _ => {}
        }
        return Some(Modal::Pause { selected, custom });
    }
    match k.code {
        KeyCode::Up | KeyCode::Char('k') => selected = selected.saturating_sub(1),
        KeyCode::Down | KeyCode::Char('j') => selected = (selected + 1).min(choices.len() - 1),
        KeyCode::Esc | KeyCode::Char('q') => return None,
        KeyCode::Enter => match choices[selected.min(choices.len() - 1)].0 {
            PauseChoice::Resume => {
                fx.push(Effect::Resume);
                return None;
            }
            PauseChoice::For(secs) => {
                fx.push(Effect::Pause(secs));
                return None;
            }
            PauseChoice::Forever => {
                fx.push(Effect::Pause(PAUSE_FOREVER_SECS));
                return None;
            }
            PauseChoice::Custom => custom = Some(TextBuf::new(12)),
        },
        _ => {}
    }
    Some(Modal::Pause { selected, custom })
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- time

fn on_tick(app: &mut AppState, mono_ms: u64, wall_ms: u64, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    app.mono_ms = mono_ms;
    app.wall_ms = wall_ms;
    app.ticks += 1;
    let before = app.toasts.len();
    app.toasts.retain(|t| t.until_ms > mono_ms);
    if app.toasts.len() != before || (app.ticks & 3) == 0 || matches!(app.conn, Conn::Connecting) {
        app.dirty = true;
    }
    if !app.inflight.status && mono_ms.saturating_sub(app.clock.status_ms) >= STATUS_EVERY_MS {
        app.inflight.status = true;
        app.clock.status_ms = mono_ms;
        fx.push(Effect::Status);
    }
    crate::screens::on_tick(app, fx);
}

// ----------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- replies

fn on_reply(app: &mut AppState, reply: Reply, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    match reply {
        Reply::Hello(res) => {
            app.inflight.hello = false;
            match res {
                Ok(h) => app.hello = HelloState::New(h),
                Err(ApiError::Unsupported) | Err(ApiError::Refused(_)) => app.hello = HelloState::Old,
                Err(_) => {} // no answer says nothing about the protocol; asked again after reconnecting
            }
        }
        Reply::Status(res) => {
            app.inflight.status = false;
            match res {
                Ok(s) => on_status(app, s, fx),
                Err(e) => on_down(app, e),
            }
        }
        Reply::Paused(Ok(secs)) => {
            app.toast(ToastKind::Ok, format!("Paused: {}", fmt::remaining(secs)));
            app.clock.status_ms = 0;
        }
        Reply::Paused(Err(e)) => app.toast(ToastKind::Error, format!("Could not pause: {}", e.plain())),
        Reply::Resumed(Ok(())) => {
            app.toast(ToastKind::Ok, "Syncing resumed");
            app.clock.status_ms = 0;
        }
        Reply::Resumed(Err(e)) => app.toast(ToastKind::Error, format!("Could not resume: {}", e.plain())),
        Reply::Unpaired(Ok(name)) => {
            app.toast(ToastKind::Ok, format!("Unpaired {}", crate::sanitize::one_line(&name)));
            app.clock.status_ms = 0;
        }
        Reply::Unpaired(Err(e)) => app.toast(ToastKind::Error, format!("Could not unpair: {}", e.plain())),
        Reply::DaemonStopped(Ok(())) => app.toast(ToastKind::Info, "Klip stopped"),
        Reply::DaemonStopped(Err(e)) => app.toast(ToastKind::Error, format!("Could not stop Klip: {}", e.plain())),
        Reply::DaemonStarted(Ok(pid)) => app.toast(ToastKind::Ok, format!("Klip started (PID {pid})")),
        Reply::DaemonStarted(Err(e)) => {
            app.modal = Some(Modal::Notice {
                title: "Could not start Klip".to_string(),
                body: format!("{}\n\nThe Log screen (6) shows more.", e.plain()),
            });
        }
    }
}

fn on_status(app: &mut AppState, s: DaemonStatusInfo, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    let from_down = matches!(app.conn, Conn::Down { .. });
    app.conn = Conn::Up;
    app.status = Some(s);
    if from_down {
        // The daemon may have been restarted (even upgraded): ask again what it can do.
        app.hello = HelloState::Unknown;
        if !app.inflight.hello {
            app.inflight.hello = true;
            fx.push(Effect::Hello);
        }
    }
}

fn on_down(app: &mut AppState, e: ApiError) {
    let was_down = matches!(app.conn, Conn::Down { .. });
    app.conn = Conn::Down { reason: e.plain() };
    app.status = None;
    if was_down {
        return;
    }
    if matches!(app.modal, Some(Modal::Confirm { .. }) | Some(Modal::Pause { .. })) {
        app.modal = Some(Modal::Notice {
            title: "Klip stopped answering".to_string(),
            body: "Your choice was not applied. This screen reconnects by itself when Klip is back.".to_string(),
        });
    }
    crate::screens::on_daemon_down(app);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Write `screens/devices.rs`**

```rust
//! The Devices screen: the linked devices, unpair one or all.
use crate::api::PeerStatusInfo;
use crate::app::{AppState, ConfirmAction, Conn, Effect, Modal, ToastKind};
use crate::sanitize::{one_line, pad_to, truncate};
use crate::theme::Theme;
use crossterm::event::{KeyCode, KeyEvent};
use ratatui::layout::{Constraint, Layout, Rect};
use ratatui::text::{Line, Span};
use ratatui::widgets::{Block, Paragraph};
use ratatui::Frame;

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct DevicesState {
    pub selected: usize,
}

fn peers(app: &AppState) -> &[PeerStatusInfo] {
    app.status.as_ref().map(|s| s.peers.as_slice()).unwrap_or(&[])
}

pub fn scroll(app: &mut AppState, delta: i32) {
    let n = peers(app).len();
    let next = (app.devices.selected as i64 + i64::from(delta)).clamp(0, n.saturating_sub(1) as i64);
    app.devices.selected = next as usize;
}

pub fn key(app: &mut AppState, k: KeyEvent, _fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    let n = peers(app).len();
    match k.code {
        KeyCode::Up | KeyCode::Char('k') => scroll(app, -1),
        KeyCode::Down | KeyCode::Char('j') => scroll(app, 1),
        KeyCode::Home | KeyCode::Char('g') => app.devices.selected = 0,
        KeyCode::End | KeyCode::Char('G') => app.devices.selected = n.saturating_sub(1),
        KeyCode::Char('u') => {
            let target = peers(app)
                .get(app.devices.selected)
                .map(|p| (one_line(&p.name), p.node_id.fingerprint()));
            match target {
                None => app.toast(ToastKind::Info, "No linked devices to unpair"),
                Some((name, selector)) => {
                    app.modal = Some(Modal::Confirm {
                        title: format!("Unpair {name}?"),
                        body: "It stops syncing with this computer until you pair it again.".to_string(),
                        action: ConfirmAction::Unpair { selector, name },
                    });
                }
            }
        }
        KeyCode::Char('U') => {
            let names: Vec<String> = peers(app).iter().map(|p| one_line(&p.name)).collect();
            if names.is_empty() {
                app.toast(ToastKind::Info, "No linked devices to unpair");
            } else {
                app.modal = Some(Modal::Confirm {
                    title: "Unpair ALL devices?".to_string(),
                    body: format!(
                        "This removes {} linked device(s): {}.\nThey stop syncing until you pair them again.",
                        names.len(),
                        names.join(", ")
                    ),
                    action: ConfirmAction::UnpairAll { count: names.len() },
                });
            }
        }
        _ => {}
    }
}

/// One row of a device list: marker, online dot, padded name, fingerprint, state.
pub fn peer_line(p: &PeerStatusInfo, width_cols: usize, theme: &Theme, selected: bool) -> Line<'static> {
    let (dot, dot_style) = if p.is_online { ("●", theme.st_signal()) } else { ("○", theme.st_dim()) };
    let name = pad_to(&one_line(&p.name), 22);
    let fp = format!("[{}]", p.node_id.fingerprint());
    let state = if p.is_online {
        "connected".to_string()
    } else {
        match &p.last_error {
            Some(e) => format!("offline · {}", one_line(e)),
            None => "offline".to_string(),
        }
    };
    let used = 2 + 1 + 1 + 22 + 2 + fp.chars().count() + 2;
    let state = truncate(&state, width_cols.saturating_sub(used));
    let base = if selected { theme.st_selected() } else { theme.st_text() };
    let state_style = if selected {
        base
    } else if p.is_online {
        theme.st_signal()
    } else {
        theme.st_dim()
    };
    Line::from(vec![
        Span::styled(if selected { "▸ " } else { "  " }, base),
        Span::styled(dot, if selected { base } else { dot_style }),
        Span::styled(format!(" {name}  {fp}  "), base),
        Span::styled(state, state_style),
    ])
}

pub fn view(app: &AppState, theme: &Theme, f: &mut Frame, area: Rect) {
    let Some(st) = &app.status else {
        let msg = if matches!(app.conn, Conn::Down { .. }) { "Klip is not running" } else { "Connecting to Klip…" };
        crate::screens::centered_text(f, area, theme.st_dim(), msg);
        return;
    };
    let [list_area, detail_area] =
        Layout::vertical([Constraint::Min(4), Constraint::Length(6)]).areas(area);

    let block = Block::bordered()
        .title(Span::styled(format!(" Devices ({}) ", st.peers.len()), theme.st_title()))
        .border_style(theme.st_faint());
    let inner = block.inner(list_area);
    f.render_widget(block, list_area);
    if st.peers.is_empty() {
        crate::screens::centered_text(f, inner, theme.st_dim(), "No devices yet. Press 3 to pair one.");
    } else {
        let selected = app.devices.selected.min(st.peers.len() - 1);
        let (start, end) = crate::screens::window(selected, st.peers.len(), inner.height as usize);
        let lines: Vec<Line> = (start..end)
            .map(|i| peer_line(&st.peers[i], inner.width as usize, theme, i == selected))
            .collect();
        f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(lines), inner);
    }

    let block = Block::bordered().title(Span::styled(" Details ", theme.st_title())).border_style(theme.st_faint());
    let inner = block.inner(detail_area);
    f.render_widget(block, detail_area);
    if let Some(p) = st.peers.get(app.devices.selected.min(st.peers.len().saturating_sub(1))) {
        let row = |k: &str, v: String| {
            Line::from(vec![
                Span::styled(format!("{k:<13}"), theme.st_dim()),
                Span::styled(truncate(&one_line(&v), (inner.width as usize).saturating_sub(13)), theme.st_text()),
            ])
        };
        let lines = vec![
            row("Name", p.name.clone()),
            row("Fingerprint", p.node_id.fingerprint()),
            row("State", if p.is_online { "connected".to_string() } else { "not connected".to_string() }),
            row("Last error", p.last_error.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| "none".to_string())),
        ];
        f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(lines), inner);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write `screens/home.rs`**

```rust
//! The Home screen: state of Klip, this device, the last clip and the linked devices.
use crate::api::DaemonStatusInfo;
use crate::app::{AppState, ConfirmAction, Conn, Effect, Modal, ToastKind};
use crate::fmt;
use crate::sanitize::{one_line, truncate};
use crate::screens::devices::peer_line;
use crate::theme::Theme;
use crossterm::event::{KeyCode, KeyEvent};
use ratatui::layout::{Constraint, Layout, Rect};
use ratatui::text::{Line, Span};
use ratatui::widgets::{Block, Paragraph};
use ratatui::Frame;

pub fn key(app: &mut AppState, k: KeyEvent, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    match k.code {
        KeyCode::Char('s') if matches!(app.conn, Conn::Down { .. }) => {
            fx.push(Effect::StartDaemon);
            app.toast(ToastKind::Info, "Starting Klip…");
        }
        KeyCode::Char('x') if matches!(app.conn, Conn::Up) => {
            app.modal = Some(Modal::Confirm {
                title: "Stop Klip?".to_string(),
                body: "Syncing stops on this computer until you start Klip again (the s key here, or `klip start`)."
                    .to_string(),
                action: ConfirmAction::StopDaemon,
            });
        }
        _ => {}
    }
}

pub fn view(app: &AppState, theme: &Theme, f: &mut Frame, area: Rect) {
    match (&app.conn, &app.status) {
        (Conn::Down { reason }, _) => down_card(reason, theme, f, area),
        (Conn::Up, Some(st)) => up(st, theme, f, area),
        _ => crate::screens::centered_text(f, area, theme.st_dim(), "Connecting to Klip…"),
    }
}

fn last_clip_line(st: &DaemonStatusInfo, width: usize) -> String {
    match (st.last_sync_time_secs_ago, &st.last_clip_preview) {
        (Some(secs), Some(preview)) => truncate(
            &format!(
                "{} clips synced · last {} · \"{}\" ({})",
                st.total_synced_clips,
                fmt::ago(secs),
                one_line(preview),
                fmt::bytes(st.last_clip_size_bytes)
            ),
            width,
        ),
        _ if st.total_synced_clips > 0 => format!("{} clips synced", st.total_synced_clips),
        _ => "no clips synced yet".to_string(),
    }
}

fn up(st: &DaemonStatusInfo, theme: &Theme, f: &mut Frame, area: Rect) {
    let status_h = if st.pairing_open { 6 } else { 5 };
    let [top, bottom] = Layout::vertical([Constraint::Length(status_h), Constraint::Min(3)]).areas(area);

    let block = Block::bordered().title(Span::styled(" Status ", theme.st_title())).border_style(theme.st_faint());
    let inner = block.inner(top);
    f.render_widget(block, top);
    let w = inner.width as usize;
    let state = if st.is_paused {
        Span::styled(format!("● PAUSED {}", fmt::remaining(st.paused_remaining_secs)), theme.st_warn())
    } else {
        Span::styled("● ACTIVE", theme.st_signal())
    };
    let mut lines = vec![
        Line::from(vec![
            state,
            Span::styled(format!("   {}", truncate(&one_line(&st.relay_state), w.saturating_sub(24))), theme.st_dim()),
        ]),
        Line::from(vec![
            Span::styled("this device  ", theme.st_dim()),
            Span::styled(st.node_id.fingerprint(), theme.st_text()),
            Span::styled(
                format!(
                    "   {}   RAM {:.1} MB",
                    truncate(&one_line(&st.platform), 28),
                    st.memory_rss_bytes as f64 / 1_048_576.0
                ),
                theme.st_dim(),
            ),
        ]),
        Line::from(Span::styled(last_clip_line(st, w), theme.st_dim())),
    ];
    if st.pairing_open {
        lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled("pairing window open", theme.st_warn())));
    }
    f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(lines), inner);

    let block = Block::bordered()
        .title(Span::styled(format!(" Devices ({}) ", st.peers.len()), theme.st_title()))
        .border_style(theme.st_faint());
    let inner = block.inner(bottom);
    f.render_widget(block, bottom);
    if st.peers.is_empty() {
        crate::screens::centered_text(f, inner, theme.st_dim(), "No devices yet. Press 3 to pair one.");
    } else {
        let shown = (inner.height as usize).min(st.peers.len());
        let lines: Vec<Line> = st.peers[..shown]
            .iter()
            .map(|p| peer_line(p, inner.width as usize, theme, false))
            .collect();
        f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(lines), inner);
    }
}

fn down_card(reason: &str, theme: &Theme, f: &mut Frame, area: Rect) {
    let r = crate::view::centered(area, 54, 9);
    let block = Block::bordered()
        .title(Span::styled(" Klip is not running ", theme.st_bad()))
        .border_style(theme.st_faint());
    let inner = block.inner(r);
    f.render_widget(block, r);
    let lines = vec![
        Line::from(Span::styled(truncate(&one_line(reason), inner.width as usize), theme.st_dim())),
        Line::from(""),
        Line::from(vec![Span::styled("s", theme.st_signal()), Span::raw("  start it")]),
        Line::from(Span::styled("or run `klip start` in a shell", theme.st_dim())),
        Line::from(""),
        Line::from(Span::styled("6  shows the log: why it stopped", theme.st_dim())),
    ];
    f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(lines), inner);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Replace `screens/mod.rs` with the router**

```rust
//! One module per screen; each has its own state, key handling and drawing. This module routes.
pub mod devices;
pub mod home;

use crate::app::{AppState, Effect, Screen};
use crate::theme::Theme;
use crossterm::event::KeyEvent;
use ratatui::layout::{Alignment, Rect};
use ratatui::style::Style;
use ratatui::widgets::Paragraph;
use ratatui::Frame;

/// Which rows of a list of `len` items are shown in `height` rows, keeping `selected` in view.
pub fn window(selected: usize, len: usize, height: usize) -> (usize, usize) {
    if height == 0 || len == 0 {
        return (0, 0);
    }
    let start = selected.saturating_sub(height / 2).min(len.saturating_sub(height));
    (start, (start + height).min(len))
}

/// One centred line of text in the middle of `area`.
pub fn centered_text(f: &mut Frame, area: Rect, style: Style, text: &str) {
    if area.height == 0 || area.width == 0 {
        return;
    }
    let row = Rect { x: area.x, y: area.y + area.height / 2, width: area.width, height: 1 };
    f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(text.to_string()).style(style).alignment(Alignment::Center), row);
}

pub fn key(app: &mut AppState, k: KeyEvent, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    match app.screen {
        Screen::Home => home::key(app, k, fx),
        Screen::Devices => devices::key(app, k, fx),
        _ => {}
    }
}

pub fn paste(_app: &mut AppState, _text: &str, _fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {}

pub fn scroll(app: &mut AppState, delta: i32) {
    if app.screen == Screen::Devices {
        devices::scroll(app, delta);
    }
}

pub fn text_focus(_app: &AppState) -> bool {
    false
}

pub fn on_enter(_app: &mut AppState, _to: Screen, _fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {}
pub fn on_leave(_app: &mut AppState, _from: Screen, _fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {}
pub fn on_tick(_app: &mut AppState, _fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {}
pub fn on_daemon_down(_app: &mut AppState) {}

pub fn view(app: &AppState, theme: &Theme, f: &mut Frame, area: Rect) {
    match app.screen {
        Screen::Home => home::view(app, theme, f, area),
        Screen::Devices => devices::view(app, theme, f, area),
        other => centered_text(
            f,
            area,
            theme.st_dim(),
            &format!("{}: this screen is built in a later task of the plan", other.title()),
        ),
    }
}

/// Screen-specific key hints, each followed by " · ", shown in front of the global ones.
pub fn hints(app: &AppState) -> &'static str {
    match app.screen {
        Screen::Home => "s start · x stop · ",
        Screen::Devices => "↑↓ select · u unpair · U unpair all · ",
        _ => "",
    }
}

/// Key list for the help overlay: global keys first, then the screen's own.
pub fn help_lines(app: &AppState) -> Vec<(&'static str, &'static str)> {
    let mut v = vec![
        ("1-7 / Tab", "switch screen"),
        ("p", "pause or resume syncing"),
        ("?", "this help"),
        ("m", "mouse on/off (off lets you select text)"),
        ("Alt+1-7", "switch screen, also inside a field"),
        ("Ctrl+L", "redraw"),
        ("q / Ctrl+C", "quit (Klip keeps running)"),
    ];
    match app.screen {
        Screen::Home => {
            v.push(("s", "start Klip when it is not running"));
            v.push(("x", "stop Klip"));
        }
        Screen::Devices => {
            v.push(("↑↓ / j k", "select a device"));
            v.push(("u", "unpair the selected device"));
            v.push(("U", "unpair all devices"));
        }
        _ => {}
    }
    v
}
```

```rust
//! Drawing: the frame around every screen — tab bar, status bar, toasts and modals.
use crate::app::{
    is_compact, pause_choices, tab_label, AppState, Conn, Modal, Screen, ToastKind, MIN_COLS, MIN_ROWS,
};
use crate::fmt;
use crate::sanitize::{one_line, truncate, width};
use crate::screens;
use crate::theme::Theme;
use ratatui::layout::{Alignment, Constraint, Layout, Rect};
use ratatui::style::Modifier;
use ratatui::text::{Line, Span};
use ratatui::widgets::{Block, Clear, Paragraph, Wrap};
use ratatui::Frame;

/// A `w × h` rectangle in the middle of `area` (clamped to it).
pub fn centered(area: Rect, w: u16, h: u16) -> Rect {
    let w = w.min(area.width);
    let h = h.min(area.height);
    Rect { x: area.x + (area.width - w) / 2, y: area.y + (area.height - h) / 2, width: w, height: h }
}

pub fn view(app: &AppState, theme: &Theme, f: &mut Frame) {
    let area = f.area();
    if area.width < MIN_COLS || area.height < MIN_ROWS {
        too_small(f, area, theme);
        return;
    }
    let [top, body, bottom] =
        Layout::vertical([Constraint::Length(1), Constraint::Min(0), Constraint::Length(1)]).areas(area);
    tab_bar(app, theme, f, top);
    screens::view(app, theme, f, body);
    status_bar(app, theme, f, bottom);
    toasts(app, theme, f, body);
    if let Some(m) = &app.modal {
        modal(app, m, theme, f, area);
    }
}

fn too_small(f: &mut Frame, area: Rect, theme: &Theme) {
    let msg = format!(
        "Terminal too small: Klip needs {MIN_COLS}×{MIN_ROWS}, this is {}×{}",
        area.width, area.height
    );
    f.render_widget(
        Paragraph::new(msg).style(theme.st_warn()).alignment(Alignment::Center).wrap(Wrap { trim: true }),
        area,
    );
}

fn chip(app: &AppState, theme: &Theme) -> (String, ratatui::style::Style) {
    match (&app.conn, &app.status) {
        (Conn::Down { .. }, _) => ("○ STOPPED".to_string(), theme.st_bad()),
        (Conn::Connecting, _) => ("… connecting".to_string(), theme.st_dim()),
        (_, Some(s)) if s.is_paused => {
            (format!("● PAUSED {}", fmt::remaining(s.paused_remaining_secs)), theme.st_warn())
        }
        _ => ("● ACTIVE".to_string(), theme.st_signal()),
    }
}

fn tab_bar(app: &AppState, theme: &Theme, f: &mut Frame, area: Rect) {
    let mut spans = vec![
        Span::styled("●─●", theme.st_signal()),
        Span::raw(" "),
        Span::styled("klip", theme.st_text().add_modifier(Modifier::BOLD)),
        Span::raw("  "),
    ];
    let compact = is_compact(area.width);
    for s in Screen::ALL {
        let label = tab_label(s, compact, app.screen);
        spans.push(if s == app.screen {
            Span::styled(label, theme.st_selected())
        } else {
            Span::styled(label, theme.st_dim())
        });
    }
    let (text, style) = chip(app, theme);
    let used: usize = spans.iter().map(|s| width(&s.content)).sum();
    let room = (area.width as usize).saturating_sub(used);
    let chip_w = width(&text);
    if room > chip_w {
        spans.push(Span::raw(" ".repeat(room - chip_w)));
        spans.push(Span::styled(text, style));
    }
    f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(Line::from(spans)), area);
}

fn status_bar(app: &AppState, theme: &Theme, f: &mut Frame, area: Rect) {
    let text = if app.text_focus() {
        "Esc leave the field · Alt+1-7 screens · Ctrl+C quit".to_string()
    } else {
        format!("{}1-7 screens · p pause · ? help · q quit", screens::hints(app))
    };
    f.render_widget(
        Paragraph::new(truncate(&text, area.width as usize)).style(theme.st_dim()),
        area,
    );
}

fn toasts(app: &AppState, theme: &Theme, f: &mut Frame, body: Rect) {
    if app.toasts.is_empty() || body.height == 0 {
        return;
    }
    let n = app.toasts.len().min(body.height as usize);
    let area = Rect { x: body.x, y: body.y + body.height - n as u16, width: body.width, height: n as u16 };
    let lines: Vec<Line> = app
        .toasts
        .iter()
        .rev()
        .take(n)
        .rev()
        .map(|t| {
            let (icon, style) = match t.kind {
                ToastKind::Ok => ("✓ ", theme.st_signal()),
                ToastKind::Info => ("· ", theme.st_text()),
                ToastKind::Warn => ("! ", theme.st_warn()),
                ToastKind::Error => ("✗ ", theme.st_bad()),
            };
            Line::from(Span::styled(
                truncate(&format!(" {icon}{} ", one_line(&t.text)), body.width as usize),
                style,
            ))
            .right_aligned()
        })
        .collect();
    f.render_widget(Clear, area);
    f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(lines), area);
}

/// Rows a text takes when wrapped to `inner_w` columns (an estimate good enough to size a box).
fn wrapped_rows(lines: &[String], inner_w: usize) -> usize {
    lines
        .iter()
        .map(|l| width(l).max(1).div_ceil(inner_w.max(1)))
        .sum::<usize>()
        .max(1)
}

fn boxed(f: &mut Frame, area: Rect, title: &str, theme: &Theme, lines: Vec<Line<'static>>, plain: &[String], w: u16) {
    let w = w.min(area.width.saturating_sub(2)).max(10);
    let inner_w = w.saturating_sub(4) as usize;
    let h = (wrapped_rows(plain, inner_w) as u16 + 2).min(area.height.saturating_sub(2)).max(3);
    let r = centered(area, w, h);
    f.render_widget(Clear, r);
    let block = Block::bordered().title(Span::styled(format!(" {title} "), theme.st_title())).border_style(theme.st_signal());
    let inner = block.inner(r);
    f.render_widget(block, r);
    f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(lines).wrap(Wrap { trim: false }), inner);
}

fn modal(app: &AppState, m: &Modal, theme: &Theme, f: &mut Frame, area: Rect) {
    match m {
        Modal::Help => {
            let rows = screens::help_lines(app);
            let plain: Vec<String> = rows.iter().map(|(k, d)| format!("{k:<12}{d}")).collect();
            let mut lines: Vec<Line<'static>> = rows
                .iter()
                .map(|(k, d)| {
                    Line::from(vec![
                        Span::styled(format!("{k:<12}"), theme.st_signal()),
                        Span::styled((*d).to_string(), theme.st_text()),
                    ])
                })
                .collect();
            lines.push(Line::from(""));
            lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled("Esc  close", theme.st_dim())));
            let mut plain = plain;
            plain.push(String::new());
            plain.push("Esc  close".to_string());
            boxed(f, area, "Keys", theme, lines, &plain, 62);
        }
        Modal::Confirm { title, body, .. } => {
            let body = one_line_keep_newlines(body);
            let mut plain: Vec<String> = body.lines().map(|l| l.to_string()).collect();
            let mut lines: Vec<Line<'static>> =
                body.lines().map(|l| Line::from(Span::styled(l.to_string(), theme.st_text()))).collect();
            lines.push(Line::from(""));
            lines.push(Line::from(vec![
                Span::styled("y", theme.st_signal()),
                Span::styled("  yes        ", theme.st_text()),
                Span::styled("n", theme.st_signal()),
                Span::styled("  no", theme.st_text()),
            ]));
            plain.push(String::new());
            plain.push("y  yes        n  no".to_string());
            boxed(f, area, &one_line(title), theme, lines, &plain, 62);
        }
        Modal::Notice { title, body } => {
            let body = one_line_keep_newlines(body);
            let mut plain: Vec<String> = body.lines().map(|l| l.to_string()).collect();
            let mut lines: Vec<Line<'static>> =
                body.lines().map(|l| Line::from(Span::styled(l.to_string(), theme.st_text()))).collect();
            lines.push(Line::from(""));
            lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled("Enter  close", theme.st_dim())));
            plain.push(String::new());
            plain.push("Enter  close".to_string());
            boxed(f, area, &one_line(title), theme, lines, &plain, 62);
        }
        Modal::Pause { selected, custom } => {
            let choices = pause_choices(app.paused());
            let mut lines: Vec<Line<'static>> = Vec::new();
            let mut plain: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
            for (i, (_, label)) in choices.iter().enumerate() {
                let mark = if i == *selected { "▸ " } else { "  " };
                let style = if i == *selected { theme.st_selected() } else { theme.st_text() };
                lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(format!("{mark}{label}"), style)));
                plain.push(format!("{mark}{label}"));
            }
            if let Some(buf) = custom {
                lines.push(Line::from(""));
                lines.push(Line::from(vec![
                    Span::styled("seconds: ", theme.st_dim()),
                    Span::styled(format!("{}▏", buf.text()), theme.st_text()),
                ]));
                plain.push(String::new());
                plain.push("seconds: ".to_string());
            }
            lines.push(Line::from(""));
            lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled("Enter choose · Esc cancel", theme.st_dim())));
            plain.push(String::new());
            plain.push("Enter choose · Esc cancel".to_string());
            boxed(f, area, "Pause syncing", theme, lines, &plain, 44);
        }
    }
}

/// Sanitize text that may contain newlines, keeping the newlines.
fn one_line_keep_newlines(s: &str) -> String {
    crate::sanitize::clean(s)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Wire the modules, add the test helpers**

In `lib.rs` add `pub mod view;`. In `Cargo.toml` of klip-tui add:

```toml
[[test]]
name = "snapshots"
required-features = ["testkit"]
```
Append to `testkit.rs` (above its tests module):

```rust
use crate::app::AppState;
use crate::theme::{Theme, TuiEnv};
use klip_core::KlipPaths;
use ratatui::backend::TestBackend;
use ratatui::buffer::Buffer;
use ratatui::Terminal;
use std::path::PathBuf;

/// An environment without colours (so snapshots contain text only) and a private temp directory.
pub fn test_env(tag: &str) -> TuiEnv {
    let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("klip-tui-test-{}-{tag}", std::process::id()));
    let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir);
    TuiEnv::from_vars(KlipPaths::from_dir(dir), |k| match k {
        "NO_COLOR" => Some("1".to_string()),
        _ => None,
    })
}

pub fn plain_theme() -> Theme {
    Theme::from_env(&test_env("theme"))
}

/// The text of a buffer, one line per row, trailing spaces removed.
pub fn dump(buf: &Buffer) -> String {
    let mut out = String::new();
    for y in 0..buf.area.height {
        let mut row = String::new();
        for x in 0..buf.area.width {
            row.push_str(buf[(x, y)].symbol());
        }
        out.push_str(row.trim_end());
        out.push('\n');
    }
    out
}

/// Draw `app` on a `cols × rows` test terminal and return the screen as text.
pub fn render(app: &AppState, theme: &Theme, cols: u16, rows: u16) -> String {
    let mut terminal = Terminal::new(TestBackend::new(cols, rows)).expect("test terminal");
    terminal.draw(|f| crate::view::view(app, theme, f)).expect("draw");
    dump(terminal.backend().buffer())
}

/// Compare with `tests/snapshots/<name>.txt`; `UPDATE_SNAPSHOTS=1` rewrites the file.
pub fn assert_snapshot(name: &str, actual: &str) {
    let path = PathBuf::from(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("tests/snapshots").join(format!("{name}.txt"));
    if std::env::var_os("UPDATE_SNAPSHOTS").is_some() {
        std::fs::create_dir_all(path.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(&path, actual).unwrap();
        return;
    }
    let want = std::fs::read_to_string(&path)
        .unwrap_or_else(|_| panic!("missing snapshot {}; run with UPDATE_SNAPSHOTS=1 and review it", path.display()));
    assert_eq!(actual, want, "snapshot {name} changed; if intended, regenerate and review the diff");
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Write the snapshot tests** — `crates/klip-tui/tests/snapshots.rs`:

```rust
use klip_tui::app::{AppState, ConfirmAction, Conn, HelloState, Modal, Screen, ToastKind};
use klip_tui::testkit::*;

fn up(cols: u16, rows: u16, peers: Vec<klip_tui::api::PeerStatusInfo>) -> AppState {
    let mut a = AppState::new((cols, rows));
    a.conn = Conn::Up;
    a.hello = HelloState::New(sample_hello());
    a.status = Some(sample_status(peers));
    a
}

fn two_peers() -> Vec<klip_tui::api::PeerStatusInfo> {
    let mut offline = sample_peer("Pixel 8", false, 2);
    offline.last_error = Some("could not reach the device".to_string());
    vec![sample_peer("Vivo V2253", true, 1), offline]
}

fn snap(name: &str, app: &AppState, cols: u16, rows: u16) {
    let text = render(app, &plain_theme(), cols, rows);
    assert_snapshot(&format!("{name}_{cols}x{rows}"), &text);
}

#[test]
fn home_with_devices() {
    snap("home_up", &up(100, 30, two_peers()), 100, 30);
    snap("home_up", &up(60, 16, two_peers()), 60, 16);
}

#[test]
fn home_without_devices() {
    snap("home_empty", &up(100, 30, vec![]), 100, 30);
}

#[test]
fn home_paused_and_pairing_open() {
    let mut a = up(100, 30, two_peers());
    let st = a.status.as_mut().unwrap();
    st.is_paused = true;
    st.paused_remaining_secs = 754;
    st.pairing_open = true;
    snap("home_paused", &a, 100, 30);
}

#[test]
fn home_when_the_daemon_is_down_and_while_connecting() {
    let mut a = AppState::new((100, 30));
    a.conn = Conn::Down { reason: "Klip is not running".into() };
    snap("home_down", &a, 100, 30);
    let b = AppState::new((100, 30));
    snap("home_connecting", &b, 100, 30);
}

#[test]
```
