- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `term.rs`**

```rust
//! Raw mode, alternate screen, bracketed paste and mouse capture — and, above all, giving the
//! terminal back on every exit path (normal quit, signal, panic).
use crossterm::cursor::Show;
use crossterm::event::{DisableBracketedPaste, DisableMouseCapture, EnableBracketedPaste, EnableMouseCapture};
use crossterm::execute;
use crossterm::terminal::{disable_raw_mode, enable_raw_mode, EnterAlternateScreen, LeaveAlternateScreen};
use std::io::{self, Write};

pub struct TerminalGuard {
    active: bool,
    mouse: bool,
}

impl TerminalGuard {
    pub fn enter(mouse: bool) -> io::Result<Self> {
        enable_raw_mode()?;
        let mut out = io::stdout();
        if let Err(e) = execute!(out, EnterAlternateScreen, EnableBracketedPaste) {
            let _ = disable_raw_mode();
            return Err(e);
        }
        if mouse {
            let _ = execute!(out, EnableMouseCapture);
        }
        install_panic_hook();
        Ok(Self { active: true, mouse })
    }

    pub fn mouse(&self) -> bool {
        self.mouse
    }

    pub fn set_mouse(&mut self, on: bool) {
        if on == self.mouse || !self.active {
            return;
        }
        let mut out = io::stdout();
        let _ = if on { execute!(out, EnableMouseCapture) } else { execute!(out, DisableMouseCapture) };
        self.mouse = on;
    }

    /// Give the terminal back. Safe to call more than once.
    pub fn restore(&mut self) {
        if self.active {
            restore_terminal_best_effort();
            self.active = false;
        }
    }
}

impl Drop for TerminalGuard {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        self.restore();
    }
}

/// Leave the alternate screen, show the cursor, stop mouse/paste modes and leave raw mode. Used by
/// the guard, by signal handling and by the panic hook; every step is best effort.
pub fn restore_terminal_best_effort() {
    let mut out = io::stdout();
    let _ = execute!(out, DisableMouseCapture, DisableBracketedPaste, LeaveAlternateScreen, Show);
    let _ = disable_raw_mode();
    let _ = out.flush();
}

fn install_panic_hook() {
    static ONCE: std::sync::Once = std::sync::Once::new();
    ONCE.call_once(|| {
        let previous = std::panic::take_hook();
        std::panic::set_hook(Box::new(move |info| {
            // Restore first, so the panic message lands on the normal screen and stays readable.
            restore_terminal_best_effort();
            previous(info);
        }));
    });
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `runtime.rs`**

```rust
//! Executes effects against a [`DaemonApi`] and owns the terminal. Generic over the ratatui backend
//! so the tests run exactly this code on a `TestBackend`.
use crate::api::{ApiError, DaemonApi};
use crate::app::{boot, update, AppState, Effect, Msg, Reply};
use crate::theme::{Theme, TuiEnv};
use crate::view;
use ratatui::backend::Backend;
use ratatui::Terminal;
use std::fmt::Debug;
use std::io;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::{SystemTime, UNIX_EPOCH};
use tokio::sync::mpsc::{unbounded_channel, UnboundedReceiver, UnboundedSender};

fn io_err<E: Debug>(e: E) -> io::Error {
    io::Error::other(format!("{e:?}"))
}

pub fn unix_ms() -> u64 {
    SystemTime::now().duration_since(UNIX_EPOCH).map(|d| d.as_millis() as u64).unwrap_or(0)
}

pub struct Runtime<B: Backend> {
    pub app: AppState,
    terminal: Terminal<B>,
    api: Arc<dyn DaemonApi>,
    theme: Theme,
    tx: UnboundedSender<Msg>,
}

impl<B: Backend> Runtime<B>
where
    B::Error: Debug,
{
    pub fn new(
        api: Arc<dyn DaemonApi>,
        backend: B,
        env: TuiEnv,
        size: (u16, u16),
    ) -> io::Result<(Self, UnboundedReceiver<Msg>)> {
        let terminal = Terminal::new(backend).map_err(io_err)?;
        let (tx, rx) = unbounded_channel();
        let mut app = AppState::new(size);
        app.mouse_on = env.mouse;
        let first = boot(&mut app);
        let rt = Self { app, terminal, api, theme: Theme::from_env(&env), tx };
        for fx in first {
            rt.execute(fx);
        }
        Ok((rt, rx))
    }

    /// Apply one event or reply and start the effects it asks for.
    pub fn dispatch(&mut self, msg: Msg) {
        let effects = update(&mut self.app, msg);
        for fx in effects {
            self.execute(fx);
        }
    }

    /// Draw if anything changed since the last draw (and clear first after Ctrl+L).
    pub fn draw(&mut self) -> io::Result<()> {
        if self.app.force_clear {
            self.terminal.clear().map_err(io_err)?;
            self.app.force_clear = false;
            self.app.dirty = true;
        }
        if !self.app.dirty {
            return Ok(());
        }
        self.app.dirty = false;
        let app = &self.app;
        let theme = &self.theme;
        self.terminal.draw(|f| view::view(app, theme, f)).map_err(io_err)?;
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn backend(&self) -> &B {
        self.terminal.backend()
    }

    pub fn backend_mut(&mut self) -> &mut B {
        self.terminal.backend_mut()
    }

    fn execute(&self, fx: Effect) {
        let api = Arc::clone(&self.api);
        let tx = self.tx.clone();
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            // Run in a child task: if the call panics, the poll must still get an answer, or its
            // "in flight" flag would never clear and polling would stop for good.
            let inner = tokio::spawn(run_effect(api, fx.clone()));
            let reply = match inner.await {
                Ok(r) => r,
                Err(e) => failed_reply(&fx, ApiError::Io(format!("internal error: {e}"))),
            };
            let _ = tx.send(Msg::Reply(reply));
        });
    }
}

async fn run_effect(api: Arc<dyn DaemonApi>, fx: Effect) -> Reply {
    match fx {
        Effect::Hello => Reply::Hello(api.hello().await),
        Effect::Status => Reply::Status(api.status().await),
        Effect::Pause(secs) => Reply::Paused(api.pause(secs).await),
        Effect::Resume => Reply::Resumed(api.resume().await),
        Effect::Unpair { selector } => Reply::Unpaired(api.unpair(selector).await),
        Effect::UnpairAll => Reply::UnpairedAll(api.unpair_all().await),
        Effect::StopDaemon => Reply::DaemonStopped(api.stop_daemon().await),
        Effect::StartDaemon => Reply::DaemonStarted(api.start_daemon().await),
    }
}

/// The reply to hand back when an effect's task died.
fn failed_reply(fx: &Effect, e: ApiError) -> Reply {
    match fx {
        Effect::Hello => Reply::Hello(Err(e)),
        Effect::Status => Reply::Status(Err(e)),
        Effect::Pause(_) => Reply::Paused(Err(e)),
        Effect::Resume => Reply::Resumed(Err(e)),
        Effect::Unpair { .. } => Reply::Unpaired(Err(e)),
        Effect::UnpairAll => Reply::UnpairedAll(Err(e)),
        Effect::StopDaemon => Reply::DaemonStopped(Err(e)),
        Effect::StartDaemon => Reply::DaemonStarted(Err(e)),
    }
}

pub fn to_msg(e: crossterm::event::Event) -> Option<Msg> {
    use crossterm::event::Event;
    match e {
        Event::Key(k) => Some(Msg::Key(k)),
        Event::Mouse(m) => Some(Msg::Mouse(m)),
        Event::Paste(s) => Some(Msg::Paste(s)),
        Event::Resize(w, h) => Some(Msg::Resize(w, h)),
        _ => None,
    }
}

/// The real terminal loop: keys, mouse, paste, resize, a 250 ms tick, effect replies and signals.
pub async fn run(api: Arc<dyn DaemonApi>, env: TuiEnv) -> io::Result<()> {
    use crossterm::backend::CrosstermBackend;
    use crossterm::event::EventStream;
    use futures_util::StreamExt;
    use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
    use tokio::signal::unix::{signal, SignalKind};
    use tokio::time::MissedTickBehavior;

    let mut guard = crate::term::TerminalGuard::enter(env.mouse)?;
    #[cfg(debug_assertions)]
    if std::env::var_os("KLIP_TUI_TEST_PANIC").is_some() {
        panic!("injected panic for the terminal-restore test");
    }
    let size = crossterm::terminal::size().unwrap_or((80, 24));
    let (mut rt, mut rx) = Runtime::new(api, CrosstermBackend::new(io::stdout()), env, size)?;
    let mut events = EventStream::new();
    let mut tick = tokio::time::interval(Duration::from_millis(250));
    tick.set_missed_tick_behavior(MissedTickBehavior::Delay);
    let mut on_term = signal(SignalKind::terminate())?;
    let mut on_hup = signal(SignalKind::hangup())?;
    let mut on_int = signal(SignalKind::interrupt())?;
    let start = Instant::now();
    rt.draw()?;
    loop {
        tokio::select! {
            ev = events.next() => match ev {
                Some(Ok(e)) => {
                    if let Some(m) = to_msg(e) {
                        rt.dispatch(m);
                    }
                }
                Some(Err(_)) | None => break,
            },
            _ = tick.tick() => rt.dispatch(Msg::Tick {
                mono_ms: start.elapsed().as_millis() as u64,
                wall_ms: unix_ms(),
            }),
            Some(m) = rx.recv() => rt.dispatch(m),
            _ = on_term.recv() => break,
            _ = on_hup.recv() => break,
            _ = on_int.recv() => break,
        }
        while let Ok(m) = rx.try_recv() {
            rt.dispatch(m);
        }
        guard.set_mouse(rt.app.mouse_on);
        rt.draw()?;
        if rt.app.quit {
            break;
        }
    }
    guard.restore();
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crossterm::event::{Event, KeyCode, KeyEvent, KeyModifiers};

    #[test]
    fn terminal_events_become_messages() {
        let k = Event::Key(KeyEvent::new(KeyCode::Char('a'), KeyModifiers::NONE));
        assert!(matches!(to_msg(k), Some(Msg::Key(_))));
        assert!(matches!(to_msg(Event::Resize(10, 5)), Some(Msg::Resize(10, 5))));
        assert!(matches!(to_msg(Event::Paste("x".into())), Some(Msg::Paste(_))));
        assert!(to_msg(Event::FocusGained).is_none());
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Extend `testkit.rs`** — add `panic_next` to `FakeState` (`pub panic_next: bool,`, initialised `false`) and make `enter` honour it. Replace the body of `FakeDaemon::enter` with:

```rust
    async fn enter(&self, call: &str) -> Result<(), ApiError> {
        let (delay, panic_now) = {
            let mut s = self.state.lock().unwrap();
            s.calls.push(call.to_string());
            if let Some(e) = s.fail_next.take() {
                return Err(e);
            }
            let panic_now = std::mem::take(&mut s.panic_next);
            if !panic_now && !s.up {
                return Err(ApiError::DaemonDown);
            }
            (s.delay_ms, panic_now)
        };
        // Panic only after the lock is released, so the mutex is not poisoned for later calls.
        if panic_now {
            panic!("fake daemon panic requested by the test");
        }
        if delay > 0 {
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(delay)).await;
        }
        Ok(())
    }
```
and append the harness (above the tests module):

```rust
use crate::api::DaemonApi as _DaemonApiForHarness;
use crate::app::Msg;
use crate::runtime::{unix_ms, Runtime};
use crossterm::event::{KeyCode, KeyEvent, KeyModifiers};
use std::time::Instant;
use tokio::sync::mpsc::UnboundedReceiver;

/// The real runtime on a `TestBackend`, with the clock driven by `pump`.
pub struct Harness {
    pub rt: Runtime<TestBackend>,
    rx: UnboundedReceiver<Msg>,
    start: Instant,
}

impl Harness {
    pub fn new(api: Arc<dyn crate::api::DaemonApi>, env: TuiEnv, cols: u16, rows: u16) -> Self {
        let (rt, rx) = Runtime::new(api, TestBackend::new(cols, rows), env, (cols, rows)).expect("runtime");
        Self { rt, rx, start: Instant::now() }
    }

    fn now(&self) -> u64 {
        self.start.elapsed().as_millis() as u64
    }

    pub fn key(&mut self, code: KeyCode) {
        self.key_mod(code, KeyModifiers::NONE);
    }

    pub fn key_mod(&mut self, code: KeyCode, mods: KeyModifiers) {
        self.rt.dispatch(Msg::Key(KeyEvent::new(code, mods)));
    }

    pub fn text(&mut self, s: &str) {
        for c in s.chars() {
            self.key(KeyCode::Char(c));
        }
    }

    pub fn paste(&mut self, s: &str) {
        self.rt.dispatch(Msg::Paste(s.to_string()));
    }

    pub fn resize(&mut self, cols: u16, rows: u16) {
        self.rt.backend_mut().resize(cols, rows);
        self.rt.dispatch(Msg::Resize(cols, rows));
    }

    async fn step(&mut self) {
        let now = self.now();
        self.rt.dispatch(Msg::Tick { mono_ms: now, wall_ms: unix_ms() });
        while let Ok(m) = self.rx.try_recv() {
            self.rt.dispatch(m);
        }
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20)).await;
    }

    /// Let `ms` real milliseconds pass: ticks fire and replies are delivered.
    pub async fn pump(&mut self, ms: u64) {
        let end = Instant::now() + Duration::from_millis(ms);
        while Instant::now() < end {
            self.step().await;
        }
    }

    /// Pump until the screen contains `needle` or `timeout_ms` passed.
    pub async fn wait_for(&mut self, needle: &str, timeout_ms: u64) -> bool {
        let end = Instant::now() + Duration::from_millis(timeout_ms);
        loop {
            if self.screen().contains(needle) {
                return true;
            }
            if Instant::now() >= end {
                return false;
            }
            self.step().await;
        }
    }

    /// The current screen as text (draws first if something changed).
    pub fn screen(&mut self) -> String {
        self.rt.draw().expect("draw");
        dump(self.rt.backend().buffer())
    }
}
```

```rust
#![cfg(target_os = "linux")]
use std::process::Command;

#[test]
fn install_service_writes_the_systemd_unit_into_home_and_says_what_to_run() {
    let home = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("klip-install-{}", std::process::id()));
    let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&home);
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&home).unwrap();
    let out = Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_klip"))
        .arg("install-service")
        .env("HOME", &home)
        .env("KLIP_CONFIG_DIR", home.join("cfg"))
        .output()
        .unwrap();
    let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stdout).into_owned();
    assert!(out.status.success(), "{text}{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&out.stderr));
    assert!(text.contains("Installed systemd unit:"), "{text}");
    assert!(text.contains("systemctl --user enable --now klip.service"), "{text}");
    let unit = home.join(".config/systemd/user/klip.service");
    let body = std::fs::read_to_string(&unit).expect("unit file written");
    assert!(body.contains("ExecStart="), "{body}");
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Move the shared daemon helper** — create `crates/klip-cli/tests/common/mod.rs` with the `Isolated` struct, `text()` and `LAN_ONLY` from `cli_daemon.rs`, extended so a daemon can be created without being started and the child environment is reusable:

```rust
#![allow(dead_code)]
use std::io::Write;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::process::{Command, Output, Stdio};

pub const LAN_ONLY: &str = r#"{"relay":"lan","lan_discovery":false}"#;

pub struct Isolated {
    pub dir: PathBuf,
}

impl Isolated {
    /// A private Klip directory with this `config.json`; no daemon is started.
    pub fn create(tag: &str, config: &str) -> Self {
        let dir = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("klip-cli-{}-{tag}", std::process::id()));
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&dir);
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&dir).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(dir.join("config.json"), config).unwrap();
        Self { dir }
    }

    /// `create`, then `klip start`.
    pub fn start(tag: &str, config: &str) -> Self {
        let me = Self::create(tag, config);
        let out = me.run(&["start"], None);
        assert!(out.status.success(), "start failed: {}", text(&out));
        me
    }

    pub fn env_pairs(&self) -> Vec<(String, String)> {
        vec![
            ("KLIP_CONFIG_DIR".to_string(), self.dir.display().to_string()),
            ("KLIP_CLIPBOARD".to_string(), "memory".to_string()),
        ]
    }

    pub fn run(&self, args: &[&str], stdin: Option<&[u8]>) -> Output {
        let mut cmd = Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_klip"));
        cmd.args(args)
            .env("KLIP_CONFIG_DIR", &self.dir)
            .env("KLIP_CLIPBOARD", "memory")
            .stdout(Stdio::piped())
            .stderr(Stdio::piped());
        match stdin {
            Some(bytes) => {
                cmd.stdin(Stdio::piped());
                let mut child = cmd.spawn().unwrap();
                let mut sin = child.stdin.take().unwrap();
                let data = bytes.to_vec();
                let writer = std::thread::spawn(move || {
                    let _ = sin.write_all(&data); // the CLI may stop reading early on an error
                });
                let out = child.wait_with_output().unwrap();
                let _ = writer.join();
                out
            }
            None => {
                cmd.stdin(Stdio::null());
                cmd.output().unwrap()
            }
        }
    }
}

impl Drop for Isolated {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let _ = self.run(&["stop"], None);
        let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&self.dir);
    }
}

pub fn text(o: &Output) -> String {
    format!("{}{}", String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout), String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stderr))
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Extend `ops.rs`** — add (imports: `use klip_daemon::KlipPaths; use std::path::{Path, PathBuf}; use std::process::Stdio;`):

```rust
pub struct Spawned {
    pub child: std::process::Child,
    pub log_path: PathBuf,
}

/// A log file only the owner can read.
pub fn open_private_log(path: &Path) -> std::io::Result<std::fs::File> {
    let mut opts = std::fs::OpenOptions::new();
    opts.create(true).append(true);
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        use std::os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt;
        opts.mode(0o600);
    }
    opts.open(path)
}

/// Start `klip start --foreground` as a detached background process that logs into the Klip directory.
pub fn spawn_daemon_process(paths: &KlipPaths) -> Result<Spawned, String> {
    let log_path = paths.dir().join("daemon.log");
    let log = open_private_log(&log_path).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let log_err = log.try_clone().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let exe = std::env::current_exe().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    let mut cmd = std::process::Command::new(exe);
    cmd.arg("start")
        .arg("--foreground")
        .stdin(Stdio::null())
        .stdout(log)
        .stderr(log_err);
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        use std::os::unix::process::CommandExt;
        cmd.process_group(0);
    }
    let child = cmd.spawn().map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
    Ok(Spawned { child, log_path })
}

/// The last `lines` lines of a file, or an empty string when it cannot be read.
pub fn log_tail(path: &Path, lines: usize) -> String {
    std::fs::read_to_string(path)
        .map(|t| {
            let all: Vec<&str> = t.lines().collect();
            all[all.len().saturating_sub(lines)..].join("\n")
        })
        .unwrap_or_default()
}

/// Write the auto-start unit for this OS. Returns the text to show (without the leading tick), or
/// `None` when this OS/environment has nothing to install (for example no `$HOME`).
pub fn install_service() -> Result<Option<String>, String> {
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    {
        if let Some(home) = std::env::var_os("HOME") {
            let dest = PathBuf::from(home).join(".config/systemd/user/klip.service");
            klip_daemon::generate_systemd_unit_file(&dest).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            return Ok(Some(format!(
                "Installed systemd unit: {}\nNow run: systemctl --user enable --now klip.service",
                dest.display()
            )));
        }
    }
    #[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
    {
        if let Some(home) = std::env::var_os("HOME") {
            let dest = PathBuf::from(home).join("Library/LaunchAgents/com.klip.daemon.plist");
            klip_daemon::generate_launchd_plist_file(&dest).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
            return Ok(Some(format!(
                "Installed launchd agent: {}\nNow run: launchctl load {}",
                dest.display(),
                dest.display()
            )));
        }
    }
    #[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
    {
        let dest = klip_daemon::get_klip_dir().join("register-service.ps1");
        klip_daemon::generate_windows_service_script(&dest).map_err(|e| e.to_string())?;
        return Ok(Some(format!(
            "Wrote {}\nRun it in PowerShell: powershell -ExecutionPolicy Bypass -File \"{}\"",
            dest.display(),
            dest.display()
        )));
    }
    #[allow(unreachable_code)]
    Ok(None)
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Create `api.rs`** (`#[cfg(unix)] pub mod api;` in `lib.rs`; add the Cargo dependencies from Step 6 first)

```rust
//! The TUI's view of the daemon, over the control socket.
use crate::client::{ClientError, DaemonClient, OLD_DAEMON_REQUEST_CAP};
use crate::ops::{log_tail, request_cap, spawn_daemon_process, unpair_all_compat, Spawned};
use async_trait::async_trait;
use klip_daemon::KlipPaths;
use klip_proto::ipc::{IpcRequest, IpcResponse, IPC_PROTOCOL_VERSION};
use klip_tui::api::*;
use std::time::Duration;

pub struct IpcDaemonApi {
    client: DaemonClient,
    paths: KlipPaths,
}

impl IpcDaemonApi {
    pub fn new(client: DaemonClient, paths: KlipPaths) -> Self {
        Self { client, paths }
    }

    /// One call; the daemon's own refusal becomes `ApiError::Refused`.
    async fn call(&self, req: IpcRequest) -> Result<IpcResponse, ApiError> {
        match self.client.request(&req, OLD_DAEMON_REQUEST_CAP).await.map_err(map_err)? {
            IpcResponse::Error(e) => Err(ApiError::Refused(e)),
            other => Ok(other),
        }
    }
}

fn map_err(e: ClientError) -> ApiError {
    match e {
        ClientError::Down(_) => ApiError::DaemonDown,
        ClientError::Timeout(s) => ApiError::Timeout(s),
        ClientError::TooLarge { size, max } => ApiError::TooLarge { size, max },
        ClientError::Io(m) => ApiError::Io(m),
    }
}

fn unexpected(r: IpcResponse) -> ApiError {
    ApiError::Io(format!("unexpected reply: {r:?}"))
}

#[async_trait]
impl DaemonApi for IpcDaemonApi {
    async fn hello(&self) -> Result<HelloInfo, ApiError> {
        match self.call(IpcRequest::Hello { client_protocol: IPC_PROTOCOL_VERSION }).await {
            Ok(IpcResponse::Hello(h)) => Ok(h),
            Ok(other) => Err(unexpected(other)),
            // A daemon that predates protocol 2 answers an unknown request with an error.
            Err(ApiError::Refused(_)) => Err(ApiError::Unsupported),
            Err(e) => Err(e),
        }
    }

    async fn status(&self) -> Result<DaemonStatusInfo, ApiError> {
        match self.call(IpcRequest::GetStatus).await? {
            IpcResponse::Status(s) => Ok(s),
            other => Err(unexpected(other)),
        }
    }

    async fn history(&self, limit: usize) -> Result<Vec<HistoryItem>, ApiError> {
        match self.call(IpcRequest::GetHistory { limit }).await? {
            IpcResponse::History(items) => Ok(items),
            other => Err(unexpected(other)),
        }
    }

    async fn send(&self, text: String) -> Result<SendReport, ApiError> {
        let cap = request_cap(&self.client).await;
        match self.client.request(&IpcRequest::SendClip { text }, cap).await.map_err(map_err)? {
            IpcResponse::Sent { bytes, delivered, failed } => Ok(SendReport { bytes, delivered, failed }),
            IpcResponse::Error(e) => Err(ApiError::Refused(e)),
            other => Err(unexpected(other)),
        }
    }

    async fn pause(&self, secs: u64) -> Result<u64, ApiError> {
        match self.call(IpcRequest::Pause { duration_secs: secs }).await? {
            IpcResponse::Paused { remaining_secs } => Ok(remaining_secs),
            other => Err(unexpected(other)),
        }
    }

    async fn resume(&self) -> Result<(), ApiError> {
        match self.call(IpcRequest::Resume).await? {
            IpcResponse::Resumed => Ok(()),
            other => Err(unexpected(other)),
        }
    }

    async fn unpair(&self, selector: String) -> Result<String, ApiError> {
        match self.call(IpcRequest::Unpair { selector }).await? {
            IpcResponse::Unpaired { name } => Ok(name),
            other => Err(unexpected(other)),
        }
    }

    async fn unpair_all(&self) -> Result<(Vec<String>, Vec<(String, String)>), ApiError> {
        unpair_all_compat(&self.client).await.map_err(map_err)
    }

    async fn copy_to_clipboard(&self, id: [u8; 16]) -> Result<(), ApiError> {
        match self.call(IpcRequest::CopyToClipboard { id }).await? {
            IpcResponse::Copied => Ok(()),
            other => Err(unexpected(other)),
        }
    }

    async fn clear_history(&self) -> Result<(), ApiError> {
        match self.call(IpcRequest::ClearHistory).await? {
            IpcResponse::HistoryCleared => Ok(()),
            other => Err(unexpected(other)),
        }
    }

    async fn pair_start(&self, ttl_secs: u64, lan: bool) -> Result<PairTicket, ApiError> {
        match self.call(IpcRequest::PairStart { ttl_secs, include_lan_addrs: lan }).await? {
            IpcResponse::PairTicket { ticket, expires_in_secs, host_fingerprint } => {
                Ok(PairTicket { ticket, expires_in_secs, host_fingerprint })
            }
            other => Err(unexpected(other)),
        }
    }

    async fn pair_poll(&self) -> Result<PairPollInfo, ApiError> {
        match self.call(IpcRequest::PairPoll).await? {
            IpcResponse::PairPoll(i) => Ok(i),
            other => Err(unexpected(other)),
        }
    }

    async fn pair_decide(&self, accept: bool) -> Result<PairPollInfo, ApiError> {
        match self.call(IpcRequest::PairDecide { accept }).await? {
            IpcResponse::PairPoll(i) => Ok(i),
            other => Err(unexpected(other)),
        }
    }

    async fn pair_cancel(&self) -> Result<(), ApiError> {
        match self.call(IpcRequest::PairCancel).await? {
            IpcResponse::PairCancelled => Ok(()),
            other => Err(unexpected(other)),
        }
    }

    async fn pair_join(&self, ticket: String) -> Result<Paired, ApiError> {
        match self.call(IpcRequest::PairJoin { ticket }).await? {
            IpcResponse::PairSuccess { peer_name, peer_fingerprint } => {
                Ok(Paired { name: peer_name, fingerprint: peer_fingerprint })
            }
            other => Err(unexpected(other)),
        }
    }

    async fn stop_daemon(&self) -> Result<(), ApiError> {
        match self.call(IpcRequest::StopDaemon).await? {
            IpcResponse::DaemonStopping => Ok(()),
            other => Err(unexpected(other)),
        }
    }

    async fn start_daemon(&self) -> Result<u32, ApiError> {
        if self.status().await.is_ok() {
            return Err(ApiError::Refused("Klip is already running".to_string()));
        }
        let Spawned { mut child, log_path } = spawn_daemon_process(&self.paths).map_err(ApiError::Io)?;
        let pid = child.id();
        for _ in 0..120 {
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(250)).await;
            if self.status().await.is_ok() {
                return Ok(pid);
            }
            if let Ok(Some(status)) = child.try_wait() {
                return Err(ApiError::Refused(format!(
                    "Klip stopped right after starting ({status}). Last log lines:\n{}",
                    log_tail(&log_path, 4)
                )));
            }
        }
        Err(ApiError::Refused(format!("Klip did not come up within 30 s. See the log: {}", log_path.display())))
    }

    async fn install_service(&self) -> Result<String, ApiError> {
        match tokio::task::spawn_blocking(crate::ops::install_service).await {
            Ok(Ok(Some(msg))) => Ok(msg),
            Ok(Ok(None)) => Err(ApiError::Refused("this system has no auto-start installer".to_string())),
            Ok(Err(e)) => Err(ApiError::Io(e)),
            Err(e) => Err(ApiError::Io(e.to_string())),
        }
    }
}
```

```rust
#[cfg(unix)]
fn can_open_tui() -> bool {

    std::io::stdin().is_terminal() && std::io::stdout().is_terminal() && term_ok

        DaemonClient::for_current_user(),
        paths.clone(),
    ));

    klip_tui::runtime::run(api, env).await?;
    Ok(())
}
```

- [ ] **Step 8: Write the pseudo-terminal tests** — `crates/klip-cli/tests/tui_pty.rs`:

```rust
//! The real `klip` binary in a pseudo-terminal: keystrokes in, the screen read back through a
//! terminal emulator. Covers the parts no in-process test can: raw mode, the alternate screen, signals.
#![cfg(unix)]
mod common;
use common::*;
use portable_pty::{native_pty_system, Child, CommandBuilder, MasterPty, PtySize};
use std::io::{Read, Write};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

struct Pty {
    master: Box<dyn MasterPty + Send>,
    writer: Box<dyn Write + Send>,
    child: Box<dyn Child + Send + Sync>,
    parser: Arc<Mutex<vt100::Parser>>,
}

impl Pty {
    fn spawn(args: &[&str], env: &[(String, String)], size: (u16, u16)) -> Pty {
        let pair = native_pty_system()
            .openpty(PtySize { rows: size.1, cols: size.0, pixel_width: 0, pixel_height: 0 })
            .expect("openpty");
        let mut cmd = CommandBuilder::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_klip"));
        cmd.args(args);
        cmd.env("TERM", "xterm-256color");
        for (k, v) in env {
            cmd.env(k, v);
        }
        let child = pair.slave.spawn_command(cmd).expect("spawn klip");
        drop(pair.slave);
        let mut reader = pair.master.try_clone_reader().unwrap();
        let writer = pair.master.take_writer().unwrap();
        let parser = Arc::new(Mutex::new(vt100::Parser::new(size.1, size.0, 0)));
        let feed = Arc::clone(&parser);
        std::thread::spawn(move || {
            let mut buf = [0u8; 8192];
            while let Ok(n) = reader.read(&mut buf) {
                if n == 0 {
                    break;
                }
                feed.lock().unwrap().process(&buf[..n]);
            }
        });
        Pty { master: pair.master, writer, child, parser }
    }

    fn screen(&self) -> String {
        self.parser.lock().unwrap().screen().contents()
    }
    fn alt_screen(&self) -> bool {
        self.parser.lock().unwrap().screen().alternate_screen()
    }
    fn cursor_hidden(&self) -> bool {
        self.parser.lock().unwrap().screen().hide_cursor()
    }
    fn send(&mut self, bytes: &[u8]) {
        self.writer.write_all(bytes).unwrap();
        self.writer.flush().unwrap();
    }
    fn wait_for(&self, needle: &str, secs: u64) -> bool {
        let end = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(secs);
        while Instant::now() < end {
            if self.screen().contains(needle) {
                return true;
            }
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(40));
        }
        self.screen().contains(needle)
    }
    fn wait_exit(&mut self, secs: u64) -> Option<u32> {
        let end = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(secs);
        while Instant::now() < end {
            if let Ok(Some(status)) = self.child.try_wait() {
                std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100)); // let the reader see the last bytes
                return Some(status.exit_code());
            }
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(40));
        }
        None
    }
    fn resize(&mut self, cols: u16, rows: u16) {
        self.master
            .resize(PtySize { rows, cols, pixel_width: 0, pixel_height: 0 })
            .unwrap();
        self.parser.lock().unwrap().set_size(rows, cols);
    }
    fn signal(&self, sig: i32) {
        let pid = self.child.process_id().expect("pid") as i32;
        // SAFETY: plain kill(2) on the child we spawned.
        unsafe {
            libc::kill(pid, sig);
        }
    }
}

fn running() -> (Isolated, Pty) {
    let d = Isolated::start(&format!("pty{}", std::process::id() % 1000), LAN_ONLY);
    let p = Pty::spawn(&[], &d.env_pairs(), (100, 30));
    (d, p)
}

#[test]
fn home_opens_in_the_alternate_screen_and_q_gives_the_terminal_back() {
    let (_d, mut p) = running();
    assert!(p.wait_for("ACTIVE", 15), "{}", p.screen());
    assert!(p.alt_screen());
    assert!(p.screen().contains("No devices yet"));
    p.send(b"q");
    assert_eq!(p.wait_exit(5), Some(0), "{}", p.screen());
    assert!(!p.alt_screen(), "the alternate screen must be left");
    assert!(!p.cursor_hidden(), "the cursor must be visible again");
}

#[test]
fn number_keys_open_screens_and_the_help_overlay_lists_keys() {
    let (_d, mut p) = running();
    assert!(p.wait_for("ACTIVE", 15));
    p.send(b"2");
    assert!(p.wait_for("Devices (0)", 5), "{}", p.screen());
    p.send(b"?");
    assert!(p.wait_for("quit (Klip keeps running)", 5), "{}", p.screen());
    p.send(b"q"); // closes the help
    p.send(b"1");
    assert!(p.wait_for("this device", 5), "{}", p.screen());
    p.send(b"q");
    assert_eq!(p.wait_exit(5), Some(0));
}

#[test]
fn sigterm_and_sighup_restore_the_terminal() {
    for (sig, tag) in [(libc::SIGTERM, "term"), (libc::SIGHUP, "hup")] {
        let d = Isolated::start(&format!("sig{tag}"), LAN_ONLY);
        let mut p = Pty::spawn(&[], &d.env_pairs(), (100, 30));
        assert!(p.wait_for("ACTIVE", 15), "{}", p.screen());
        p.signal(sig);
        assert!(p.wait_exit(5).is_some(), "klip did not exit after signal {sig}");
        assert!(!p.alt_screen(), "alternate screen left after signal {sig}");
        assert!(!p.cursor_hidden());
    }
}

#[test]
fn a_panic_restores_the_terminal_before_the_message_is_printed() {
    if !cfg!(debug_assertions) {
        return; // the injection switch exists in debug builds only
    }
    let d = Isolated::create("panic", LAN_ONLY);
    let mut env = d.env_pairs();
    env.push(("KLIP_TUI_TEST_PANIC".to_string(), "1".to_string()));
    let mut p = Pty::spawn(&[], &env, (100, 30));
    let code = p.wait_exit(10).expect("klip should exit after the panic");
    assert_ne!(code, 0);
    assert!(!p.alt_screen(), "alternate screen must be left");
    assert!(p.screen().contains("injected panic"), "the panic message must be readable: {}", p.screen());
}

#[test]
fn term_dumb_means_help_and_exit_2_like_without_a_terminal() {
    let d = Isolated::create("dumb", LAN_ONLY);
    let mut env = d.env_pairs();
    env.push(("TERM".to_string(), "dumb".to_string()));
    let mut p = Pty::spawn(&[], &env, (100, 30));
    assert_eq!(p.wait_exit(10), Some(2), "{}", p.screen());
    assert!(p.screen().contains("Usage: klip"), "{}", p.screen());
    assert!(!p.alt_screen());
}

#[test]
fn resizing_works_and_a_tiny_window_shows_the_message() {
    let (_d, mut p) = running();
    assert!(p.wait_for("ACTIVE", 15));
    p.resize(70, 20);
    p.send(b"\x0c"); // Ctrl+L: redraw after the emulator's own resize
    assert!(p.wait_for("ACTIVE", 5), "{}", p.screen());
    p.resize(50, 12);
    assert!(p.wait_for("Terminal too small", 5), "{}", p.screen());
    p.resize(100, 30);
    assert!(p.wait_for("ACTIVE", 5), "{}", p.screen());
    p.send(b"q");
    assert_eq!(p.wait_exit(5), Some(0));
}

#[test]
fn with_no_daemon_the_start_card_starts_it() {
    let d = Isolated::create("nodaemon", LAN_ONLY);
    let mut p = Pty::spawn(&[], &d.env_pairs(), (100, 30));
    assert!(p.wait_for("Klip is not running", 10), "{}", p.screen());
    p.send(b"s");
    assert!(p.wait_for("ACTIVE", 40), "{}", p.screen());
    p.send(b"q");
    assert_eq!(p.wait_exit(5), Some(0));
    // `d` is dropped here and runs `klip stop`.
}

#[test]
#[ignore = "manual: needs a running daemon in your normal Klip directory (not isolated)"]
fn against_the_installed_daemon() {
    let mut p = Pty::spawn(&[], &[], (100, 30));
    assert!(p.wait_for("ACTIVE", 15) || p.wait_for("PAUSED", 1), "{}", p.screen());
    println!("---- Home ----\n{}", p.screen());
    p.send(b"2");
    assert!(p.wait_for("Devices (", 5), "{}", p.screen());
    println!("---- Devices ----\n{}", p.screen());
    p.send(b"q");
    assert_eq!(p.wait_exit(5), Some(0));
}
```

- [ ] **Step 9: Write the key-fuzz test** — `crates/klip-tui/tests/keyfuzz.rs` (add `[[test]] name = "keyfuzz" required-features = ["testkit"]` to `Cargo.toml`):

```rust
//! Random keys, mouse events, pastes, resizes, ticks and replies at the pure core and the drawing
//! code: nothing may panic, and the invariants must hold after every step.
use crossterm::event::{
    KeyCode, KeyEvent, KeyEventKind, KeyEventState, KeyModifiers, MouseButton, MouseEvent, MouseEventKind,
};
use klip_tui::api::ApiError;
use klip_tui::app::*;
use klip_tui::testkit::*;
use rand::rngs::StdRng;
use rand::{Rng, SeedableRng};
use ratatui::backend::TestBackend;
use ratatui::Terminal;

const CHARS: &str = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789 ?/-_.,:;'\"[]{}\\|~`!@#$%^&*()+=<>日é";
const PASTES: [&str; 6] = ["klip1abc", "line1\nline2\r\nline3", "\x1b[31mred\x1b]0;x\x07", "日本語", "", "1234567890"];

fn random_key(rng: &mut StdRng) -> KeyEvent {
    let code = match rng.gen_range(0..24) {
        0..=11 => {
            let chars: Vec<char> = CHARS.chars().collect();
            KeyCode::Char(chars[rng.gen_range(0..chars.len())])
        }
        12 => KeyCode::Enter,
        13 => KeyCode::Esc,
        14 => KeyCode::Tab,
        15 => KeyCode::BackTab,
        16 => KeyCode::Up,
        17 => KeyCode::Down,
        18 => KeyCode::Left,
        19 => KeyCode::Right,
        20 => KeyCode::PageUp,
        21 => KeyCode::PageDown,
        22 => KeyCode::Backspace,
        _ => *[KeyCode::Delete, KeyCode::Home, KeyCode::End, KeyCode::F(5)]
            .get(rng.gen_range(0..4))
            .unwrap(),
    };
    let mut mods = KeyModifiers::NONE;
    if rng.gen_bool(0.1) {
        mods |= KeyModifiers::CONTROL;
    }
    if rng.gen_bool(0.1) {
        mods |= KeyModifiers::ALT;
    }
    if rng.gen_bool(0.1) {
        mods |= KeyModifiers::SHIFT;
    }
    KeyEvent { code, modifiers: mods, kind: KeyEventKind::Press, state: KeyEventState::NONE }
}

fn random_size(rng: &mut StdRng) -> (u16, u16) {
    match rng.gen_range(0..100) {
        0..=14 => (rng.gen_range(0..4), rng.gen_range(0..4)),
        15..=24 => (rng.gen_range(1..40), rng.gen_range(1..20)),
        25 => (rng.gen_range(200..500), rng.gen_range(100..200)),
        _ => (rng.gen_range(50..160), rng.gen_range(10..50)),
    }
}

fn random_reply(rng: &mut StdRng) -> Reply {
    // Later tasks add their own replies to this pool (pairing, history, send, log, settings).
    let peers = (0..rng.gen_range(0..5u8))
        .map(|i| sample_peer(&format!("device {i} 日本 \x1b[31m"), rng.gen_bool(0.5), i + 1))
        .collect();
    match rng.gen_range(0..12) {
        0..=2 => Reply::Status(Ok(sample_status(peers))),
        3 => Reply::Status(Err(ApiError::DaemonDown)),
        4 => Reply::Status(Err(ApiError::Timeout(15))),
        5 => Reply::Hello(Ok(sample_hello())),
        6 => Reply::Hello(Err(ApiError::Unsupported)),
        7 => Reply::Paused(Ok(rng.gen_range(0..400_000_000))),
        8 => Reply::Resumed(Err(ApiError::Refused("no".into()))),
        9 => Reply::Unpaired(Ok("x\x1b".into())),
        10 => Reply::UnpairedAll(Ok((vec!["a".into()], vec![("b".into(), "\x1b[2J".into())]))),
        _ => Reply::DaemonStarted(Err(ApiError::Refused("boom\nmore".into()))),
    }
}

fn random_msg(rng: &mut StdRng, now: &mut u64, size: (u16, u16)) -> Msg {
    match rng.gen_range(0..100) {
        0..=64 => Msg::Key(random_key(rng)),
        65..=69 => Msg::Mouse(MouseEvent {
            kind: match rng.gen_range(0..4) {
                0 => MouseEventKind::Down(MouseButton::Left),
                1 => MouseEventKind::Up(MouseButton::Left),
                2 => MouseEventKind::ScrollUp,
                _ => MouseEventKind::ScrollDown,
            },
            column: rng.gen_range(0..size.0.saturating_add(10).max(1)),
            row: rng.gen_range(0..size.1.saturating_add(5).max(1)),
            modifiers: KeyModifiers::NONE,
        }),
        70..=74 => Msg::Paste(PASTES[rng.gen_range(0..PASTES.len())].to_string()),
        75..=79 => {
            let (w, h) = random_size(rng);
            Msg::Resize(w, h)
        }
        80..=89 => {
            *now += rng.gen_range(0..1500);
            Msg::Tick { mono_ms: *now, wall_ms: 1_700_000_000_000 + *now }
        }
        _ => Msg::Reply(random_reply(rng)),
    }
}

fn run(seed: u64, mut app: AppState, messages: usize) {
    let mut rng = StdRng::seed_from_u64(seed);
    let theme = plain_theme();
    let mut terminal = Terminal::new(TestBackend::new(app.size.0, app.size.1)).unwrap();
    let mut now = 0u64;
    for i in 0..messages {
        let msg = random_msg(&mut rng, &mut now, app.size);
        let resized = matches!(msg, Msg::Resize(..));
        let _effects = update(&mut app, msg);
        if resized {
            terminal.backend_mut().resize(app.size.0, app.size.1);
        }
        // Invariants.
        let peers = app.status.as_ref().map(|s| s.peers.len()).unwrap_or(0);
        assert!(app.devices.selected <= peers.saturating_sub(1), "selection out of range at message {i}");
        assert!(app.toasts.len() <= 3);
        // Drawing at every third message (and always after a resize) keeps the run affordable.
        if resized || i % 3 == 0 {
            terminal.draw(|f| klip_tui::view::view(&app, &theme, f)).unwrap();
        }
        if app.quit {
            app.quit = false; // keep going: quitting is just a flag here
        }
    }
}

fn base(conn: Conn) -> AppState {
    let mut a = AppState::new((100, 30));
    if matches!(conn, Conn::Up) {
        a.status = Some(sample_status(vec![sample_peer("Vivo V2253", true, 1), sample_peer("Pixel 8", false, 2)]));
    }
    a.conn = conn;
    a
}

#[test]
fn random_input_never_panics_when_the_daemon_is_up() {
    for seed in 0..4 {
        run(seed, base(Conn::Up), 10_000);
    }
}

#[test]
fn random_input_never_panics_when_the_daemon_is_down_or_connecting() {
    run(100, base(Conn::Down { reason: "Klip is not running".into() }), 8_000);
    run(101, base(Conn::Connecting), 8_000);
}
```

```rust
    fn tail_keeps_the_last_columns_whole_clusters_only() {
        assert_eq!(tail("abcdef", 3), "def");
        assert_eq!(tail("ab", 5), "ab");
        assert_eq!(tail("日本語", 5), "本語");
        assert_eq!(tail("ae\u{301}", 1), "e\u{301}");
        assert_eq!(tail("abc", 0), "");
    }

    #[test]
    fn wrap_rows_breaks_long_lines_and_keeps_newlines() {
        assert_eq!(wrap_rows("abcdef", 4), ["abcd", "ef"]);
        assert_eq!(wrap_rows("ab\ncd", 4), ["ab", "cd"]);
        assert_eq!(wrap_rows("", 4), [""]);
```

Run `cargo test -p klip-tui sanitize 2>&1 | tail -4` → compile error. Then add above the tests module:

```rust
/// The last `max_cols` columns of `s`, whole grapheme clusters only.
pub fn tail(s: &str, max_cols: usize) -> String {
    let mut cols = 0;
    let mut parts: Vec<&str> = Vec::new();
    for g in s.graphemes(true).rev() {
        let w = width(g);
        if cols + w > max_cols {
            break;
        }
        cols += w;
        parts.push(g);
    }
    parts.reverse();
    parts.concat()
}

/// Hard-wrap `s` (already cleaned) into rows of at most `cols` columns; a newline starts a new row.
pub fn wrap_rows(s: &str, cols: usize) -> Vec<String> {
    let cols = cols.max(1);
    let mut rows = Vec::new();
    for line in s.split('\n') {
        let mut cur = String::new();
        let mut used = 0;
        for g in line.graphemes(true) {
            let w = width(g);
            if used + w > cols && used > 0 {
                rows.push(std::mem::take(&mut cur));
                used = 0;
            }
            cur.push_str(g);
            used += w;
        }
        rows.push(cur);
    }
    rows
}
```
Run again → pass.

```rust
        },
```
(h) `on_down`: the replaced-modal pattern becomes `Some(Modal::Confirm { .. }) | Some(Modal::Pause { .. }) | Some(Modal::PairApproval { .. })`.

(i) `on_reply` gets the five arms:
```rust
        Reply::PairStarted { session, result } => crate::screens::pair::on_started(app, session, result),
        Reply::PairPolled { session, result } => crate::screens::pair::on_polled(app, session, result),
        Reply::PairDecided { session, result } => crate::screens::pair::on_decided(app, session, result),
        Reply::PairCancelled(_) => {}
        Reply::PairJoined { session, result } => crate::screens::pair::on_joined(app, session, result),
```
(j) `runtime.rs`: `Runtime::new` also sets `app.invert_qr = !env.light;`. In `run_effect` add
```rust
        Effect::PairStart { ttl_secs, lan, session, restart } => {
            if restart {
                let _ = api.pair_cancel().await; // one task, so the cancel always happens before the start
            }
            Reply::PairStarted { session, result: api.pair_start(ttl_secs, lan).await }
        }
        Effect::PairPoll { session } => Reply::PairPolled { session, result: api.pair_poll().await },
        Effect::PairDecide { accept, session } => Reply::PairDecided { session, result: api.pair_decide(accept).await },
        Effect::PairCancel => Reply::PairCancelled(api.pair_cancel().await),
        Effect::PairJoin { ticket, session } => Reply::PairJoined { session, result: api.pair_join(ticket).await },
```
and in `failed_reply`:
```rust
        Effect::PairStart { session, .. } => Reply::PairStarted { session: *session, result: Err(e) },
        Effect::PairPoll { session } => Reply::PairPolled { session: *session, result: Err(e) },
        Effect::PairDecide { session, .. } => Reply::PairDecided { session: *session, result: Err(e) },
        Effect::PairCancel => Reply::PairCancelled(Err(e)),
        Effect::PairJoin { session, .. } => Reply::PairJoined { session: *session, result: Err(e) },
```

```rust
        let mut a = app();
        update(&mut a, ch('3'));
        started(&mut a);
        poll(&mut a, pending());
        a.inflight.status = true;
        update(&mut a, Msg::Reply(Reply::Status(Err(crate::api::ApiError::DaemonDown))));
        assert!(matches!(a.modal, Some(Modal::Notice { .. })));

        let t = "klip1".to_string() + &"A".repeat(120);
        let inverted = qr_lines(&t, true).unwrap();
        let normal = qr_lines(&t, false).unwrap();
        assert!(inverted.len() > 10 && inverted.len() == normal.len());
        assert_ne!(inverted, normal);
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `screens/pair.rs`** (above the tests)

```rust
//! The Pair screen: show a code for another device, or join with a ticket. A request from another
//! device is answered in a modal on any screen (`Modal::PairApproval`).
use crate::api::{ApiError, PairPollInfo, PairTicket, Paired};
use crate::app::{AppState, Conn, Effect, Modal, Screen, ToastKind};
use crate::fmt;
use crate::sanitize::{clean, one_line, tail, truncate, width};
use crate::textbuf::TextBuf;
use crate::theme::Theme;
use crossterm::event::{KeyCode, KeyEvent, KeyModifiers};
use ratatui::layout::{Constraint, Layout, Rect};
use ratatui::text::{Line, Span};
use ratatui::widgets::{Block, Paragraph, Wrap};
use ratatui::Frame;

/// Validity choices for a code, in seconds (the `t` key cycles through them).
pub const TTLS: [u64; 4] = [60, 120, 300, 600];
const POLL_EVERY_MS: u64 = 500;
/// After approving, how long to wait for the other device to finish before saying so.
const APPROVED_WAIT_MS: u64 = 15_000;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum PairMode {
    Show,
    Join,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum PairResult {
    Linked(String),
    Failed(String),
    Expired,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct PairState {
    pub mode: PairMode,
    /// Bumped whenever a window is started, cancelled or answered; replies for an old value are dropped.
    pub session: u64,
    /// True while a window this TUI opened is open.
    pub ours: bool,
    pub starting: bool,
    pub ticket: Option<PairTicket>,
    pub started_ms: u64,
    pub lan: bool,
    pub ttl_idx: usize,
    pub join: TextBuf,
    pub join_focus: bool,
    pub joining: bool,
    pub result: Option<PairResult>,
    /// After Approve: keep polling until this time for the "paired" notice.
    pub awaiting_paired_until_ms: u64,
    pub qr: Option<Vec<String>>,
}

impl Default for PairState {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            mode: PairMode::Show,
            session: 0,
            ours: false,
            starting: false,
            ticket: None,
            started_ms: 0,
            lan: false,
            ttl_idx: 1,
            join: TextBuf::new(4096),
            join_focus: false,
            joining: false,
            result: None,
            awaiting_paired_until_ms: 0,
            qr: None,
        }
    }
}

/// The QR code as lines of half-block characters. `invert` draws light modules as blocks, which is
/// right for the usual light-text-on-dark terminal.
pub fn qr_lines(ticket: &str, invert: bool) -> Option<Vec<String>> {
    use qrcode::render::unicode::Dense1x2;
    let code = qrcode::QrCode::new(ticket.as_bytes()).ok()?;
    let (dark, light) = if invert { (Dense1x2::Light, Dense1x2::Dark) } else { (Dense1x2::Dark, Dense1x2::Light) };
    let drawn = code.render::<Dense1x2>().dark_color(dark).light_color(light).quiet_zone(true).build();
    Some(drawn.lines().map(|l| l.to_string()).collect())
}

pub fn text_focus(app: &AppState) -> bool {
    app.pair.mode == PairMode::Join && app.pair.join_focus
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ windows

fn start_window(app: &mut AppState, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>, restart: bool) {
    if !matches!(app.conn, Conn::Up) {
        app.toast(ToastKind::Warn, "Klip is not running: start it first (Home, s)");
        return;
    }
    app.pair.session += 1;
    app.pair.starting = true;
    app.pair.result = None;
    app.pair.qr = None;
    fx.push(Effect::PairStart {
        ttl_secs: TTLS[app.pair.ttl_idx],
        lan: app.pair.lan,
        session: app.pair.session,
        restart,
    });
}

fn cancel_window(app: &mut AppState, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    if app.pair.ours || app.pair.starting {
        app.pair.session += 1;
        app.pair.ours = false;
        app.pair.starting = false;
        app.pair.ticket = None;
        app.pair.qr = None;
        fx.push(Effect::PairCancel);
    }
}

pub fn on_enter(app: &mut AppState, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    if app.pair.mode == PairMode::Show && !app.pair.ours && !app.pair.starting && app.pair.result.is_none() {
        start_window(app, fx, false);
    }
    if app.pair.mode == PairMode::Join {
        app.pair.join_focus = true;
    }
}

pub fn on_leave(app: &mut AppState, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    let answering = matches!(app.modal, Some(Modal::PairApproval { .. })) || app.pair.awaiting_paired_until_ms > app.mono_ms;
    if !answering {
        cancel_window(app, fx);
    }
    app.pair.join_focus = false;
}

pub fn on_daemon_down(app: &mut AppState) {
    let p = &mut app.pair;
    p.ours = false;
    p.starting = false;
    p.ticket = None;
    p.qr = None;
    p.joining = false;
    p.awaiting_paired_until_ms = 0;
    p.session += 1;
}

pub fn on_tick(app: &mut AppState, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    if app.pair.awaiting_paired_until_ms > 0 && app.mono_ms >= app.pair.awaiting_paired_until_ms {
        app.pair.awaiting_paired_until_ms = 0;
        app.pair.result = Some(PairResult::Failed(
            "The other device did not finish linking. Check its screen and try again.".to_string(),
        ));
        app.dirty = true;
    }
    let open_elsewhere = app.status.as_ref().map(|s| s.pairing_open).unwrap_or(false);
    let wanted = open_elsewhere || app.pair.ours || app.pair.awaiting_paired_until_ms > 0;
    if wanted && !app.inflight.pair_poll && app.mono_ms.saturating_sub(app.clock.pair_poll_ms) >= POLL_EVERY_MS {
        app.inflight.pair_poll = true;
        app.clock.pair_poll_ms = app.mono_ms;
        fx.push(Effect::PairPoll { session: app.pair.session });
    }
    // On this screen in "show" mode there should always be a window, unless the last one ended with a result.
    if app.screen == Screen::Pair
        && app.pair.mode == PairMode::Show
        && matches!(app.conn, Conn::Up)
        && !app.pair.ours
        && !app.pair.starting
        && app.pair.result.is_none()
        && !open_elsewhere
    {
        start_window(app, fx, false);
    }
}

// ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ answers

/// Answer the request shown in the approval modal.
pub fn decide(app: &mut AppState, accept: bool, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    app.pair.session += 1;
    fx.push(Effect::PairDecide { accept, session: app.pair.session });
    if accept {
        app.pair.awaiting_paired_until_ms = app.mono_ms + APPROVED_WAIT_MS;
        app.toast(ToastKind::Info, "Approved: waiting for the other device to finish");
    } else {
        app.toast(ToastKind::Info, "Declined");
    }
}

pub fn on_started(app: &mut AppState, session: u64, result: Result<PairTicket, ApiError>) {
    if session != app.pair.session {
        return;
    }
    app.pair.starting = false;
    match result {
        Ok(t) => {
            app.pair.qr = qr_lines(&t.ticket, app.invert_qr);
            app.pair.ticket = Some(t);
            app.pair.started_ms = app.mono_ms;
            app.pair.ours = true;
        }
        Err(e) => app.pair.result = Some(PairResult::Failed(e.plain())),
    }
}

pub fn on_polled(app: &mut AppState, session: u64, result: Result<PairPollInfo, ApiError>) {
    app.inflight.pair_poll = false;
    if session != app.pair.session {
        return;
    }
    let Ok(info) = result else { return }; // a dead daemon is handled by the status poll
    if let Some(name) = info.paired {
        let name = one_line(&name);
        app.pair.result = Some(PairResult::Linked(name.clone()));
        app.pair.awaiting_paired_until_ms = 0;
        app.pair.ours = false;
        app.pair.ticket = None;
        app.pair.qr = None;
        if matches!(app.modal, Some(Modal::PairApproval { .. })) {
            app.modal = None;
        }
        app.toast(ToastKind::Ok, format!("Linked with {name}"));
        app.clock.status_ms = 0;
        return;
    }
    if let Some(p) = info.pending {
        if !matches!(app.modal, Some(Modal::PairApproval { .. })) {
            app.modal = Some(Modal::PairApproval {
                name: one_line(&p.device_name),
                fingerprint: one_line(&p.fingerprint),
            });
        }
        return;
    }
    if matches!(app.modal, Some(Modal::PairApproval { .. })) {
        app.modal = Some(Modal::Notice {
            title: "That request is gone".to_string(),
            body: "The other device stopped waiting. Show a new code and let it scan again.".to_string(),
        });
    }
    if !info.window_open && app.pair.ticket.is_some() && app.pair.awaiting_paired_until_ms == 0 {
        app.pair.result = Some(PairResult::Expired);
        app.pair.ticket = None;
        app.pair.qr = None;
        app.pair.ours = false;
    }
}

pub fn on_decided(app: &mut AppState, session: u64, result: Result<PairPollInfo, ApiError>) {
    if session != app.pair.session {
        return;
    }
    if let Err(e) = result {
        app.pair.awaiting_paired_until_ms = 0;
        if matches!(&e, ApiError::Refused(m) if m.contains("no pairing request")) {
            app.modal = Some(Modal::Notice {
                title: "That request is gone".to_string(),
                body: "The other device stopped waiting. Show a new code and let it scan again.".to_string(),
            });
        } else {
            app.toast(ToastKind::Error, format!("Could not answer the request: {}", e.plain()));
        }
    }
}

pub fn on_joined(app: &mut AppState, session: u64, result: Result<Paired, ApiError>) {
    if session != app.pair.session {
        return;
    }
    app.pair.joining = false;
    match result {
        Ok(p) => {
            let name = one_line(&p.name);
            app.pair.join.clear();
            app.pair.result = Some(PairResult::Linked(name.clone()));
            app.toast(ToastKind::Ok, format!("Linked with {name}"));
            app.clock.status_ms = 0;
        }
        Err(e) => app.pair.result = Some(PairResult::Failed(e.plain())),
    }
}

// --------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- keys

fn switch_mode(app: &mut AppState, mode: PairMode, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    if app.pair.mode == mode {
        return;
    }
    app.pair.result = None;
    match mode {
        PairMode::Show => {
            app.pair.mode = PairMode::Show;
            app.pair.join_focus = false;
            start_window(app, fx, false);
        }
        PairMode::Join => {
            cancel_window(app, fx);
            app.pair.mode = PairMode::Join;
            app.pair.join_focus = true;
        }
    }
}

pub fn key(app: &mut AppState, k: KeyEvent, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    if text_focus(app) {
        return join_key(app, k, fx);
    }
    match (k.code, app.pair.mode) {
        (KeyCode::Char('c'), _) => switch_mode(app, PairMode::Show, fx),
        (KeyCode::Char('j'), _) => switch_mode(app, PairMode::Join, fx),
        (KeyCode::Char('n'), PairMode::Show) => start_window(app, fx, true),
        (KeyCode::Char('l'), PairMode::Show) => {
            app.pair.lan = !app.pair.lan;
            start_window(app, fx, true);
        }
        (KeyCode::Char('t'), PairMode::Show) => {
            app.pair.ttl_idx = (app.pair.ttl_idx + 1) % TTLS.len();
            start_window(app, fx, true);
        }
        (KeyCode::Enter, PairMode::Join) => app.pair.join_focus = true,
        _ => {}
    }
}

fn join_key(app: &mut AppState, k: KeyEvent, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    let ctrl = k.modifiers.contains(KeyModifiers::CONTROL);
    let j = &mut app.pair.join;
    match k.code {
        KeyCode::Esc => app.pair.join_focus = false,
        KeyCode::Enter => submit_join(app, fx),
        KeyCode::Backspace => j.backspace(),
        KeyCode::Delete => j.delete(),
        KeyCode::Left => j.left(),
        KeyCode::Right => j.right(),
        KeyCode::Home => j.home(),
        KeyCode::End => j.end(),
        KeyCode::Char('u') if ctrl => j.clear(),
        KeyCode::Char('w') if ctrl => j.delete_word_back(),
        KeyCode::Char(c) if !ctrl && !c.is_whitespace() => {
            j.insert_char(c);
        }
        _ => {}
    }
}

fn submit_join(app: &mut AppState, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    let raw = app.pair.join.text();
    let ticket = raw.trim().trim_matches(|c| c == '"' || c == '\'').to_string();
    if ticket.is_empty() {
        app.toast(ToastKind::Warn, "Paste the ticket first");
    } else if !ticket.starts_with("klip1") {
        app.pair.result = Some(PairResult::Failed(
            "That does not look like a Klip ticket: it starts with klip1.".to_string(),
        ));
    } else if !matches!(app.conn, Conn::Up) {
        app.toast(ToastKind::Warn, "Klip is not running: start it first (Home, s)");
    } else {
        app.pair.joining = true;
        app.pair.session += 1;
        app.pair.result = None;
        fx.push(Effect::PairJoin { ticket, session: app.pair.session });
    }
}

pub fn paste(app: &mut AppState, text: &str) {
    if text_focus(app) {
        let compact: String = text.chars().filter(|c| !c.is_whitespace()).collect();
        app.pair.join.insert_str(&compact);
    }
}

// -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- drawing

fn result_line(r: &PairResult, theme: &Theme) -> Line<'static> {
    match r {
        PairResult::Linked(name) => Line::from(Span::styled(format!("✓ Linked with {name}"), theme.st_signal())),
        PairResult::Failed(m) => Line::from(Span::styled(format!("✗ {}", one_line(m)), theme.st_bad())),
        PairResult::Expired => {
            Line::from(Span::styled("The code expired. Press n for a new one.", theme.st_warn()))
        }
    }
}

pub fn view(app: &AppState, theme: &Theme, f: &mut Frame, area: Rect) {
    let block = Block::bordered()
        .title(Span::styled(" Pair a device ", theme.st_title()))
        .border_style(theme.st_faint());
    let inner = block.inner(area);
    f.render_widget(block, area);
    let [modes, body] = Layout::vertical([Constraint::Length(2), Constraint::Min(0)]).areas(inner);
    let chip = |label: &str, on: bool| {
        Span::styled(label.to_string(), if on { theme.st_selected() } else { theme.st_dim() })
    };
    f.render_widget(
        Paragraph::new(Line::from(vec![
            chip(" c  Show a code ", app.pair.mode == PairMode::Show),
            Span::raw("  "),
            chip(" j  Join with a ticket ", app.pair.mode == PairMode::Join),
        ])),
        modes,
    );
    match app.pair.mode {
        PairMode::Show => show(app, theme, f, body),
        PairMode::Join => join(app, theme, f, body),
    }
}

fn show(app: &AppState, theme: &Theme, f: &mut Frame, area: Rect) {
    let p = &app.pair;
    let mut lines: Vec<Line<'static>> = Vec::new();
    if let Some(r) = &p.result {
        lines.push(result_line(r, theme));
        lines.push(Line::from(""));
    }
    let Some(t) = &p.ticket else {
        let msg = if p.starting {
            "Opening a pairing window…"
        } else if matches!(app.conn, Conn::Up) {
            "Press n for a new code."
        } else {
            "Klip is not running."
        };
        lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(msg, theme.st_dim())));
        f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(lines), area);
        return;
    };
    let w = area.width as usize;
    let ticket = clean(&t.ticket);
    let ticket_rows: Vec<String> = ticket
        .as_bytes()
        .chunks(w.saturating_sub(2).max(1))
        .map(|c| format!("  {}", String::from_utf8_lossy(c)))
        .collect();
    let fixed = lines.len() + 1 + ticket_rows.len() + 1 + 2;
    let qr_fits = p.qr.as_ref().map(|q| {
        q.len() + fixed <= area.height as usize && q.iter().all(|l| width(l) <= w)
    });
    match (&p.qr, qr_fits) {
        (Some(q), Some(true)) => {
            for l in q {
                lines.push(Line::from(l.clone()).centered());
            }
        }
        _ => lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
            "The QR code does not fit this window: use the ticket below, or make the window bigger.",
            theme.st_dim(),
        ))),
    }
    lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled("Ticket (single use):", theme.st_dim())));
    for r in ticket_rows {
        lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(r, theme.st_signal())));
    }
    let left = t.expires_in_secs.saturating_sub(app.mono_ms.saturating_sub(p.started_ms) / 1000);
    lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
        truncate(
            &format!(
                "This device {} · expires in {} · LAN addresses {}",
                one_line(&t.host_fingerprint),
                fmt::mmss(left),
                if p.lan { "included" } else { "off" }
            ),
            w,
        ),
        theme.st_text(),
    )));
    lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
        format!("n new code · l LAN {} · t validity {} s", if p.lan { "off" } else { "on" }, TTLS[p.ttl_idx]),
        theme.st_dim(),
    )));
    f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(lines), area);
}

fn join(app: &AppState, theme: &Theme, f: &mut Frame, area: Rect) {
    let p = &app.pair;
    let [label, field, msg] =
        Layout::vertical([Constraint::Length(2), Constraint::Length(3), Constraint::Min(0)]).areas(area);
    f.render_widget(
        Paragraph::new("Run `klip pair` on the other computer (or open “Show my code” in the app), then paste the klip1… ticket here.")
            .style(theme.st_dim())
            .wrap(Wrap { trim: true }),
        label,
    );
    let block = Block::bordered().border_style(if p.join_focus { theme.st_signal() } else { theme.st_faint() });
    let inner = block.inner(field);
    f.render_widget(block, field);
    let shown = tail(&clean(&p.join.text()), (inner.width as usize).saturating_sub(1));
    f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(shown.clone()).style(theme.st_text()), inner);
    if p.join_focus && inner.width > 0 && inner.height > 0 {
        f.set_cursor_position((inner.x + width(&shown) as u16, inner.y));
    }
    let mut lines: Vec<Line<'static>> = Vec::new();
    if p.joining {
        lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
            "Waiting for the other device to approve… (Esc only stops waiting here; the other device may still link)",
            theme.st_warn(),
        )));
    }
    if let Some(r) = &p.result {
        lines.push(result_line(r, theme));
    }
    f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(lines).wrap(Wrap { trim: true }), msg);
}
```

```rust
pub fn key(app: &mut AppState, k: KeyEvent, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    match app.screen {
        Screen::Home => home::key(app, k, fx),
        Screen::Devices => devices::key(app, k, fx),
        Screen::Pair => pair::key(app, k, fx),
        _ => {}
    }
}

pub fn paste(app: &mut AppState, text: &str, _fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    if app.screen == Screen::Pair {
        pair::paste(app, text);
    }
}

pub fn text_focus(app: &AppState) -> bool {
    match app.screen {
        Screen::Pair => pair::text_focus(app),
        _ => false,
    }
}

pub fn on_enter(app: &mut AppState, to: Screen, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    if to == Screen::Pair {
        pair::on_enter(app, fx);
    }
}

pub fn on_leave(app: &mut AppState, from: Screen, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    if from == Screen::Pair {
        pair::on_leave(app, fx);
    }
}

pub fn on_tick(app: &mut AppState, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    pair::on_tick(app, fx);
}

pub fn on_daemon_down(app: &mut AppState) {
    pair::on_daemon_down(app);
}
```

```rust
#[tokio::test]
async fn pairing_through_the_screen_shows_the_code_asks_before_linking_and_confirms() {
    use klip_tui::api::PendingPairInfo;
    let fake = FakeDaemon::new();
    let mut h = harness(&fake, "pair");
    assert!(h.wait_for("ACTIVE", 3000).await);
    h.text("3");
    assert!(h.wait_for("klip1TESTTICKET", 3000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    assert!(h.screen().contains("Ticket (single use):"));
    fake.with(|s| {
        s.poll.pending = Some(PendingPairInfo { device_name: "Pixel 8".into(), fingerprint: "DDDD-EEEE-FFFF".into() });
    });
    assert!(h.wait_for("Link “Pixel 8”?", 3000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    assert!(h.screen().contains("DDDD-EEEE-FFFF"));
    h.text("y");
    assert!(fake.calls().iter().any(|c| c == "pair_decide true"));
    fake.with(|s| s.poll.paired = Some("Pixel 8".into()));
    assert!(h.wait_for("Linked with Pixel 8", 3000).await, "{}", h.screen());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn leaving_the_pair_screen_closes_the_window() {
    let fake = FakeDaemon::new();
    let mut h = harness(&fake, "pairleave");
    assert!(h.wait_for("ACTIVE", 3000).await);
    h.text("3");
    assert!(h.wait_for("klip1TESTTICKET", 3000).await);
    h.text("1");
    h.pump(300).await;
    assert!(fake.calls().iter().any(|c| c == "pair_cancel"), "{:?}", fake.calls());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn joining_with_a_pasted_ticket_reports_success_and_failure() {
    let fake = FakeDaemon::new();
    let mut h = harness(&fake, "join");
    assert!(h.wait_for("ACTIVE", 3000).await);
    h.text("3");
    h.text("j");
    h.paste("klip1JOINJOINJOIN\n");
    h.key(KeyCode::Enter);
    assert!(h.wait_for("Linked with Pixel 8", 3000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    fake.with(|s| s.join_reply = Err(ApiError::Refused("invalid or expired pairing code".into())));
    h.key(KeyCode::Char('u')); // typed into the field after the earlier Esc? the field kept focus
    h.key(KeyCode::Backspace);
    h.paste("klip1AGAIN");
    h.key(KeyCode::Enter);
    assert!(h.wait_for("invalid or expired pairing code", 3000).await, "{}", h.screen());
}
```

and a function (Task 17 extends it with the Send screen's result):
```rust
fn on_sent(app: &mut AppState, res: Result<SendReport, ApiError>) {
    match res {
        Ok(r) if r.delivered.is_empty() && r.failed.is_empty() => {
            app.toast(ToastKind::Warn, "No linked devices: nothing was sent. Pair one first (3).")
        }
        Ok(r) if r.failed.is_empty() => {
            app.toast(ToastKind::Ok, format!("Sent {} to {} device(s)", fmt::bytes(r.bytes), r.delivered.len()))
        }
        Ok(r) => app.toast(
            ToastKind::Warn,
            format!("Delivered to {}, failed for {}", r.delivered.len(), r.failed.len()),
        ),
        Err(e) => app.toast(ToastKind::Error, format!("Could not send: {}", e.plain())),
    }
}
```
`runtime.rs` `run_effect`: `Effect::History { limit } => Reply::History(api.history(limit).await), Effect::CopyItem(id) => Reply::Copied(api.copy_to_clipboard(id).await), Effect::ClearHistory => Reply::HistoryCleared(api.clear_history().await), Effect::Send(text) => Reply::Sent(api.send(text).await),` and the matching `failed_reply` arms (`Effect::History { .. } => Reply::History(Err(e))`, `Effect::CopyItem(_) => Reply::Copied(Err(e))`, `Effect::ClearHistory => Reply::HistoryCleared(Err(e))`, `Effect::Send(_) => Reply::Sent(Err(e))`).

```rust
        vec![
            sample_item(1, "https://example.com/Docs", false),
            sample_item(2, "api_key_secret0123456789ABCDEF", true),
            sample_item(3, "fn main() {\n    println!(\"hi\");\n}", false),
        ]
    }

    #[test]
    fn entering_the_screen_fetches_the_history_at_once() {
        let mut a = app_with(vec![]);
        a.screen = Screen::Home;
        let fx = update(&mut a, ch('4'));
        assert!(matches!(fx.as_slice(), [Effect::History { limit: 50 }]), "{fx:?}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn it_refetches_when_the_clip_count_changes_every_ten_seconds_and_on_r_but_not_otherwise() {
        let mut a = app_with(three());
        a.history.last_total = 128; // sample_status has 128 clips synced
        a.clock.history_ms = 1_000;
        let t = |a: &mut AppState, ms| update(a, Msg::Tick { mono_ms: ms, wall_ms: ms });
        assert!(t(&mut a, 2_000).iter().all(|e| !matches!(e, Effect::History { .. })), "nothing changed");
        a.status.as_mut().unwrap().total_synced_clips = 129;
        assert!(t(&mut a, 2_100).iter().any(|e| matches!(e, Effect::History { .. })), "a new clip arrived");
        a.inflight.history = false;
        assert!(t(&mut a, 3_000).iter().all(|e| !matches!(e, Effect::History { .. })));
        assert!(t(&mut a, 12_200).iter().any(|e| matches!(e, Effect::History { .. })), "10 s safety net");
        a.inflight.history = false;
        let fx = update(&mut a, ch('r'));
        assert!(fx.iter().any(|e| matches!(e, Effect::History { .. })));
    }

    #[test]
    fn it_does_not_poll_when_the_screen_is_not_visible() {
        let mut a = app_with(three());
        a.screen = Screen::Home;
        a.status.as_mut().unwrap().total_synced_clips = 999;
        let fx = update(&mut a, Msg::Tick { mono_ms: 60_000, wall_ms: 60_000 });
        assert!(fx.iter().all(|e| !matches!(e, Effect::History { .. })));
    }

    #[test]
    fn selection_moves_and_stays_in_range() {
        let mut a = app_with(three());
        update(&mut a, ch('j'));
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Down));
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Down));
        assert_eq!(a.history.selected, 2);
        update(&mut a, ch('g'));
        assert_eq!(a.history.selected, 0);
        update(&mut a, ch('G'));
        assert_eq!(a.history.selected, 2);
    }

    #[test]
    fn search_is_case_insensitive_live_and_escape_clears_it() {
        let mut a = app_with(three());
        update(&mut a, ch('/'));
        assert!(a.text_focus());
        for c in "DOCS".chars() {
            update(&mut a, ch(c));
        }
        assert_eq!(a.history.visible(), vec![0]);
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter)); // leave the field, keep the filter
        assert!(!a.text_focus());
        assert_eq!(a.history.visible(), vec![0]);
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Esc)); // clears the filter
        assert_eq!(a.history.visible().len(), 3);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_hidden_secret_cannot_be_found_by_its_content_until_it_is_revealed() {
        let mut a = app_with(three());
        a.history.filter.insert_str("secret0123");
        assert!(a.history.visible().is_empty(), "the content of a hidden secret is not searchable");
        a.history.selected = 0;
        a.history.filter.clear();
        update(&mut a, ch('j')); // select the secret
        update(&mut a, ch('v'));
        a.history.filter.insert_str("secret0123");
        assert_eq!(a.history.visible(), vec![1]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn enter_copies_back_only_when_the_daemon_can() {
        let mut a = app_with(three());
        let fx = update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        assert!(matches!(fx.as_slice(), [Effect::CopyItem(id)] if *id == [1; 16]), "{fx:?}");
        a.hello = HelloState::Old;
        let fx = update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        assert!(fx.is_empty());
        assert!(a.toasts.iter().any(|t| t.text.contains("older")), "{:?}", a.toasts);
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_item_whose_content_was_dropped_cannot_be_copied_or_resent() {
        let mut big = sample_item(9, "x", false);
        big.full_content.clear();
        big.size_bytes = 300_000;
        let mut a = app_with(vec![big]);
        assert!(update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter)).is_empty());
        assert!(update(&mut a, ch('s')).is_empty());
        assert!(a.toasts.iter().filter(|t| t.text.contains("too large")).count() >= 1);
    }

    #[test]
    fn s_sends_the_text_again_but_not_binary_content() {
        let mut a = app_with(three());
        let fx = update(&mut a, ch('s'));
        assert!(matches!(fx.as_slice(), [Effect::Send(t)] if t == "https://example.com/Docs"), "{fx:?}");
        let mut png = sample_item(5, "", false);
        png.full_content = vec![0x89, b'P', b'N', b'G', 0xff, 0xfe];
        png.size_bytes = 6;
        let mut a = app_with(vec![png]);
        assert!(update(&mut a, ch('s')).is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn reveal_toggles_per_item_and_is_forgotten_on_leaving() {
        let mut a = app_with(three());
        update(&mut a, ch('j'));
        update(&mut a, ch('v'));
        assert!(a.history.revealed.contains(&[2; 16]));
        update(&mut a, ch('v'));
        assert!(a.history.revealed.is_empty());
        update(&mut a, ch('v'));
        update(&mut a, ch('1'));
        assert!(a.history.revealed.is_empty(), "leaving the screen hides secrets again");
    }

    #[test]
    fn clearing_asks_first_and_needs_a_capable_daemon() {
        let mut a = app_with(three());
        update(&mut a, ch('C'));
        assert!(matches!(a.modal, Some(Modal::Confirm { .. })));
        let fx = update(&mut a, ch('y'));
        assert!(matches!(fx.as_slice(), [Effect::ClearHistory]), "{fx:?}");
        let mut a = app_with(three());
        a.hello = HelloState::Old;
        update(&mut a, ch('C'));
        assert!(a.modal.is_none());
        assert!(a.toasts.iter().any(|t| t.text.contains("older")));
    }

    #[test]
    fn searching_a_full_history_stays_fast() {
        let big = "lorem ipsum dolor sit amet ".repeat(6_000); // ~160 KB each, like a full 8 MiB store
        let items: Vec<HistoryItem> = (0..50u8).map(|n| sample_item(n, &big, false)).collect();
        let mut a = app_with(items);
        a.history.filter.insert_str("zzz-not-there");
        let started = std::time::Instant::now();
        for _ in 0..5 {
            assert!(a.history.visible().is_empty());
        }
        assert!(
            started.elapsed() < std::time::Duration::from_secs(5),
            "5 searches over ~8 MB took {:?}",
            started.elapsed()
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn new_items_keep_the_selection_in_range() {
        let mut a = app_with(three());
        a.history.selected = 2;
        update(&mut a, Msg::Reply(Reply::History(Ok(vec![sample_item(1, "only", false)]))));
        assert_eq!(a.history.selected, 0);
        update(&mut a, Msg::Reply(Reply::History(Err(crate::api::ApiError::DaemonDown))));
        assert!(a.history.error.is_some());
    }
}
```
