- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `screens/history.rs`** (above the tests)

```rust
//! The History screen: what was synced recently. Browse, search, copy an item back to this
//! computer's clipboard, send it again, reveal a hidden secret, clear.
use crate::api::{ApiError, HistoryItem};
use crate::app::{AppState, ConfirmAction, Conn, Effect, Modal, Screen, ToastKind};
use crate::fmt;
use crate::sanitize::{clean, one_line, pad_to, truncate, width, wrap_rows};
use crate::textbuf::TextBuf;
use crate::theme::Theme;
use crossterm::event::{KeyCode, KeyEvent, KeyModifiers};
use ratatui::layout::{Constraint, Direction, Layout, Rect};
use ratatui::text::{Line, Span};
use ratatui::widgets::{Block, Paragraph};
use ratatui::Frame;
use std::collections::HashSet;

/// Longest stretch of one item shown in the detail pane.
const DETAIL_MAX_BYTES: usize = 256 * 1024;
const SAFETY_REFETCH_MS: u64 = 10_000;
const DEFAULT_LIMIT: usize = 50;

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct HistoryState {
    pub items: Vec<HistoryItem>,
    pub loaded: bool,
    /// Index into `visible()`.
    pub selected: usize,
    pub detail_scroll: usize,
    pub filter: TextBuf,
    pub filtering: bool,
    pub revealed: HashSet<[u8; 16]>,
    /// `total_synced_clips` at the last fetch: a change means there is something new.
    pub last_total: u64,
    pub error: Option<String>,
}

impl Default for TextBuf {
    fn default() -> Self {
        TextBuf::new(256)
    }
}

/// What an item looks like to a human, or `None` when it is not text.
fn item_text(item: &HistoryItem) -> Option<String> {
    if item.full_content.is_empty() {
        return None;
    }
    let bytes = &item.full_content[..item.full_content.len().min(DETAIL_MAX_BYTES)];
    match std::str::from_utf8(bytes) {
        Ok(s) => Some(s.to_string()),
        Err(e) if e.error_len().is_none() && bytes.len() == DETAIL_MAX_BYTES => {
            // Cut in the middle of a character by the size limit only.
            Some(String::from_utf8_lossy(&bytes[..e.valid_up_to()]).into_owned())
        }
        Err(_) => None,
    }
}

impl HistoryState {
    fn matches(&self, item: &HistoryItem, needle: &str) -> bool {
        if needle.is_empty() {
            return true;
        }
        let needle = needle.to_lowercase();
        if item.origin_name.to_lowercase().contains(&needle) {
            return true;
        }
        if item.is_sensitive && !self.revealed.contains(&item.id) {
            return false; // a hidden secret must not be discoverable by guessing its content
        }
        match item_text(item) {
            Some(t) => t.to_lowercase().contains(&needle),
            None => item.content_preview.to_lowercase().contains(&needle),
        }
    }

    /// Indices into `items` that pass the search filter, newest first (the daemon's order).
    pub fn visible(&self) -> Vec<usize> {
        let needle = self.filter.text();
        (0..self.items.len()).filter(|&i| self.matches(&self.items[i], &needle)).collect()
    }

    pub fn selected_item(&self) -> Option<&HistoryItem> {
        let vis = self.visible();
        vis.get(self.selected).map(|&i| &self.items[i])
    }

    fn clamp(&mut self) {
        let n = self.visible().len();
        self.selected = self.selected.min(n.saturating_sub(1));
    }
}

fn limit(app: &AppState) -> usize {
    match &app.hello {
        crate::app::HelloState::New(h) => h.config.max_history_items.max(1),
        _ => DEFAULT_LIMIT,
    }
}

fn fetch(app: &mut AppState, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    if app.inflight.history {
        return;
    }
    app.inflight.history = true;
    app.clock.history_ms = app.mono_ms;
    app.history.last_total = app.status.as_ref().map(|s| s.total_synced_clips).unwrap_or(0);
    fx.push(Effect::History { limit: limit(app) });
}

pub fn on_enter(app: &mut AppState, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    fetch(app, fx);
}

pub fn on_leave(app: &mut AppState) {
    app.history.revealed.clear();
    app.history.filtering = false;
}

pub fn on_tick(app: &mut AppState, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    if app.screen != Screen::History || app.inflight.history || !matches!(app.conn, Conn::Up) {
        return;
    }
    let total = app.status.as_ref().map(|s| s.total_synced_clips).unwrap_or(0);
    let stale = app.mono_ms.saturating_sub(app.clock.history_ms) >= SAFETY_REFETCH_MS;
    if !app.history.loaded || total != app.history.last_total || stale {
        fetch(app, fx);
    }
}

pub fn on_items(app: &mut AppState, res: Result<Vec<HistoryItem>, ApiError>) {
    app.inflight.history = false;
    match res {
        Ok(items) => {
            app.history.items = items;
            app.history.loaded = true;
            app.history.error = None;
            app.history.clamp();
        }
        Err(e) => app.history.error = Some(e.plain()),
    }
}

pub fn on_cleared(app: &mut AppState) {
    app.history.items.clear();
    app.history.selected = 0;
    app.history.revealed.clear();
}

pub fn text_focus(app: &AppState) -> bool {
    app.history.filtering
}

pub fn scroll(app: &mut AppState, delta: i32) {
    let n = app.history.visible().len();
    let next = (app.history.selected as i64 + i64::from(delta)).clamp(0, n.saturating_sub(1) as i64);
    app.history.selected = next as usize;
    app.history.detail_scroll = 0;
}

pub fn key(app: &mut AppState, k: KeyEvent, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    if app.history.filtering {
        return filter_key(app, k);
    }
    let ctrl = k.modifiers.contains(KeyModifiers::CONTROL);
    let n = app.history.visible().len();
    match k.code {
        KeyCode::Up | KeyCode::Char('k') => scroll(app, -1),
        KeyCode::Down | KeyCode::Char('j') => scroll(app, 1),
        KeyCode::Home | KeyCode::Char('g') => {
            app.history.selected = 0;
            app.history.detail_scroll = 0;
        }
        KeyCode::End | KeyCode::Char('G') => {
            app.history.selected = n.saturating_sub(1);
            app.history.detail_scroll = 0;
        }
        KeyCode::PageDown => app.history.detail_scroll += 10,
        KeyCode::PageUp => app.history.detail_scroll = app.history.detail_scroll.saturating_sub(10),
        KeyCode::Char('/') => app.history.filtering = true,
        KeyCode::Esc if !app.history.filter.is_empty() => {
            app.history.filter.clear();
            app.history.selected = 0;
        }
        KeyCode::Char('r') if !ctrl => fetch(app, fx),
        KeyCode::Char('v') => {
            if let Some(item) = app.history.selected_item() {
                let id = item.id;
                if !app.history.revealed.remove(&id) {
                    app.history.revealed.insert(id);
                }
            }
        }
        KeyCode::Enter => copy_back(app, fx),
        KeyCode::Char('s') => resend(app, fx),
        KeyCode::Char('C') => clear_all(app),
        _ => {}
    }
}

fn filter_key(app: &mut AppState, k: KeyEvent) {
    let ctrl = k.modifiers.contains(KeyModifiers::CONTROL);
    let f = &mut app.history.filter;
    match k.code {
        KeyCode::Esc => {
            f.clear();
            app.history.filtering = false;
        }
        KeyCode::Enter => app.history.filtering = false,
        KeyCode::Backspace => f.backspace(),
        KeyCode::Char('u') if ctrl => f.clear(),
        KeyCode::Char(c) if !ctrl => {
            f.insert_char(c);
        }
        _ => {}
    }
    app.history.selected = 0;
    app.history.detail_scroll = 0;
}

fn copy_back(app: &mut AppState, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    let Some(item) = app.history.selected_item() else { return };
    let (id, empty) = (item.id, item.full_content.is_empty());
    if !app.has_capability(klip_proto::ipc::CAP_COPY_TO_CLIPBOARD) {
        app.toast(
            ToastKind::Warn,
            "The running Klip is older: restart it after upgrading to copy items back",
        );
    } else if empty {
        app.toast(ToastKind::Warn, "That clip was too large to keep in the history");
    } else {
        fx.push(Effect::CopyItem(id));
    }
}

fn resend(app: &mut AppState, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    let Some(item) = app.history.selected_item() else { return };
    if item.full_content.is_empty() {
        app.toast(ToastKind::Warn, "That clip was too large to keep in the history");
        return;
    }
    match String::from_utf8(item.full_content.clone()) {
        Ok(text) => {
            fx.push(Effect::Send(text));
            app.toast(ToastKind::Info, "Sending again…");
        }
        Err(_) => app.toast(ToastKind::Warn, "Only text can be sent again from here"),
    }
}

fn clear_all(app: &mut AppState) {
    if !app.has_capability(klip_proto::ipc::CAP_CLEAR_HISTORY) {
        app.toast(
            ToastKind::Warn,
            "The running Klip is older: restart it after upgrading to clear the history",
        );
        return;
    }
    app.modal = Some(Modal::Confirm {
        title: "Clear the history?".to_string(),
        body: format!(
            "This forgets the {} clip(s) kept on this computer. Other devices are not affected.",
            app.history.items.len()
        ),
        action: ConfirmAction::ClearHistory,
    });
}

// -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- drawing

fn row_text(app: &AppState, item: &HistoryItem) -> String {
    if item.is_sensitive && !app.history.revealed.contains(&item.id) {
        return format!("•••• secret ({})", fmt::bytes(item.size_bytes));
    }
    match item_text(item) {
        Some(t) => one_line(&t.chars().take(200).collect::<String>()),
        None if item.full_content.is_empty() => format!("[too large to keep: {}]", fmt::bytes(item.size_bytes)),
        None => format!("[binary, {}]", fmt::bytes(item.size_bytes)),
    }
}

fn detail_rows(app: &AppState, item: &HistoryItem, cols: usize) -> Vec<String> {
    if item.is_sensitive && !app.history.revealed.contains(&item.id) {
        return vec![format!("•••• secret ({}): press v to show it", fmt::bytes(item.size_bytes))];
    }
    match item_text(item) {
        Some(t) => {
            let mut rows = wrap_rows(&clean(&t), cols);
            if item.full_content.len() > DETAIL_MAX_BYTES {
                rows.push(format!("… {} more bytes not shown", item.full_content.len() - DETAIL_MAX_BYTES));
            }
            rows
        }
        None if item.full_content.is_empty() => vec![format!(
            "[This clip is {}: too large to keep in the history. Copy it again on the device it came from.]",
            fmt::bytes(item.size_bytes)
        )],
        None => vec![format!("[binary content, {}]", fmt::bytes(item.size_bytes))],
    }
}

pub fn view(app: &AppState, theme: &Theme, f: &mut Frame, area: Rect) {
    let dir = if area.width >= 100 { Direction::Horizontal } else { Direction::Vertical };
    let [list_area, detail_area] =
        Layout::default().direction(dir).constraints([Constraint::Percentage(50), Constraint::Percentage(50)]).areas(area);

    let vis = app.history.visible();
    let title = if app.history.filter.is_empty() && !app.history.filtering {
        format!(" History ({}) ", app.history.items.len())
    } else {
        format!(" History · search “{}” ({}) ", one_line(&app.history.filter.text()), vis.len())
    };
    let block = Block::bordered().title(Span::styled(title, theme.st_title())).border_style(theme.st_faint());
    let inner = block.inner(list_area);
    f.render_widget(block, list_area);
    if let Some(err) = &app.history.error {
        crate::screens::centered_text(f, inner, theme.st_bad(), &truncate(&one_line(err), inner.width as usize));
    } else if vis.is_empty() {
        let msg = if !app.history.loaded {
            "Loading…"
        } else if app.history.items.is_empty() {
            "No clips yet. Copy something on a linked device."
        } else {
            "Nothing matches the search."
        };
        crate::screens::centered_text(f, inner, theme.st_dim(), msg);
    } else {
        let selected = app.history.selected.min(vis.len() - 1);
        let (start, end) = crate::screens::window(selected, vis.len(), inner.height as usize);
        let w = inner.width as usize;
        let lines: Vec<Line> = (start..end)
            .map(|pos| {
                let item = &app.history.items[vis[pos]];
                let head = format!(
                    "{} {} {} ",
                    pad_to(&fmt::ago_ms(app.wall_ms, item.timestamp_ms), 10),
                    pad_to(&one_line(&item.origin_name), 14),
                    pad_to(&fmt::bytes(item.size_bytes), 9)
                );
                let text = truncate(&row_text(app, item), w.saturating_sub(width(&head) + 2));
                let style = if pos == selected { theme.st_selected() } else { theme.st_text() };
                Line::from(vec![
                    Span::styled(if pos == selected { "▸ " } else { "  " }, style),
                    Span::styled(head, if pos == selected { style } else { theme.st_dim() }),
                    Span::styled(text, style),
                ])
            })
            .collect();
        f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(lines), inner);
    }

    let block = Block::bordered().title(Span::styled(" Clip ", theme.st_title())).border_style(theme.st_faint());
    let inner = block.inner(detail_area);
    f.render_widget(block, detail_area);
    if let Some(item) = app.history.selected_item() {
        let head = format!(
            "{} · {} · {}",
            one_line(&item.origin_name),
            fmt::ago_ms(app.wall_ms, item.timestamp_ms),
            fmt::bytes(item.size_bytes)
        );
        let [h, body] = Layout::vertical([Constraint::Length(2), Constraint::Min(0)]).areas(inner);
        f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(truncate(&head, h.width as usize)).style(theme.st_dim()), h);
        let rows = detail_rows(app, item, body.width as usize);
        let skip = app.history.detail_scroll.min(rows.len().saturating_sub(1));
        let lines: Vec<Line> = rows
            .into_iter()
            .skip(skip)
            .take(body.height as usize)
            .map(|r| Line::from(Span::styled(r, theme.st_text())))
            .collect();
        f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(lines), body);
    }
    if app.history.filtering {
        let x = list_area.x + 2 + (" History · search “".chars().count() + width(&one_line(&app.history.filter.text()))) as u16;
        if x < list_area.right() && list_area.height > 0 {
            f.set_cursor_position((x.min(list_area.right().saturating_sub(2)), list_area.y));
        }
    }
}
```
`TextBuf` needs `Default` for `HistoryState: Default` — the `impl Default for TextBuf` above lives in `history.rs` for convenience; **move it to `textbuf.rs`** (`impl Default for TextBuf { fn default() -> Self { TextBuf::new(256) } }`) to respect module ownership.

- [ ] **Step 4: Route History** — `screens/mod.rs`:

```rust
        Screen::History => history::key(app, k, fx),
```
in `key`; in `text_focus`: `Screen::History => history::text_focus(app),`; in `scroll`: `Screen::History => history::scroll(app, delta)` (turn the `if` into a `match`); in `on_enter`: `Screen::History => history::on_enter(app, fx)`; in `on_leave`: `Screen::History => history::on_leave(app)`; in `on_tick`: call `history::on_tick(app, fx)` after `pair::on_tick`; in `view`: `Screen::History => history::view(app, theme, f, area),`; `hints`: `Screen::History => "↑↓ select · / search · Enter copy back · s send again · v reveal · C clear · "`; `help_lines` for History: `("↑↓ / j k", "select a clip")`, `("PgUp PgDn", "scroll the clip")`, `("/", "search (Esc clears)")`, `("Enter", "copy the clip back to this computer")`, `("s", "send it to your devices again")`, `("v", "show or hide a secret")`, `("C", "clear the history")`, `("r", "reload")`. Also in `app.rs::go` nothing else is needed.

```rust
#[tokio::test]
async fn history_shows_clips_copies_back_and_clears() {
    let fake = FakeDaemon::new();
    fake.with(|s| {
        s.history = vec![sample_item(1, "https://example.com/Docs", false), sample_item(2, "api_key_SECRET0123456789", true)];
    });
    let mut h = harness(&fake, "hist");
    assert!(h.wait_for("ACTIVE", 3000).await);
    h.text("4");
    assert!(h.wait_for("https://example.com/Docs", 3000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    assert!(h.screen().contains("•••• secret"), "{}", h.screen());
    assert!(!h.screen().contains("SECRET0123"), "a hidden secret must not appear on screen");
    h.key(KeyCode::Enter);
    h.pump(200).await;
    assert!(fake.calls().iter().any(|c| c == "copy"));
    assert!(h.wait_for("Copied to this computer", 2000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    h.text("j");
    h.text("v");
    assert!(h.wait_for("SECRET0123", 1000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    h.text("C");
    assert!(h.screen().contains("Clear the history?"));
    h.text("y");
    assert!(h.wait_for("History cleared", 3000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    assert!(h.wait_for("No clips yet", 3000).await, "{}", h.screen());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn on_an_old_daemon_copy_and_clear_explain_themselves() {
    let fake = FakeDaemon::old_daemon();
    fake.with(|s| s.history = vec![sample_item(1, "hello", false)]);
    let mut h = harness(&fake, "histold");
    assert!(h.wait_for("ACTIVE", 3000).await);
    h.text("4");
    assert!(h.wait_for("hello", 3000).await);
    h.key(KeyCode::Enter);
    assert!(h.wait_for("is older", 1000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    assert!(!fake.calls().iter().any(|c| c == "copy"));
    h.text("s");
    assert!(h.wait_for("Sent", 3000).await || h.wait_for("Sending", 100).await, "{}", h.screen());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_huge_hostile_item_renders_quickly_and_safely() {
    let fake = FakeDaemon::new();
    let hostile = format!("\x1b]0;PWNED\x07\x1b[2J\x1b[?1049l{}", "A".repeat(250_000));
    fake.with(|s| s.history = vec![sample_item(1, &hostile, false)]);
    let mut h = harness(&fake, "histbig");
    assert!(h.wait_for("ACTIVE", 3000).await);
    h.text("4");
    let started = std::time::Instant::now();
    assert!(h.wait_for("PWNED", 5000).await, "the text is shown, not interpreted: {}", h.screen());
    let screen = h.screen();
    assert!(!screen.contains('\x1b') && !screen.contains('\x07'));
    assert!(screen.contains('␛'));
    assert!(started.elapsed() < std::time::Duration::from_secs(5), "drawing a 250 KB item took {:?}", started.elapsed());
}
```

```rust
fn history_screen() {
    use klip_tui::testkit::sample_item;
    let items = vec![
        sample_item(3, "fn main() {\n    println!(\"日本語 hello\");\n}\n", false),
        sample_item(2, "api_key_secret0123456789ABCDEF", true),
        sample_item(1, "evil \x1b[2J\x1b]0;x\x07 \u{202E}text", false),
    ];

    a.history.loaded = true;
    snap("history", &a, 100, 30);
    snap("history", &a, 60, 16);
    a.history.selected = 1;
    snap("history_secret_hidden", &a, 100, 30);
    a.history.revealed.insert([2; 16]);
    snap("history_secret_revealed", &a, 100, 30);
    a.history.revealed.clear();
    a.history.selected = 0;
    a.history.filter.insert_str("hello");
    a.history.filtering = true;

    a.history.filter.clear();

    a.history.items = vec![big];

    a.history.items.clear();
    snap("history_empty", &a, 100, 30);
}
```

```rust
#[test]
fn hostile_clip_text_cannot_leave_the_alternate_screen_or_change_the_terminal() {
    let d = Isolated::start("hostile", LAN_ONLY);
    // A local send is recorded in the history, so no second device is needed.
    let hostile = "\x1b]0;PWNED\x07\x1b[2J\x1b[?1049l\x1b[31mred\u{202E}evil";
    assert!(d.run(&["send", hostile], None).status.success());
    let mut p = Pty::spawn(&[], &d.env_pairs(), (100, 30));
    assert!(p.wait_for("ACTIVE", 15));
    p.send(b"4");
    assert!(p.wait_for("Clip", 10), "{}", p.screen());
    if p.wait_for("•••• secret", 2) {
        p.send(b"v"); // the secret detector may flag odd text: reveal it, the safety question is the same
    }
    assert!(p.wait_for("PWNED", 10), "the text must be visible as text: {}", p.screen());
    assert!(p.alt_screen(), "an escape sequence in a clip must not take the terminal out of the alternate screen");
    assert!(p.screen().contains("␛"), "{}", p.screen());
    p.send(b"q");
    assert_eq!(p.wait_exit(5), Some(0));
}
```

```rust
        typed(&mut a, "abc");
        let fx = update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Esc));
        assert!(fx.is_empty() && a.send.prompt.is_none());
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `screens/send.rs`** (above the tests)

```rust
//! The Send screen: a multi-line editor whose text goes to every linked device, with a file loader
//! for big payloads and a truthful per-device result.
use crate::api::{ApiError, SendReport};
use crate::app::{AppState, Conn, Effect, ToastKind};
use crate::fmt;
use crate::sanitize::{clean, one_line, truncate, width, wrap_rows};
use crate::textbuf::TextBuf;
use crate::theme::Theme;
use crossterm::event::{KeyCode, KeyEvent, KeyModifiers};
use ratatui::layout::{Constraint, Layout, Rect};
use ratatui::text::{Line, Span};
use ratatui::widgets::{Block, Paragraph};
use ratatui::Frame;
use unicode_segmentation::UnicodeSegmentation;

/// The editor never holds more than this; bigger files are sent as they are ("large payload").
pub const EDITOR_MAX_BYTES: usize = 1024 * 1024;
/// How much of a large payload is shown as a read-only preview.
const PREVIEW_BYTES: usize = 64 * 1024;
const SPINNER: [&str; 10] = ["⠋", "⠙", "⠹", "⠸", "⠼", "⠴", "⠦", "⠧", "⠇", "⠏"];

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum SendOutcome {
    Done(SendReport),
    Failed(String),
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct LargePayload {
    pub path: String,
    pub text: String,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct SendState {
    pub buf: TextBuf,
    pub focus: bool,
    pub sending: bool,
    pub result: Option<SendOutcome>,
    /// The "open a file" path prompt, when open.
    pub prompt: Option<TextBuf>,
    pub large: Option<LargePayload>,
    /// First visible row and first visible column (in grapheme clusters) of the editor.
    pub top: usize,
    pub left: usize,
}

impl Default for SendState {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            buf: TextBuf::new(EDITOR_MAX_BYTES),
            focus: false,
            sending: false,
            result: None,
            prompt: None,
            large: None,
            top: 0,
            left: 0,
        }
    }
}

/// Rows and columns available inside the editor box, from the terminal size.
pub fn editor_height(app: &AppState) -> usize {
    (app.size.1 as usize).saturating_sub(2 + 5 + 2).max(1)
}
fn editor_width(app: &AppState) -> usize {
    (app.size.0 as usize).saturating_sub(2).max(1)
}

fn ensure_visible(app: &mut AppState) {
    let (h, w) = (editor_height(app), editor_width(app));
    let (row, col) = app.send.buf.cursor();
    if row < app.send.top {
        app.send.top = row;
    } else if row >= app.send.top + h {
        app.send.top = row + 1 - h;
    }
    if col < app.send.left {
        app.send.left = col;
    } else if col >= app.send.left + w.saturating_sub(1) {
        app.send.left = col + 2 - w.max(2);
    }
}

pub fn text_focus(app: &AppState) -> bool {
    app.send.prompt.is_some() || (app.send.focus && app.send.large.is_none())
}

pub fn on_enter(app: &mut AppState) {
    app.send.focus = app.send.large.is_none();
}

pub fn on_leave(app: &mut AppState) {
    app.send.focus = false;
    app.send.prompt = None;
}

pub fn on_daemon_down(app: &mut AppState) {
    app.send.sending = false;
}

/// Called with every send result (also those started from the History screen).
pub fn on_sent(app: &mut AppState, res: &Result<SendReport, ApiError>) {
    app.send.sending = false;
    app.send.result = Some(match res {
        Ok(r) => SendOutcome::Done(r.clone()),
        Err(e) => SendOutcome::Failed(e.plain()),
    });
}

pub fn on_file_loaded(app: &mut AppState, path: String, result: Result<String, String>) {
    match result {
        Err(e) => app.toast(ToastKind::Error, one_line(&e)),
        Ok(text) if text.len() <= EDITOR_MAX_BYTES => {
            app.send.buf = TextBuf::from_text(&text, EDITOR_MAX_BYTES);
            app.send.large = None;
            app.send.focus = true;
            app.send.top = 0;
            app.send.left = 0;
            app.toast(ToastKind::Ok, format!("Loaded {} ({})", one_line(&path), fmt::bytes(text.len())));
        }
        Ok(text) => {
            let size = text.len();
            app.send.buf.clear();
            app.send.large = Some(LargePayload { path: path.clone(), text });
            app.send.focus = false;
            app.toast(
                ToastKind::Info,
                format!("{} ({}) is sent as it is; the editor shows only its start", one_line(&path), fmt::bytes(size)),
            );
        }
    }
}

fn send(app: &mut AppState, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    if app.send.sending {
        return;
    }
    let text = match &app.send.large {
        Some(l) => l.text.clone(),
        None => app.send.buf.text(),
    };
    if text.trim().is_empty() {
        app.toast(ToastKind::Warn, "Nothing to send: type or paste some text first");
        return;
    }
    let bytes = text.len();
    let (limit, cap) = (app.clip_limit(), app.request_cap());
    if bytes > limit {
        app.send.result = Some(SendOutcome::Failed(format!(
            "{} is over the limit of {} per clip",
            fmt::bytes(bytes),
            fmt::bytes(limit)
        )));
        return;
    }
    if bytes + 64 > cap {
        app.send.result = Some(SendOutcome::Failed(ApiError::TooLarge { size: bytes, max: cap }.plain()));
        return;
    }
    if !matches!(app.conn, Conn::Up) {
        app.toast(ToastKind::Warn, "Klip is not running: start it first (Home, s)");
        return;
    }
    app.send.sending = true;
    app.send.result = None;
    fx.push(Effect::Send(text));
}

pub fn paste(app: &mut AppState, text: &str) {
    if let Some(p) = &mut app.send.prompt {
        let one: String = text.chars().filter(|c| *c != '\n' && *c != '\r').collect();
        p.insert_str(&one);
    } else if app.send.focus && app.send.large.is_none() {
        let inserted = app.send.buf.insert_str(text);
        if inserted < text.replace("\r\n", "\n").replace('\r', "\n").len() {
            app.toast(ToastKind::Warn, "The pasted text was cut at 1 MiB: use Ctrl+O to send a bigger file");
        }
        ensure_visible(app);
    }
}

pub fn key(app: &mut AppState, k: KeyEvent, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    if app.send.prompt.is_some() {
        return prompt_key(app, k, fx);
    }
    let ctrl = k.modifiers.contains(KeyModifiers::CONTROL);
    if (ctrl && k.code == KeyCode::Char('s')) || k.code == KeyCode::F(5) {
        return send(app, fx);
    }
    if ctrl && k.code == KeyCode::Char('o') {
        app.send.prompt = Some(TextBuf::new(4096));
        return;
    }
    if ctrl && k.code == KeyCode::Char('u') {
        app.send.buf.clear();
        app.send.large = None;
        app.send.result = None;
        app.send.top = 0;
        app.send.left = 0;
        return;
    }
    if !(app.send.focus && app.send.large.is_none()) {
        // Not editing: Enter or i starts editing, o opens a file, x clears.
        match k.code {
            KeyCode::Enter | KeyCode::Char('i') if app.send.large.is_none() => app.send.focus = true,
            KeyCode::Char('o') => app.send.prompt = Some(TextBuf::new(4096)),
            KeyCode::Char('x') => {
                app.send.buf.clear();
                app.send.large = None;
                app.send.result = None;
            }
            _ => {}
        }
        return;
    }
    let b = &mut app.send.buf;
    match k.code {
        KeyCode::Esc => app.send.focus = false,
        KeyCode::Enter => b.newline(),
        KeyCode::Tab => {
            b.insert_char('\t');
        }
        KeyCode::Backspace => b.backspace(),
        KeyCode::Delete => b.delete(),
        KeyCode::Left => b.left(),
        KeyCode::Right => b.right(),
        KeyCode::Up => b.up(),
        KeyCode::Down => b.down(),
        KeyCode::Home => b.home(),
        KeyCode::End => b.end(),
        KeyCode::Char('a') if ctrl => b.home(),
        KeyCode::Char('e') if ctrl => b.end(),
        KeyCode::Char('w') if ctrl => b.delete_word_back(),
        KeyCode::Char(c) if !ctrl => {
            b.insert_char(c);
        }
        _ => {}
    }
    ensure_visible(app);
}

fn prompt_key(app: &mut AppState, k: KeyEvent, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    let ctrl = k.modifiers.contains(KeyModifiers::CONTROL);
    let Some(p) = app.send.prompt.as_mut() else { return };
    match k.code {
        KeyCode::Esc => app.send.prompt = None,
        KeyCode::Enter => {
            let path = p.text().trim().to_string();
            app.send.prompt = None;
            if path.is_empty() {
                app.toast(ToastKind::Warn, "Type the path of a text file");
            } else {
                fx.push(Effect::LoadFile(path));
            }
        }
        KeyCode::Backspace => p.backspace(),
        KeyCode::Delete => p.delete(),
        KeyCode::Left => p.left(),
        KeyCode::Right => p.right(),
        KeyCode::Home => p.home(),
        KeyCode::End => p.end(),
        KeyCode::Char('u') if ctrl => p.clear(),
        KeyCode::Char(c) if !ctrl => {
            p.insert_char(c);
        }
        _ => {}
    }
}

// -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- drawing

pub fn view(app: &AppState, theme: &Theme, f: &mut Frame, area: Rect) {
    let [editor, result] = Layout::vertical([Constraint::Min(5), Constraint::Length(5)]).areas(area);
    draw_editor(app, theme, f, editor);
    draw_result(app, theme, f, result);
    if let Some(p) = &app.send.prompt {
        draw_prompt(p, theme, f, editor);
    }
}

fn counter(app: &AppState, theme: &Theme) -> (String, ratatui::style::Style) {
    let bytes = match &app.send.large {
        Some(l) => l.text.len(),
        None => app.send.buf.byte_len(),
    };
    let limit = app.clip_limit();
    let style = if bytes > limit {
        theme.st_bad()
    } else if bytes * 10 > limit * 9 {
        theme.st_warn()
    } else {
        theme.st_dim()
    };
    (format!(" {} / {} ", fmt::bytes(bytes), fmt::bytes(limit)), style)
}

fn draw_editor(app: &AppState, theme: &Theme, f: &mut Frame, area: Rect) {
    let (count, count_style) = counter(app, theme);
    let title = match &app.send.large {
        Some(_) => " Send a large file ",
        None => " Send text to all devices ",
    };
    let block = Block::bordered()
        .title(Span::styled(title, theme.st_title()))
        .title_bottom(Line::from(Span::styled(count, count_style)).right_aligned())
        .border_style(if text_focus(app) { theme.st_signal() } else { theme.st_faint() });
    let inner = block.inner(area);
    f.render_widget(block, area);
    if inner.width == 0 || inner.height == 0 {
        return;
    }
    if let Some(l) = &app.send.large {
        let head = format!("{} · {} · sent as it is, not editable here · Ctrl+U discards it", one_line(&l.path), fmt::bytes(l.text.len()));
        let cut = l.text.char_indices().nth(PREVIEW_BYTES).map(|(i, _)| i).unwrap_or(l.text.len());
        let mut lines = vec![Line::from(Span::styled(truncate(&head, inner.width as usize), theme.st_warn()))];
        lines.extend(
            wrap_rows(&clean(&l.text[..cut]), inner.width as usize)
                .into_iter()
                .take((inner.height as usize).saturating_sub(1))
                .map(|r| Line::from(Span::styled(r, theme.st_dim()))),
        );
        f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(lines), inner);
        return;
    }
    let b = &app.send.buf;
    if b.is_empty() && !app.send.focus {
        crate::screens::centered_text(f, inner, theme.st_dim(), "Press Enter to type, or Ctrl+O to load a file");
        return;
    }
    let (h, w) = (inner.height as usize, inner.width as usize);
    let lines: Vec<Line> = b
        .lines()
        .iter()
        .skip(app.send.top)
        .take(h)
        .map(|l| {
            let visible: String = l.graphemes(true).skip(app.send.left).collect();
            Line::from(Span::styled(truncate(&clean(&visible), w), theme.st_text()))
        })
        .collect();
    f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(lines), inner);
    if text_focus(app) && app.send.prompt.is_none() {
        let (row, col) = b.cursor();
        let prefix: String = b.lines()[row].graphemes(true).skip(app.send.left).take(col.saturating_sub(app.send.left)).collect();
        let x = (width(&clean(&prefix))).min(w.saturating_sub(1));
        let y = row.saturating_sub(app.send.top).min(h.saturating_sub(1));
        f.set_cursor_position((inner.x + x as u16, inner.y + y as u16));
    }
}

fn draw_result(app: &AppState, theme: &Theme, f: &mut Frame, area: Rect) {
    let block = Block::bordered().title(Span::styled(" Result ", theme.st_title())).border_style(theme.st_faint());
    let inner = block.inner(area);
    f.render_widget(block, area);
    let mut lines: Vec<Line> = Vec::new();
    if app.send.sending {
        let frame = SPINNER[(app.mono_ms / 120) as usize % SPINNER.len()];
        lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(format!("{frame} Sending… (Esc only stops the screen waiting)"), theme.st_warn())));
    } else {
        match &app.send.result {
            None => lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
                "Ctrl+S or F5 sends · Ctrl+O loads a file · Ctrl+U clears · Esc leaves the editor",
                theme.st_dim(),
            ))),
            Some(SendOutcome::Failed(m)) => lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(format!("✗ {}", one_line(m)), theme.st_bad()))),
            Some(SendOutcome::Done(r)) if r.delivered.is_empty() && r.failed.is_empty() => lines.push(Line::from(
                Span::styled("No linked devices: nothing was sent. Pair one first (3).", theme.st_warn()),
            )),
            Some(SendOutcome::Done(r)) => {
                lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(format!("Sent {}", fmt::bytes(r.bytes)), theme.st_text())));
                for n in &r.delivered {
                    lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(format!("✓ {}", one_line(n)), theme.st_signal())));
                }
                for (n, why) in &r.failed {
                    lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
                        format!("✗ {}: {}", one_line(n), one_line(why)),
                        theme.st_bad(),
                    )));
                }
            }
        }
    }
    let h = inner.height as usize;
    if lines.len() > h && h > 0 {
        lines.truncate(h - 1);
        lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled("…", theme.st_dim())));
    }
    f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(lines), inner);
}

fn draw_prompt(p: &TextBuf, theme: &Theme, f: &mut Frame, editor: Rect) {
    let r = Rect { x: editor.x + 2, y: editor.y + editor.height.saturating_sub(4), width: editor.width.saturating_sub(4), height: 3 };
    if r.width < 10 || r.y < editor.y {
        return;
    }
    f.render_widget(ratatui::widgets::Clear, r);
    let block = Block::bordered().title(Span::styled(" Open a text file (Enter loads, Esc cancels) ", theme.st_title())).border_style(theme.st_signal());
    let inner = block.inner(r);
    f.render_widget(block, r);
    let shown = crate::sanitize::tail(&clean(&p.text()), (inner.width as usize).saturating_sub(1));
    f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(shown.clone()).style(theme.st_text()), inner);
    f.set_cursor_position((inner.x + width(&shown) as u16, inner.y));
}
```

```rust
#[tokio::test]
async fn typing_and_sending_text_reaches_the_daemon_and_shows_who_got_it() {
    use crossterm::event::KeyModifiers;
    let fake = FakeDaemon::new();
    let mut h = harness(&fake, "send");
    assert!(h.wait_for("ACTIVE", 3000).await);
    h.text("5");
    h.text("héllo 日本\n");
    h.paste("second line\r\nthird");
    assert!(h.screen().contains("third"));
    h.key_mod(KeyCode::Char('s'), KeyModifiers::CONTROL);
    assert!(h.wait_for("✓ Vivo V2253", 3000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    let sent = fake.calls().into_iter().find(|c| c.starts_with("send ")).expect("a send call");
    assert_eq!(sent, format!("send {}", "héllo 日本\nsecond line\nthird".len()));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_failed_delivery_is_shown_per_device() {
    use crossterm::event::KeyModifiers;
    let fake = FakeDaemon::new();
    fake.with(|s| {
        s.send_reply = Ok(klip_tui::api::SendReport {
            bytes: 0,
            delivered: vec!["Vivo V2253".into()],
            failed: vec![("Pixel 8".into(), "the other device could not write its clipboard".into())],
        });
    });
    let mut h = harness(&fake, "sendfail");
    assert!(h.wait_for("ACTIVE", 3000).await);
    h.text("5");
    h.text("x");
    h.key_mod(KeyCode::Char('s'), KeyModifiers::CONTROL);
    assert!(h.wait_for("✗ Pixel 8: the other device could not write its clipboard", 3000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    assert!(h.screen().contains("✓ Vivo V2253"));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn sending_with_no_linked_devices_says_so() {
    use crossterm::event::KeyModifiers;
    let fake = FakeDaemon::new();
    fake.with(|s| s.send_reply = Ok(klip_tui::api::SendReport { bytes: 1, delivered: vec![], failed: vec![] }));
    let mut h = harness(&fake, "sendnone");
    assert!(h.wait_for("ACTIVE", 3000).await);
    h.text("5");
    h.text("x");
    h.key_mod(KeyCode::Char('s'), KeyModifiers::CONTROL);
    assert!(h.wait_for("No linked devices: nothing was sent", 3000).await, "{}", h.screen());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn loading_a_real_file_from_disk_works_and_a_missing_one_is_reported() {
    use crossterm::event::KeyModifiers;
    let fake = FakeDaemon::new();
    let mut h = harness(&fake, "sendfile");
    assert!(h.wait_for("ACTIVE", 3000).await);
    let path = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("klip-tui-send-{}.txt", std::process::id()));
    std::fs::write(&path, "from a file\nsecond\n").unwrap();
    h.text("5");
    h.key_mod(KeyCode::Char('o'), KeyModifiers::CONTROL);
    h.text(path.to_str().unwrap());
    h.key(KeyCode::Enter);
    assert!(h.wait_for("from a file", 3000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    h.key_mod(KeyCode::Char('o'), KeyModifiers::CONTROL);
    h.text("/definitely/not/here.txt");
    h.key(KeyCode::Enter);
    assert!(h.wait_for("No such file", 3000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    let _ = std::fs::remove_file(path);
}
```

```rust
#[test]
fn send_screen_states() {
    use klip_tui::api::SendReport;
    use klip_tui::screens::send::{LargePayload, SendOutcome};
    let mut a = up(100, 30, two_peers());
    a.screen = Screen::Send;
    snap("send_empty", &a, 100, 30);
    a.send.focus = true;
    a.send.buf.insert_str("fn main() {\n\tprintln!(\"héllo 日本語 \u{1F980}\");\n}\nevil \x1b[2J\u{202E}text");
    snap("send_text", &a, 100, 30);
    snap("send_text", &a, 60, 16);
    a.send.result = Some(SendOutcome::Done(SendReport {
        bytes: 70,
        delivered: vec!["Vivo V2253".into()],
        failed: vec![("Pixel 8".into(), "the other device could not write its clipboard".into())],
    }));
    snap("send_result", &a, 100, 30);
    a.send.result = Some(SendOutcome::Failed("30.0 MiB is over the limit of 10 B per clip".into()));
    snap("send_failed", &a, 100, 30);
    a.send.sending = true;
    snap("send_sending", &a, 100, 30);
    a.send.sending = false;
    a.send.prompt = Some(klip_tui::textbuf::TextBuf::from_text("~/notes/todo.txt", 4096));
    snap("send_prompt", &a, 100, 30);
    a.send.prompt = None;
    a.send.buf.clear();
    a.send.focus = false;
    a.send.large = Some(LargePayload { path: "/tmp/big.log".into(), text: "log line\n".repeat(200_000) });
    snap("send_large", &a, 100, 30);
}
```

```rust
fn send_screen_types_and_sends_against_a_real_daemon() {
    let (_d, mut p) = running();
    assert!(p.wait_for("ACTIVE", 15));
    p.send(b"5");
    assert!(p.wait_for("Send text to all devices", 5), "{}", p.screen());
    p.send("héllo 日本\nsecond".as_bytes());
    p.send(b"\x13"); // Ctrl+S

    assert!(p.alt_screen());
    p.send(b"\x1b"); // Esc leaves the editor

    p.send(b"q");
    assert_eq!(p.wait_exit(5), Some(0), "{}", p.screen());
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the end-to-end tests** — `crates/klip-cli/tests/tui_daemon.rs`:

```rust
//! The TUI's own loop (Harness) driving real daemons: real iroh endpoints, real keystores, real
//! control sockets, in-memory clipboards, LAN only with discovery off (nothing leaves the machine).
#![cfg(unix)]
use crossterm::event::{KeyCode, KeyModifiers};
use klip_cli::api::IpcDaemonApi;
use klip_cli::client::DaemonClient;
use klip_crypto::identity::KeyStore;
use klip_daemon::{DaemonConfig, DaemonEngine, KlipPaths, RelayMode};
use klip_platform::memory::MemoryClipboardDriver;
use klip_proto::ipc::{deserialize_ipc_msg, serialize_ipc_msg, IpcRequest, IpcResponse};
use klip_tui::testkit::Harness;
use klip_tui::theme::TuiEnv;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
use tokio::io::{AsyncReadExt, AsyncWriteExt};
use tokio::net::UnixStream;
use tokio::task::JoinHandle;

struct TestDaemon {
    engine: Arc<DaemonEngine>,
    clipboard: Arc<MemoryClipboardDriver>,
    task: JoinHandle<()>,
    dir: PathBuf,
}

impl TestDaemon {
    fn sock(&self) -> PathBuf {
        self.dir.join("daemon.sock")
    }
    async fn stop(self) {
        let _ = ipc(&self.sock(), IpcRequest::StopDaemon).await;
        let _ = tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(10), self.task).await;
    }
}

fn fresh(tag: &str) -> PathBuf {
    let d = std::env::temp_dir().join(format!("klip-tuie2e-{}-{tag}", std::process::id()));
    let _ = std::fs::remove_dir_all(&d);
    d
}

async fn start_daemon(name: &str, dir: PathBuf) -> TestDaemon {
    let paths = KlipPaths::from_dir(&dir);
    let (_ks, identity) = KeyStore::load_or_create(paths.keystore(), name).unwrap();
    let clipboard = Arc::new(MemoryClipboardDriver::new(identity.node_id));
    let config = DaemonConfig {
        device_name: name.to_string(),
        relay: RelayMode::Lan,
        poll_interval_ms: 20,
        lan_discovery: false,
        ..DaemonConfig::default()
    };
    let engine = Arc::new(DaemonEngine::init_with(config, paths, Some(clipboard.clone())).await.expect("daemon init"));
    let runner = Arc::clone(&engine);
    let task = tokio::spawn(async move {
        let _ = runner.run().await;
    });
    let sock = dir.join("daemon.sock");
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(10);
    while !sock.exists() && Instant::now() < deadline {
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20)).await;
    }
    assert!(sock.exists(), "control socket did not appear");
    TestDaemon { engine, clipboard, task, dir }
}

async fn ipc(sock: &Path, req: IpcRequest) -> IpcResponse {
    let mut s = UnixStream::connect(sock).await.expect("connect ipc");
    let body = serialize_ipc_msg(&req).unwrap();
    s.write_all(&(body.len() as u32).to_be_bytes()).await.unwrap();
    s.write_all(&body).await.unwrap();
    let mut len = [0u8; 4];
    s.read_exact(&mut len).await.unwrap();
    let mut buf = vec![0u8; u32::from_be_bytes(len) as usize];
    s.read_exact(&mut buf).await.unwrap();
    deserialize_ipc_msg(&buf).unwrap()
}

async fn wait_until<F: FnMut() -> bool>(secs: u64, mut cond: F) -> bool {
    let end = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(secs);
    while Instant::now() < end {
        if cond() {
            return true;
        }
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(25)).await;
    }
    cond()
}

/// A TUI for `d`, wired exactly like the real binary does it.
fn tui_for(d: &TestDaemon) -> Harness {
    let paths = KlipPaths::from_dir(&d.dir);
    let api = IpcDaemonApi::new(DaemonClient::new(d.sock()), paths.clone());
    let env = TuiEnv::from_vars(paths, |k| (k == "NO_COLOR").then(|| "1".to_string()));
    Harness::new(Arc::new(api), env, 110, 40)
}

fn ctrl(h: &mut Harness, c: char) {
    h.key_mod(KeyCode::Char(c), KeyModifiers::CONTROL);
}
fn alt(h: &mut Harness, c: char) {
    h.key_mod(KeyCode::Char(c), KeyModifiers::ALT);
}

#[tokio::test(flavor = "multi_thread", worker_threads = 4)]
async fn two_real_daemons_pair_sync_copy_back_and_unpair_through_the_tui() {
    let a = start_daemon("Alpha", fresh("a")).await;
    let b = start_daemon("Beta", fresh("b")).await;
    let mut h = tui_for(&a);
    assert!(h.wait_for("ACTIVE", 10_000).await, "{}", h.screen());

    // Show a code with LAN addresses (these daemons have discovery off) and let Beta use it.
    h.text("3");
    assert!(h.wait_for("Ticket (single use):", 10_000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    h.text("l");
    assert!(h.wait_for("LAN addresses included", 10_000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    let ticket = h.rt.app.pair.ticket.as_ref().expect("a ticket").ticket.clone();
    let beta_sock = b.sock();
    let join = tokio::spawn(async move { ipc(&beta_sock, IpcRequest::PairJoin { ticket }).await });

    // Beta's request appears as a modal with its fingerprint; nothing happens until we answer.
    assert!(h.wait_for("asking to pair", 15_000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    assert!(h.screen().contains(&b.engine.node_id().fingerprint()), "{}", h.screen());
    h.text("y");
    match tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(20), join).await.expect("join in time").unwrap() {
        IpcResponse::PairSuccess { peer_name, .. } => assert_eq!(peer_name, "Alpha"),
        other => panic!("Beta could not join: {other:?}"),
    }
    assert!(h.wait_for("Linked with Beta", 15_000).await, "{}", h.screen());

    // Devices screen lists Beta.
    h.text("2");
    assert!(h.wait_for("Devices (1)", 10_000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    assert!(h.screen().contains("Beta"));

    // Send text typed in the TUI: it must land in Beta's clipboard, byte for byte.
    h.text("5");
    h.text("hello from the tui\n\tsecond line 日本語");
    ctrl(&mut h, 's');
    assert!(h.wait_for("✓ Beta", 20_000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    assert!(
        wait_until(10, || b.clipboard.current_text().as_deref() == Some("hello from the tui\n\tsecond line 日本語")).await,
        "Beta's clipboard: {:?}",
        b.clipboard.current_text()
    );

    // History shows it; Enter puts it back on Alpha's own clipboard.
    alt(&mut h, '4');
    assert!(h.wait_for("hello from the tui", 10_000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    h.key(KeyCode::Enter);
    assert!(h.wait_for("Copied to this computer", 10_000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    assert_eq!(a.clipboard.current_text().as_deref(), Some("hello from the tui\n\tsecond line 日本語"));

    // Pause from the menu, then unpair everything.
    h.key(KeyCode::Char('p'));
    h.key(KeyCode::Enter);
    assert!(h.wait_for("PAUSED", 10_000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    h.key(KeyCode::Char('p'));
    h.key(KeyCode::Enter); // "Resume now"
    assert!(h.wait_for("ACTIVE", 10_000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    h.text("2");
    h.text("U");
    assert!(h.screen().contains("Unpair ALL"), "{}", h.screen());
    h.text("y");
    assert!(h.wait_for("No devices yet", 10_000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    match ipc(&a.sock(), IpcRequest::GetStatus).await {
        IpcResponse::Status(s) => assert!(s.peers.is_empty()),
        other => panic!("{other:?}"),
    }
    a.stop().await;
    b.stop().await;
}

#[tokio::test(flavor = "multi_thread", worker_threads = 4)]
async fn the_tui_survives_the_daemon_stopping_and_coming_back() {
    let dir = fresh("restart");
    let a = start_daemon("Alpha", dir.clone()).await;
    let mut h = tui_for(&a);
    assert!(h.wait_for("ACTIVE", 10_000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    a.stop().await;
    assert!(h.wait_for("Klip is not running", 8_000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    assert!(h.screen().contains("STOPPED"));
    let a2 = start_daemon("Alpha", dir).await;
    assert!(h.wait_for("ACTIVE", 15_000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    assert!(h.rt.app.has_capability("copy_to_clipboard"), "Hello must be asked again after reconnecting");
    a2.stop().await;
}

#[tokio::test(flavor = "multi_thread", worker_threads = 4)]
async fn a_pairing_request_waiting_for_an_answer_is_replaced_by_a_notice_when_the_daemon_dies() {
    let a = start_daemon("Alpha", fresh("die-a")).await;
    let b = start_daemon("Beta", fresh("die-b")).await;
    let mut h = tui_for(&a);
    assert!(h.wait_for("ACTIVE", 10_000).await);
    h.text("3");
    assert!(h.wait_for("Ticket (single use):", 10_000).await);
    h.text("l");
    assert!(h.wait_for("LAN addresses included", 10_000).await);
    let ticket = h.rt.app.pair.ticket.as_ref().unwrap().ticket.clone();
    let beta_sock = b.sock();
    let _join = tokio::spawn(async move { ipc(&beta_sock, IpcRequest::PairJoin { ticket }).await });
    assert!(h.wait_for("asking to pair", 15_000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    a.stop().await;
    assert!(h.wait_for("Klip stopped answering", 10_000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    assert!(h.screen().contains("STOPPED"));
    b.stop().await;
}

#[tokio::test(flavor = "multi_thread", worker_threads = 4)]
async fn a_huge_text_from_the_editor_is_refused_clearly_when_it_exceeds_the_daemons_limit() {
    let dir = fresh("limit");
    let paths = KlipPaths::from_dir(&dir);
    let (_ks, identity) = KeyStore::load_or_create(paths.keystore(), "Alpha").unwrap();
    let clipboard = Arc::new(MemoryClipboardDriver::new(identity.node_id));
    let config = DaemonConfig {
        device_name: "Alpha".into(),
        relay: RelayMode::Lan,
        lan_discovery: false,
        max_clip_bytes: 2000,
        ..DaemonConfig::default()
    };
    let engine = Arc::new(DaemonEngine::init_with(config, paths, Some(clipboard.clone())).await.unwrap());
    let runner = Arc::clone(&engine);
    let task = tokio::spawn(async move {
        let _ = runner.run().await;
    });
    while !dir.join("daemon.sock").exists() {
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20)).await;
    }
    let a = TestDaemon { engine, clipboard, task, dir };
    let mut h = tui_for(&a);
    assert!(h.wait_for("ACTIVE", 10_000).await);
    h.text("5");
    h.paste(&"x".repeat(5000));
    ctrl(&mut h, 's');
    assert!(h.wait_for("is over the limit of", 5_000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    assert!(h.screen().contains("1.9 KiB") || h.screen().contains("2,000 B"), "the limit is shown: {}", h.screen());
    a.stop().await;
}
```

run `cargo test -p klip-tui strip_ansi 2>&1 | tail -4` (fails to compile), then add above the tests module:

```rust
/// Remove ANSI "CSI" sequences (`ESC [ parameters final-byte`): colours and cursor moves that
/// programs write into their logs. Everything else still goes through [`clean`].
pub fn strip_ansi(s: &str) -> String {
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(s.len());
    let mut chars = s.chars().peekable();
    while let Some(c) = chars.next() {
        if c == '\u{1b}' && chars.peek() == Some(&'[') {
            chars.next();
            while let Some(&n) = chars.peek() {
                chars.next();
                if ('\u{40}'..='\u{7e}').contains(&n) {
                    break;
                }
            }
        } else {
            out.push(c);
        }
    }
    out
}
```

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    use crate::testkit::sample_status;
    use crossterm::event::{KeyEvent, KeyModifiers};

        Msg::Key(KeyEvent::new(code, KeyModifiers::NONE))
    }

        a.conn = Conn::Up;
        a.status = Some(sample_status(vec![]));

        assert_eq!(a.screen, Screen::Log);

    }

    #[test]

        let mut a = AppState::new((100, 30));
        a.conn = Conn::Up;
        a.status = Some(sample_status(vec![]));
        let fx = update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Char('6')));
        assert!(matches!(fx.as_slice(), [Effect::TailLog(None)]), "{fx:?}");
        update(&mut a, chunk("one\ntwo\n", 8));
        assert_eq!(a.log.offset, Some(8));
        let t = |a: &mut AppState, ms| update(a, Msg::Tick { mono_ms: ms, wall_ms: ms });
        a.clock.log_ms = 1_000;
        assert!(t(&mut a, 1_300).iter().all(|e| !matches!(e, Effect::TailLog(_))));
        assert!(t(&mut a, 1_600).iter().any(|e| matches!(e, Effect::TailLog(Some(8)))));
        // Not visible: no reads at all.
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Char('1')));
        a.inflight.log = false;
        assert!(t(&mut a, 9_000).iter().all(|e| !matches!(e, Effect::TailLog(_))));
    }

    #[test]
    fn lines_are_appended_colour_codes_removed_and_the_buffer_is_capped() {
        let mut a = app();
        update(&mut a, chunk("\x1b[32m✓\x1b[0m device ABCD\n", 20));
        assert_eq!(a.log.lines.back().unwrap(), "✓ device ABCD");
        let many: String = (0..MAX_LINES + 100).map(|i| format!("line {i}\n")).collect();
        update(&mut a, chunk(&many, 99));
        assert_eq!(a.log.lines.len(), MAX_LINES);
        assert_eq!(a.log.lines.back().unwrap(), &format!("line {}", MAX_LINES + 99));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_truncated_log_starts_over_and_a_missing_one_is_flagged() {
        let mut a = app();
        update(&mut a, chunk("old line\n", 9));
        update(
            &mut a,
            Msg::Reply(Reply::Log(Ok(LogChunk { new_offset: 4, text: "new\n".into(), reset: true, missing: false }))),
        );
        assert_eq!(a.log.lines.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(a.log.lines[0], "new");
        update(
            &mut a,
            Msg::Reply(Reply::Log(Ok(LogChunk { new_offset: 0, text: String::new(), reset: true, missing: true }))),
        );
        assert!(a.log.missing && a.log.lines.is_empty());
        update(&mut a, Msg::Reply(Reply::Log(Err("denied".into()))));
        assert_eq!(a.log.error.as_deref(), Some("denied"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn scrolling_back_stops_following_and_g_resumes() {
        let mut a = app();
        update(&mut a, chunk(&(0..100).map(|i| format!("l{i}\n")).collect::<String>(), 10));
        assert!(a.log.follow);
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::PageUp));
        assert!(!a.log.follow && a.log.scroll_back == 10);
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Char('G')));
        assert!(a.log.follow && a.log.scroll_back == 0);
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Char('f')));
        assert!(!a.log.follow);
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Char('f')));
        assert!(a.log.follow);
        for _ in 0..500 {
            update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Up));
        }
        assert!(a.log.scroll_back <= 99, "cannot scroll past the first line");
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_filter_is_case_insensitive_and_escape_clears_it() {
        let mut a = app();
        update(&mut a, chunk("INFO started\nWARN relay slow\nERROR boom\nINFO done\n", 10));
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Char('/')));
        assert!(a.text_focus());
        for c in "error".chars() {
            update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Char(c)));
        }
        assert_eq!(a.log.shown().len(), 1);
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        assert!(!a.text_focus());
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Esc));
        assert_eq!(a.log.shown().len(), 4);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `screens/log.rs`** (above the tests)

```rust
//! The Log screen: the daemon's own log file, followed live. It reads the file directly, so it works
//! while the daemon is stopped, which is exactly when it is needed.
use crate::app::{AppState, Effect, Screen};
use crate::files::LogChunk;
use crate::sanitize::{one_line, strip_ansi, truncate};
use crate::textbuf::TextBuf;
use crate::theme::Theme;
use crossterm::event::{KeyCode, KeyEvent, KeyModifiers};
use ratatui::layout::Rect;
use ratatui::text::{Line, Span};
use ratatui::widgets::{Block, Paragraph};
use ratatui::Frame;
use std::collections::VecDeque;

/// The log keeps at most this many lines in memory.
pub const MAX_LINES: usize = 5000;
const TAIL_EVERY_MS: u64 = 500;

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct LogState {
    pub lines: VecDeque<String>,
    /// Where the next read starts; `None` before the first read.
    pub offset: Option<u64>,
    pub follow: bool,
    pub filter: TextBuf,
    pub filtering: bool,
    /// How many lines above the end the view is scrolled.
    pub scroll_back: usize,
    pub missing: bool,
    pub error: Option<String>,
}

impl Default for LogState {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            lines: VecDeque::new(),
            offset: None,
            follow: true,
            filter: TextBuf::default(),
            filtering: false,
            scroll_back: 0,
            missing: false,
            error: None,
        }
    }
}

impl LogState {
    /// The lines that pass the filter (case-insensitive).
    pub fn shown(&self) -> Vec<&String> {
        let needle = self.filter.text().to_lowercase();
        self.lines.iter().filter(|l| needle.is_empty() || l.to_lowercase().contains(&needle)).collect()
    }
}

fn tail(app: &mut AppState, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    if app.inflight.log {
        return;
    }
    app.inflight.log = true;
    app.clock.log_ms = app.mono_ms;
    fx.push(Effect::TailLog(app.log.offset));
}

pub fn on_enter(app: &mut AppState, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    tail(app, fx);
}

pub fn on_tick(app: &mut AppState, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    if app.screen == Screen::Log && app.mono_ms.saturating_sub(app.clock.log_ms) >= TAIL_EVERY_MS {
        tail(app, fx);
    }
}

pub fn on_chunk(app: &mut AppState, res: Result<LogChunk, String>) {
    app.inflight.log = false;
    match res {
        Ok(c) => {
            if c.reset {
                app.log.lines.clear();
                app.log.scroll_back = 0;
            }
            app.log.missing = c.missing;
            app.log.error = None;
            for l in c.text.lines() {
                app.log.lines.push_back(strip_ansi(l));
            }
            while app.log.lines.len() > MAX_LINES {
                app.log.lines.pop_front();
            }
            app.log.offset = Some(c.new_offset);
        }
        Err(e) => app.log.error = Some(e),
    }
}

pub fn text_focus(app: &AppState) -> bool {
    app.log.filtering
}

pub fn scroll(app: &mut AppState, delta: i32) {
    // The wheel and the arrows move the view: negative = towards older lines.
    let max = app.log.shown().len().saturating_sub(1);
    if delta < 0 {
        app.log.scroll_back = (app.log.scroll_back + (-delta) as usize).min(max);
        app.log.follow = false;
    } else {
        app.log.scroll_back = app.log.scroll_back.saturating_sub(delta as usize);
    }
}

pub fn key(app: &mut AppState, k: KeyEvent) {
    if app.log.filtering {
        return filter_key(app, k);
    }
    let max = app.log.shown().len().saturating_sub(1);
    match k.code {
        KeyCode::Char('f') => {
            app.log.follow = !app.log.follow;
            if app.log.follow {
                app.log.scroll_back = 0;
            }
        }
        KeyCode::Char('/') => app.log.filtering = true,
        KeyCode::Esc if !app.log.filter.is_empty() => app.log.filter.clear(),
        KeyCode::Up | KeyCode::Char('k') => scroll(app, -1),
        KeyCode::Down | KeyCode::Char('j') => scroll(app, 1),
        KeyCode::PageUp => scroll(app, -10),
        KeyCode::PageDown => scroll(app, 10),
        KeyCode::Char('G') | KeyCode::End => {
            app.log.scroll_back = 0;
            app.log.follow = true;
        }
        KeyCode::Char('g') | KeyCode::Home => {
            app.log.scroll_back = max;
            app.log.follow = false;
        }
        _ => {}
    }
}

fn filter_key(app: &mut AppState, k: KeyEvent) {
    let ctrl = k.modifiers.contains(KeyModifiers::CONTROL);
    let f = &mut app.log.filter;
    match k.code {
        KeyCode::Esc => {
            f.clear();
            app.log.filtering = false;
        }
        KeyCode::Enter => app.log.filtering = false,
        KeyCode::Backspace => f.backspace(),
        KeyCode::Char('u') if ctrl => f.clear(),
        KeyCode::Char(c) if !ctrl => {
            f.insert_char(c);
        }
        _ => {}
    }
    app.log.scroll_back = 0;
}

pub fn view(app: &AppState, theme: &Theme, f: &mut Frame, area: Rect) {
    let title = format!(
        " Log · {} · {} ",
        truncate(&one_line(&app.log_path), (area.width as usize).saturating_sub(24)),
        if app.log.follow { "following" } else { "paused: f resumes" }
    );
    let title = if app.log.filter.is_empty() && !app.log.filtering {
        title
    } else {
        format!("{title}· search “{}” ", one_line(&app.log.filter.text()))
    };
    let block = Block::bordered().title(Span::styled(title, theme.st_title())).border_style(theme.st_faint());
    let inner = block.inner(area);
    f.render_widget(block, area);
    if let Some(e) = &app.log.error {
        crate::screens::centered_text(f, inner, theme.st_bad(), &truncate(&one_line(e), inner.width as usize));
        return;
    }
    if app.log.missing && app.log.lines.is_empty() {
        crate::screens::centered_text(
            f,
            inner,
            theme.st_dim(),
            "No log yet: Klip writes it when it is started with `klip start`.",
        );
        return;
    }
    let shown = app.log.shown();
    if shown.is_empty() {
        let msg = if app.log.lines.is_empty() { "The log is empty." } else { "Nothing matches the search." };
        crate::screens::centered_text(f, inner, theme.st_dim(), msg);
        return;
    }
    let h = inner.height as usize;
    let end = shown.len().saturating_sub(app.log.scroll_back.min(shown.len().saturating_sub(1)));
    let start = end.saturating_sub(h);
    let lines: Vec<Line> = shown[start..end]
        .iter()
        .map(|l| {
            let upper = l.to_uppercase();
            let style = if upper.contains("ERROR") {
                theme.st_bad()
            } else if upper.contains("WARN") {
                theme.st_warn()
            } else {
                theme.st_text()
            };
            Line::from(Span::styled(truncate(&one_line(l), inner.width as usize), style))
        })
        .collect();
    f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(lines), inner);
}
```

Route in `screens/mod.rs`: `key`: `Screen::Log => log::key(app, k),`; `text_focus`: `Screen::Log => log::text_focus(app),`; `scroll`: `Screen::Log => log::scroll(app, delta),`; `on_enter`: `Screen::Log => log::on_enter(app, fx),`; `on_tick`: call `log::on_tick(app, fx);`; `view`: `Screen::Log => log::view(app, theme, f, area),`; `hints`: `Screen::Log => "f follow · / search · PgUp PgDn scroll · G end · "`; `help_lines`: `("f", "follow the end of the log, or stop following")`, `("/", "search (Esc clears)")`, `("↑↓ PgUp PgDn", "scroll")`, `("G / g", "jump to the end / the start")`. Add `impl Default for TextBuf` to `textbuf.rs` if not yet moved (Task 16).

(a) `tests/runtime.rs`:

```rust
#[tokio::test]
async fn the_log_screen_follows_a_real_file_and_survives_truncation_and_absence() {
    use std::io::Write;
    let env = test_env("logscreen");
    let log = klip_tui::files::log_path(&env.paths);
    let fake = FakeDaemon::new();
    let mut h = Harness::new(fake.clone(), env, 100, 30);
    assert!(h.wait_for("ACTIVE", 3000).await);
    h.text("6");
    assert!(h.wait_for("No log yet", 3000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    std::fs::write(&log, "\x1b[32m✓\x1b[0m device ABCD\nsecond line\n").unwrap();
    assert!(h.wait_for("✓ device ABCD", 3000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    assert!(!h.screen().contains('\x1b'));
    let mut f = std::fs::OpenOptions::new().append(true).open(&log).unwrap();
    f.write_all(b"WARN relay slow\nERROR boom \x1b]0;PWNED\x07\n").unwrap();
    assert!(h.wait_for("ERROR boom", 3000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    assert!(h.screen().contains("␛]0;PWNED"), "hostile bytes are shown as text: {}", h.screen());
    std::fs::write(&log, "fresh start\n").unwrap();
    assert!(h.wait_for("fresh start", 3000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    assert!(!h.screen().contains("second line"), "a truncated log starts over");
    std::fs::remove_file(&log).unwrap();
    assert!(h.wait_for("No log yet", 3000).await, "{}", h.screen());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn the_log_is_readable_while_the_daemon_is_stopped() {
    let env = test_env("logdown");
    let log = klip_tui::files::log_path(&env.paths);
    std::fs::write(&log, "Klip stopped right after starting (exit status: 1)\n").unwrap();
    let fake = FakeDaemon::new();
    fake.with(|s| s.up = false);
    let mut h = Harness::new(fake.clone(), env, 100, 30);
    assert!(h.wait_for("Klip is not running", 3000).await);
    h.text("6");
    assert!(h.wait_for("stopped right after starting", 3000).await, "{}", h.screen());
}
```

```rust
#[test]
fn log_screen_states() {
    let mut a = up(100, 30, two_peers());
    a.screen = Screen::Log;
    a.log_path = "/home/user/.config/klip/daemon.log".to_string();
    snap("log_missing", &(|| { let mut m = up(100, 30, two_peers()); m.screen = Screen::Log; m.log.missing = true; m })(), 100, 30);
    for l in [
        "Starting Klip in the foreground…",
        "✓ device VCUU-LBYY-ES2H  (n0 public relays + discovery)",
        "WARN relay unreachable: dns error",
        "ERROR send to Pixel 8 failed: timed out",
        "hostile \x1b[2J\x1b]0;PWNED\x07 \u{202E}line",
        "a very long line that does not fit in the box and has to be cut with an ellipsis at the right edge of the window, really",
    ] {
        a.log.lines.push_back(l.to_string());
    }
    snap("log", &a, 100, 30);
    snap("log", &a, 60, 16);
    a.log.filter.insert_str("warn");
    a.log.filtering = true;
    snap("log_search", &a, 100, 30);
}
```

```rust
fn the_log_screen_shows_the_real_daemon_log_without_colour_codes() {
    let (_d, mut p) = running();

    p.send(b"6");
```

Review: the hostile line shows `␛` and `⟦U+202E⟧`; the long line ends with `…`; the title shows the path and "following". Then:

```bash
git add crates/klip-tui crates/klip-cli
git commit -F - <<'EOF'
feat(tui): Log screen - follow daemon.log live, readable even when the daemon is stopped

Why / Context: when Klip does not start, the log is the answer. The screen reads the file directly
and must cope with colour codes, hostile bytes, truncation, rotation and absence.
Pins Review Focus item 3 (broken or odd files).

Inventory:
- screens/log.rs: first read = last 256 KiB from a line boundary, follow every 500 ms while visible,
  5,000-line cap, filter, scroll/follow/jump keys, ERROR/WARN highlighting; 5 unit tests
- sanitize::strip_ansi (daemon.log contains colour codes from its own start-up messages)
- runtime keeps the TuiEnv and passes it to file effects; TailLog effect / Log reply
- tests: 2 harness tests with a real file (absent -> present -> appended -> truncated -> removed;
  stopped daemon), 4 snapshots, fuzz pool, PTY test against a real daemon's real log

Verification / Impact: <paste results>.

Co-Authored-By: Claude Sonnet 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>
EOF
```

### Task 20: The Settings screen — edit `config.json` safely, restart, auto-start

**Files:**
- Create: `crates/klip-tui/src/screens/settings.rs`
- Modify: `crates/klip-tui/src/{app.rs, runtime.rs}`, `crates/klip-tui/src/screens/mod.rs`, `crates/klip-tui/tests/{runtime,snapshots,keyfuzz}.rs`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `klip_core::{DaemonConfig, RelayMode}`, `files::{write_config_atomic, service_state, ServiceState}`, `api::{ConfigInfo, HelloInfo}`.
- Produces:
  - `settings::{SettingsState, Row, parse_size(&str) -> Result<usize, String>, same(&DaemonConfig, &DaemonConfig) -> bool, info_of(&DaemonConfig) -> ConfigInfo, needs_restart(&AppState) -> bool}`
  - `Effect::{ReadConfig, WriteConfig(DaemonConfig), ReadService, InstallService, RestartDaemon}`; `Reply::{Config(Result<DaemonConfig, String>), ConfigSaved(Result<(), String>), Service(ServiceState), ServiceInstalled(Result<String, ApiError>), DaemonRestarted(Result<u32, ApiError>)}`
  - `ConfirmAction::{RestartDaemon, ResetConfig}`; `AppState.settings: SettingsState`
  - `boot()` also reads the config and the service state

```rust
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::app::{update, Conn, HelloState, Modal, Msg, Reply, Screen};
    use crate::testkit::{sample_hello, sample_status};
    use crossterm::event::{KeyEvent, KeyModifiers};

    fn key(code: KeyCode) -> Msg {
        Msg::Key(KeyEvent::new(code, KeyModifiers::NONE))
    }
    fn ch(c: char) -> Msg {
        key(KeyCode::Char(c))
    }
    fn app() -> AppState {
        let mut a = AppState::new((100, 30));
        a.conn = Conn::Up;
        a.status = Some(sample_status(vec![]));
        a.hello = HelloState::New(sample_hello());
        update(&mut a, Msg::Reply(Reply::Config(Ok(DaemonConfig::default()))));
        update(&mut a, ch('7'));
        assert_eq!(a.screen, Screen::Settings);
        a
    }
    fn select(a: &mut AppState, row: Row) {
        a.settings.selected = ROW_ORDER.iter().position(|r| *r == row).unwrap();
    }

    #[test]
    fn sizes_parse_with_binary_units_and_reject_nonsense() {
        assert_eq!(parse_size("32 MiB").unwrap(), 33_554_432);
        assert_eq!(parse_size("512kb").unwrap(), 524_288);
        assert_eq!(parse_size("1.5 MiB").unwrap(), 1_572_864);
        assert_eq!(parse_size("1,048,576").unwrap(), 1_048_576);
        assert_eq!(parse_size("2gib").unwrap(), 2_147_483_648);
        assert_eq!(parse_size("100").unwrap(), 100);
        assert!(parse_size("").is_err());
        assert!(parse_size("0").is_err());
        assert!(parse_size("10x").unwrap_err().contains("unit"));
        assert!(parse_size("MiB").is_err());
        assert!(parse_size("1e99 GiB").is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn entering_the_screen_reads_the_config_and_the_service_state() {
        let mut a = AppState::new((100, 30));
        a.conn = Conn::Up;
        let fx = update(&mut a, ch('7'));
        assert!(fx.iter().any(|e| matches!(e, Effect::ReadConfig)) && fx.iter().any(|e| matches!(e, Effect::ReadService)), "{fx:?}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn toggles_flip_and_unsaved_changes_are_flagged_and_saved_on_s() {
        let mut a = app();
        select(&mut a, Row::Relay);
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        assert_eq!(a.settings.draft.relay, RelayMode::Lan);
        assert!(dirty(&a));
        select(&mut a, Row::LanDiscovery);
        update(&mut a, ch(' '));
        assert!(!a.settings.draft.lan_discovery);
        let fx = update(&mut a, ch('s'));
        assert!(matches!(fx.as_slice(), [Effect::WriteConfig(c)] if c.relay == RelayMode::Lan && !c.lan_discovery), "{fx:?}");
        update(&mut a, Msg::Reply(Reply::ConfigSaved(Ok(()))));
        assert!(!dirty(&a));
        assert!(a.toasts.iter().any(|t| t.text.contains("Restart")), "{:?}", a.toasts);
    }

    #[test]
    fn u_discards_unsaved_changes() {
        let mut a = app();
        select(&mut a, Row::LanDiscovery);
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        assert!(dirty(&a));
        update(&mut a, ch('u'));
        assert!(!dirty(&a));
    }

    #[test]
    fn text_and_number_fields_validate_before_they_apply() {
        let mut a = app();
        select(&mut a, Row::DeviceName);
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        assert!(a.text_focus());
        for _ in 0..40 {
            update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Backspace));
        }
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        assert!(a.settings.error.is_some(), "an empty name is refused");
        for c in "My Laptop".chars() {
            update(&mut a, ch(c));
        }
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        assert_eq!(a.settings.draft.device_name, "My Laptop");
        assert!(!a.text_focus());

        select(&mut a, Row::ClipSize);
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        for _ in 0..20 {
            update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Backspace));
        }
        for c in "999 GiB".chars() {
            update(&mut a, ch(c));
        }
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        assert!(a.settings.error.is_some(), "above the daemon's 256 MiB maximum");
        assert!(a.text_focus(), "the field stays open so it can be fixed");
        for _ in 0..20 {
            update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Backspace));
        }
        for c in "8 MiB".chars() {
            update(&mut a, ch(c));
        }
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        assert_eq!(a.settings.draft.max_clip_bytes, 8 * 1024 * 1024);

        select(&mut a, Row::HistoryItems);
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        for _ in 0..10 {
            update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Backspace));
        }
        update(&mut a, ch('0'));
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        assert!(a.settings.error.is_some(), "zero history items is refused");
    }

    #[test]
    fn escape_cancels_an_edit_and_a_control_character_name_is_refused() {
        let mut a = app();
        select(&mut a, Row::DeviceName);
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        update(&mut a, ch('X'));
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Esc));
        assert!(!a.text_focus());
        assert!(!dirty(&a));
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        a.settings.editing.as_mut().unwrap().clear();
        a.settings.editing.as_mut().unwrap().insert_str("bad\x1b[2Jname");
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        assert!(a.settings.error.is_some());
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_malformed_config_blocks_saving_until_it_is_reset_explicitly() {
        let mut a = app();
        update(&mut a, Msg::Reply(Reply::Config(Err("config.json: expected value at line 1".into()))));
        assert!(a.settings.load_error.is_some());
        let fx = update(&mut a, ch('s'));
        assert!(fx.is_empty(), "never overwrite a file we could not read");
        select(&mut a, Row::Reset);
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        assert!(matches!(a.modal, Some(Modal::Confirm { .. })));
        let fx = update(&mut a, ch('y'));
        assert!(matches!(fx.as_slice(), [Effect::WriteConfig(_)]), "{fx:?}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn restart_needed_is_judged_against_what_the_daemon_really_runs() {
        let mut a = app();
        assert!(!needs_restart(&a), "the file equals what the daemon reported");
        select(&mut a, Row::HistoryItems);
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        for _ in 0..5 {
            update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Backspace));
        }
        update(&mut a, ch('9'));
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        assert!(!needs_restart(&a), "unsaved changes are not on disk yet");
        update(&mut a, ch('s'));
        update(&mut a, Msg::Reply(Reply::ConfigSaved(Ok(()))));
        assert!(needs_restart(&a));

        // An old daemon says nothing: the file as first read is the baseline.
        let mut b = AppState::new((100, 30));
        b.conn = Conn::Up;
        b.hello = HelloState::Old;
        let mut cfg = DaemonConfig::default();
        update(&mut b, Msg::Reply(Reply::Config(Ok(cfg.clone()))));
        assert!(!needs_restart(&b));
        cfg.max_history_items = 7;
        update(&mut b, Msg::Reply(Reply::Config(Ok(cfg))));
        assert!(needs_restart(&b));
    }

    #[test]
    fn restart_asks_first_and_reports_the_result() {
        let mut a = app();
        select(&mut a, Row::Restart);
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        assert!(matches!(a.modal, Some(Modal::Confirm { .. })));
        let fx = update(&mut a, ch('y'));
        assert!(matches!(fx.as_slice(), [Effect::RestartDaemon]), "{fx:?}");
        assert!(a.settings.restarting);
        update(&mut a, Msg::Reply(Reply::DaemonRestarted(Ok(4242))));
        assert!(!a.settings.restarting);
        assert!(a.toasts.iter().any(|t| t.text.contains("4242")));
        update(&mut a, Msg::Reply(Reply::DaemonRestarted(Err(crate::api::ApiError::Refused("no".into())))));
        assert!(matches!(a.modal, Some(Modal::Notice { .. })));
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_service_row_installs_only_when_missing() {
        use crate::files::ServiceState;
        let mut a = app();
        select(&mut a, Row::Service);
        update(&mut a, Msg::Reply(Reply::Service(ServiceState::NotInstalled)));
        let fx = update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        assert!(matches!(fx.as_slice(), [Effect::InstallService]), "{fx:?}");
        update(&mut a, Msg::Reply(Reply::ServiceInstalled(Ok("Installed systemd unit: /x\nNow run: systemctl --user enable --now klip.service".into()))));
        assert!(matches!(&a.modal, Some(Modal::Notice { body, .. }) if body.contains("systemctl")));
        update(&mut a, Msg::Reply(Reply::Service(ServiceState::Installed("/x".into()))));
        a.modal = None;
        let fx = update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        assert!(fx.is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_new_config_read_does_not_throw_away_unsaved_edits() {
        let mut a = app();
        select(&mut a, Row::LanDiscovery);
        update(&mut a, key(KeyCode::Enter));
        update(&mut a, Msg::Reply(Reply::Config(Ok(DaemonConfig::default()))));
        assert!(dirty(&a), "the draft survives a re-read");
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Extend the core**
