- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `screens/settings.rs`** (above the tests)

```rust
//! The Settings screen: edit `config.json` with the daemon's own validation, restart Klip to apply it,
//! install the auto-start unit. Reads and writes the file directly, so it works with Klip stopped.
use crate::app::{AppState, ConfirmAction, Effect, Modal, ToastKind};
use crate::files::ServiceState;
use crate::fmt;
use crate::sanitize::{clean, one_line, tail, truncate};
use crate::textbuf::TextBuf;
use crate::theme::Theme;
use crossterm::event::{KeyCode, KeyEvent, KeyModifiers};
use klip_core::{DaemonConfig, RelayMode};
use klip_proto::ipc::ConfigInfo;
use ratatui::layout::{Constraint, Layout, Rect};
use ratatui::text::{Line, Span};
use ratatui::widgets::{Block, Paragraph, Wrap};
use ratatui::Frame;

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Row {
    DeviceName,
    Relay,
    HistoryItems,
    ClipSize,
    LanDiscovery,
    PollInterval,
    Service,
    Restart,
    Reset,
}

pub const ROW_ORDER: [Row; 9] = [
    Row::DeviceName,
    Row::Relay,
    Row::HistoryItems,
    Row::ClipSize,
    Row::LanDiscovery,
    Row::PollInterval,
    Row::Service,
    Row::Restart,
    Row::Reset,
];

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct SettingsState {
    /// The last successful read of `config.json` (a missing file reads as the defaults).
    pub file: Option<DaemonConfig>,
    /// Why the file could not be read, when it could not.
    pub load_error: Option<String>,
    pub draft: DaemonConfig,
    /// What a daemon that cannot report its settings (v0.3.6) was started with: the file as first read.
    pub baseline: Option<DaemonConfig>,
    pub selected: usize,
    pub editing: Option<TextBuf>,
    /// Why the value being edited was refused.
    pub error: Option<String>,
    pub restarting: bool,
    pub service: Option<ServiceState>,
}

impl Default for SettingsState {
    fn default() -> Self {
        Self {
            file: None,
            load_error: None,
            draft: DaemonConfig::default(),
            baseline: None,
            selected: 0,
            editing: None,
            error: None,
            restarting: false,
            service: None,
        }
    }
}

/// Field-by-field equality (`DaemonConfig` does not implement `PartialEq`).
pub fn same(a: &DaemonConfig, b: &DaemonConfig) -> bool {
    a.device_name == b.device_name
        && a.relay == b.relay
        && a.max_history_items == b.max_history_items
        && a.poll_interval_ms == b.poll_interval_ms
        && a.max_clip_bytes == b.max_clip_bytes
        && a.lan_discovery == b.lan_discovery
}

pub fn info_of(c: &DaemonConfig) -> ConfigInfo {
    ConfigInfo {
        device_name: c.device_name.clone(),
        relay: match c.relay {
            RelayMode::N0 => "n0".to_string(),
            RelayMode::Lan => "lan".to_string(),
        },
        max_history_items: c.max_history_items,
        max_clip_bytes: c.max_clip_bytes,
        lan_discovery: c.lan_discovery,
    }
}

pub fn dirty(app: &AppState) -> bool {
    app.settings.file.as_ref().map(|f| !same(f, &app.settings.draft)).unwrap_or(false)
}

/// True when the saved file differs from what the running daemon uses.
pub fn needs_restart(app: &AppState) -> bool {
    let Some(file) = &app.settings.file else { return false };
    let running = match &app.hello {
        crate::app::HelloState::New(h) => Some(h.config.clone()),
        _ => app.settings.baseline.as_ref().map(info_of),
    };
    running.map(|r| r != info_of(file)).unwrap_or(false)
}

/// "32 MiB", "512kb", "1.5 MiB", "1,048,576", "2gib": binary units (1 KiB = 1024 bytes).
pub fn parse_size(s: &str) -> Result<usize, String> {
    let t: String = s.trim().chars().filter(|c| *c != '_' && *c != ',').collect();
    if t.is_empty() {
        return Err("type a size, for example 32 MiB".to_string());
    }
    let split = t.find(|c: char| !(c.is_ascii_digit() || c == '.')).unwrap_or(t.len());
    let (num, unit) = t.split_at(split);
    let value: f64 = num.parse().map_err(|_| format!("'{num}' is not a number"))?;
    let mult: f64 = match unit.trim().to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
        "" | "b" => 1.0,
        "k" | "kb" | "kib" => 1024.0,
        "m" | "mb" | "mib" => 1024.0 * 1024.0,
        "g" | "gb" | "gib" => 1024.0 * 1024.0 * 1024.0,
        other => return Err(format!("unknown unit '{other}': use B, KiB, MiB or GiB")),
    };
    let bytes = value * mult;
    if !bytes.is_finite() || bytes < 1.0 || bytes > usize::MAX as f64 {
        return Err("that size is out of range".to_string());
    }
    Ok(bytes as usize)
}

fn row(app: &AppState) -> Row {
    ROW_ORDER[app.settings.selected.min(ROW_ORDER.len() - 1)]
}

pub fn text_focus(app: &AppState) -> bool {
    app.settings.editing.is_some()
}

pub fn on_enter(fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    fx.push(Effect::ReadConfig);
    fx.push(Effect::ReadService);
}

pub fn on_leave(app: &mut AppState) {
    app.settings.editing = None;
    app.settings.error = None;
}

pub fn on_config(app: &mut AppState, res: Result<DaemonConfig, String>) {
    let s = &mut app.settings;
    match res {
        Ok(cfg) => {
            let was_clean = s.file.as_ref().map(|f| same(f, &s.draft)).unwrap_or(true);
            if s.baseline.is_none() {
                s.baseline = Some(cfg.clone());
            }
            s.load_error = None;
            s.file = Some(cfg.clone());
            if was_clean {
                s.draft = cfg;
            }
        }
        Err(e) => {
            s.load_error = Some(e);
            s.file = None;
        }
    }
}

pub fn on_saved(app: &mut AppState, res: Result<(), String>) {
    match res {
        Ok(()) => {
            app.settings.file = Some(app.settings.draft.clone());
            app.settings.load_error = None;
            app.toast(ToastKind::Ok, "Saved. Restart Klip to apply it (R).");
        }
        Err(e) => app.toast(ToastKind::Error, format!("Not saved: {}", one_line(&e))),
    }
}

pub fn on_restarted(app: &mut AppState, res: Result<u32, crate::api::ApiError>) {
    app.settings.restarting = false;
    match res {
        Ok(pid) => app.toast(ToastKind::Ok, format!("Klip restarted (PID {pid})")),
        Err(e) => {
            app.modal = Some(Modal::Notice {
                title: "Klip did not restart".to_string(),
                body: format!("{}\n\nThe Log screen (6) shows why.", e.plain()),
            });
        }
    }
}

fn commit(app: &mut AppState) {
    let Some(buf) = app.settings.editing.as_ref() else { return };
    let text = buf.text();
    let mut next = app.settings.draft.clone();
    let result: Result<(), String> = match row(app) {
        Row::DeviceName => {
            let name = text.trim();
            if name.is_empty() {
                Err("the name cannot be empty".to_string())
            } else if name.chars().count() > 64 {
                Err("the name can have at most 64 characters".to_string())
            } else if name.chars().any(|c| c.is_control()) {
                Err("the name cannot contain control characters".to_string())
            } else {
                next.device_name = name.to_string();
                Ok(())
            }
        }
        Row::HistoryItems => match text.trim().parse::<usize>() {
            Ok(n) if (1..=1000).contains(&n) => {
                next.max_history_items = n;
                Ok(())
            }
            _ => Err("enter a number from 1 to 1000".to_string()),
        },
        Row::ClipSize => parse_size(&text).map(|n| next.max_clip_bytes = n),
        _ => Ok(()),
    };
    let result = result.and_then(|_| next.validate());
    match result {
        Ok(()) => {
            app.settings.draft = next;
            app.settings.editing = None;
            app.settings.error = None;
        }
        Err(e) => app.settings.error = Some(e),
    }
}

pub fn paste(app: &mut AppState, text: &str) {
    if let Some(b) = app.settings.editing.as_mut() {
        let one: String = text.chars().filter(|c| *c != '\n' && *c != '\r').collect();
        b.insert_str(&one);
    }
}

pub fn scroll(app: &mut AppState, delta: i32) {
    let next = (app.settings.selected as i64 + i64::from(delta)).clamp(0, ROW_ORDER.len() as i64 - 1);
    app.settings.selected = next as usize;
}

pub fn key(app: &mut AppState, k: KeyEvent, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    if app.settings.editing.is_some() {
        return edit_key(app, k);
    }
    match k.code {
        KeyCode::Up | KeyCode::Char('k') => scroll(app, -1),
        KeyCode::Down | KeyCode::Char('j') => scroll(app, 1),
        KeyCode::Char('s') => {
            if app.settings.load_error.is_some() {
                app.toast(ToastKind::Warn, "config.json has an error: reset it from the last row first");
            } else {
                fx.push(Effect::WriteConfig(app.settings.draft.clone()));
            }
        }
        KeyCode::Char('u') => {
            if let Some(f) = app.settings.file.clone() {
                app.settings.draft = f;
                app.settings.error = None;
            }
        }
        KeyCode::Char('R') => ask_restart(app),
        KeyCode::Enter | KeyCode::Char(' ') => activate(app, fx),
        _ => {}
    }
}

fn ask_restart(app: &mut AppState) {
    app.modal = Some(Modal::Confirm {
        title: "Restart Klip?".to_string(),
        body: "Klip stops and starts again so it reads the saved settings. Syncing pauses for a few seconds.".to_string(),
        action: ConfirmAction::RestartDaemon,
    });
}

fn activate(app: &mut AppState, fx: &mut Vec<Effect>) {
    match row(app) {
        Row::DeviceName => {
            app.settings.editing = Some(TextBuf::from_text(&app.settings.draft.device_name, 256));
            if let Some(b) = app.settings.editing.as_mut() {
                b.end();
            }
        }
        Row::HistoryItems => {
            let mut b = TextBuf::from_text(&app.settings.draft.max_history_items.to_string(), 16);
            b.end();
            app.settings.editing = Some(b);
        }
        Row::ClipSize => {
            let mut b = TextBuf::from_text(&fmt::bytes(app.settings.draft.max_clip_bytes).replace(',', ""), 32);
            b.end();
            app.settings.editing = Some(b);
        }
        Row::Relay => {
            app.settings.draft.relay = match app.settings.draft.relay {
                RelayMode::N0 => RelayMode::Lan,
                RelayMode::Lan => RelayMode::N0,
            };
        }
        Row::LanDiscovery => app.settings.draft.lan_discovery = !app.settings.draft.lan_discovery,
        Row::PollInterval => app.toast(ToastKind::Info, "Advanced: edit poll_interval_ms in config.json by hand"),
        Row::Service => match &app.settings.service {
            Some(ServiceState::NotInstalled) => fx.push(Effect::InstallService),
            Some(ServiceState::Installed(p)) => {
                let msg = format!("Already installed: {}", p.display());
                app.toast(ToastKind::Info, msg);
            }
            _ => app.toast(ToastKind::Info, "Auto-start is not available on this system"),
        },
        Row::Restart => ask_restart(app),
        Row::Reset => {
            app.modal = Some(Modal::Confirm {
                title: "Reset all settings?".to_string(),
                body: "config.json is replaced by the defaults. Klip must be restarted to use them.".to_string(),
                action: ConfirmAction::ResetConfig,
            });
        }
    }
    app.settings.error = None;
}

fn edit_key(app: &mut AppState, k: KeyEvent) {
    let ctrl = k.modifiers.contains(KeyModifiers::CONTROL);
    match k.code {
        KeyCode::Esc => {
            app.settings.editing = None;
            app.settings.error = None;
        }
        KeyCode::Enter => commit(app),
        _ => {
            if let Some(b) = app.settings.editing.as_mut() {
                match k.code {
                    KeyCode::Backspace => b.backspace(),
                    KeyCode::Delete => b.delete(),
                    KeyCode::Left => b.left(),
                    KeyCode::Right => b.right(),
                    KeyCode::Home => b.home(),
                    KeyCode::End => b.end(),
                    KeyCode::Char('u') if ctrl => b.clear(),
                    KeyCode::Char('w') if ctrl => b.delete_word_back(),
                    KeyCode::Char(c) if !ctrl => {
                        b.insert_char(c);
                    }
                    _ => {}
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- drawing

fn label(r: Row) -> &'static str {
    match r {
        Row::DeviceName => "Device name",
        Row::Relay => "Network",
        Row::HistoryItems => "History items",
        Row::ClipSize => "Largest clip",
        Row::LanDiscovery => "LAN discovery",
        Row::PollInterval => "Poll interval",
        Row::Service => "Auto-start",
        Row::Restart => "Apply",
        Row::Reset => "Defaults",
    }
}

fn value(app: &AppState, r: Row) -> String {
    let d = &app.settings.draft;
    match r {
        Row::DeviceName => one_line(&d.device_name),
        Row::Relay => match d.relay {
            RelayMode::N0 => "n0 public relays: they see who talks to whom, never the content".to_string(),
            RelayMode::Lan => "lan only: no third-party server, devices must be on the same network".to_string(),
        },
        Row::HistoryItems => d.max_history_items.to_string(),
        Row::ClipSize => fmt::bytes(d.max_clip_bytes),
        Row::LanDiscovery => if d.lan_discovery { "on" } else { "off" }.to_string(),
        Row::PollInterval => format!("{} ms (advanced; edit config.json)", d.poll_interval_ms),
        Row::Service => match &app.settings.service {
            Some(ServiceState::Installed(p)) => format!("installed ({})", one_line(&p.display().to_string())),
            Some(ServiceState::NotInstalled) => "not installed: Enter installs it".to_string(),
            Some(ServiceState::Unsupported) => "not available on this system".to_string(),
            None => "checking…".to_string(),
        },
        Row::Restart => {
            if app.settings.restarting {
                "restarting Klip…".to_string()
            } else {
                "restart Klip so it reads the saved settings (R)".to_string()
            }
        }
        Row::Reset => "reset every setting to its default".to_string(),
    }
}

pub fn view(app: &AppState, theme: &Theme, f: &mut Frame, area: Rect) {
    let s = &app.settings;
    let block = Block::bordered().title(Span::styled(" Settings ", theme.st_title())).border_style(theme.st_faint());
    let inner = block.inner(area);
    f.render_widget(block, area);
    let [banner, rows, about] =
        Layout::vertical([Constraint::Length(3), Constraint::Min(4), Constraint::Length(3)]).areas(inner);

    let mut notes: Vec<Line> = Vec::new();
    if let Some(e) = &s.load_error {
        notes.push(Line::from(Span::styled(format!("✗ config.json cannot be read: {}", one_line(e)), theme.st_bad())));
        notes.push(Line::from(Span::styled("It is left untouched. Reset it from the last row, or fix it by hand.", theme.st_dim())));
    } else {
        if dirty(app) {
            notes.push(Line::from(Span::styled("● unsaved changes: s saves, u discards", theme.st_warn())));
        }
        if needs_restart(app) {
            notes.push(Line::from(Span::styled("● the running Klip uses other settings: R restarts it", theme.st_warn())));
        }
        if notes.is_empty() {
            notes.push(Line::from(Span::styled("Everything is saved and in use.", theme.st_dim())));
        }
    }
    f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(notes).wrap(Wrap { trim: true }), banner);

    let w = rows.width as usize;
    let (start, end) = crate::screens::window(s.selected, ROW_ORDER.len(), rows.height as usize);
    let mut lines: Vec<Line> = Vec::new();
    for (i, r) in ROW_ORDER.iter().enumerate().take(end).skip(start) {
        let sel = i == s.selected;
        let base = if sel { theme.st_selected() } else { theme.st_text() };
        let text = match (&s.editing, sel) {
            (Some(b), true) => format!("{}▏", clean(&b.text())),
            _ => value(app, *r),
        };
        lines.push(Line::from(vec![
            Span::styled(if sel { "▸ " } else { "  " }, base),
            Span::styled(format!("{:<15}", label(*r)), if sel { base } else { theme.st_dim() }),
            Span::styled(tail(&text, w.saturating_sub(18)), base),
        ]));
        if sel {
            if let Some(e) = &s.error {
                lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(format!("    ✗ {}", one_line(e)), theme.st_bad())));
            }
        }
    }
    f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(lines), rows);

    let daemon = match &app.hello {
        crate::app::HelloState::New(h) => format!("daemon {}", one_line(&h.daemon_version)),
        crate::app::HelloState::Old => "daemon older than this program (it reports no version)".to_string(),
        crate::app::HelloState::Unknown => "daemon: not known yet".to_string(),
    };
    let tui = env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION");
    let mut about_lines = vec![Line::from(Span::styled(
        truncate(&format!("klip {tui} · {daemon}"), about.width as usize),
        theme.st_dim(),
    ))];
    if let crate::app::HelloState::New(h) = &app.hello {
        if h.daemon_version != tui {
            about_lines.push(Line::from(Span::styled(
                "The daemon runs another version than this program: restart it (klip stop, klip start).",
                theme.st_warn(),
            )));
        }
    }
    f.render_widget(Paragraph::new(about_lines).wrap(Wrap { trim: true }), about);
}
```

Route in `screens/mod.rs`: `key`: `Screen::Settings => settings::key(app, k, fx),`; `paste`: add `Screen::Settings => settings::paste(app, text),`; `text_focus`: `Screen::Settings => settings::text_focus(app),`; `scroll`: `Screen::Settings => settings::scroll(app, delta),`; `on_enter`: `Screen::Settings => settings::on_enter(fx),`; `on_leave`: `Screen::Settings => settings::on_leave(app),`; `view`: `Screen::Settings => settings::view(app, theme, f, area),` — and with that the placeholder arm is **no longer reachable: replace the `other => centered_text(...)` arm by nothing** so the `match` is exhaustive over `Screen`; `hints`: `Screen::Settings => "↑↓ select · Enter edit/toggle · s save · u discard · R restart · "`; `help_lines`: `("↑↓ / j k", "select a setting")`, `("Enter / Space", "edit or toggle the selected setting")`, `("s", "save to config.json")`, `("u", "discard unsaved changes")`, `("R", "restart Klip to apply the saved settings")`.

- [ ] **Step 5: Harness, snapshot and fuzz coverage**

(a) `tests/runtime.rs`:

```rust
#[tokio::test]
async fn editing_a_setting_and_saving_writes_a_valid_private_file() {
    use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
    let env = test_env("settingsave");
    let cfg_path = env.paths.config_file();
    let fake = FakeDaemon::new();
    let mut h = Harness::new(fake.clone(), env, 100, 30);
    assert!(h.wait_for("ACTIVE", 3000).await);
    h.text("7");
    assert!(h.wait_for("Everything is saved and in use", 3000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    h.key(KeyCode::Enter); // edit the device name
    for _ in 0..30 {
        h.key(KeyCode::Backspace);
    }
    h.text("Desk laptop");
    h.key(KeyCode::Enter);
    assert!(h.wait_for("unsaved changes", 1000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    h.text("s");
    assert!(h.wait_for("Saved. Restart Klip", 3000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&cfg_path).unwrap();
    assert!(text.contains("Desk laptop"), "{text}");
    assert_eq!(std::fs::metadata(&cfg_path).unwrap().permissions().mode() & 0o777, 0o600);
    let back = klip_core::DaemonConfig::load(&klip_core::KlipPaths::from_dir(cfg_path.parent().unwrap())).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(back.device_name, "Desk laptop");
    // The fake daemon reports "Laptop", so the saved name differs from what runs.
    assert!(h.wait_for("the running Klip uses other settings", 2000).await, "{}", h.screen());
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_malformed_config_is_shown_and_never_overwritten() {
    let env = test_env("settingbad");
    std::fs::write(env.paths.config_file(), "{ this is not json").unwrap();
    let before = std::fs::read_to_string(env.paths.config_file()).unwrap();
    let path = env.paths.config_file();
    let fake = FakeDaemon::new();
    let mut h = Harness::new(fake.clone(), env, 100, 30);
    assert!(h.wait_for("ACTIVE", 3000).await);
    h.text("7");
    assert!(h.wait_for("config.json cannot be read", 3000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    h.text("s");
    h.pump(300).await;
    assert_eq!(std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap(), before, "the broken file must stay as it was");
    assert!(h.screen().contains("fix it by hand") || h.screen().contains("left untouched"));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn restarting_stops_waits_and_starts_the_daemon_again() {
    let fake = FakeDaemon::new();
    let mut h = harness(&fake, "restart");
    assert!(h.wait_for("ACTIVE", 3000).await);
    h.text("7");
    for _ in 0..7 {
        h.key(KeyCode::Down); // to the "Apply" row
    }
    h.key(KeyCode::Enter);
    assert!(h.screen().contains("Restart Klip?"), "{}", h.screen());
    h.text("y");
    assert!(h.wait_for("Klip restarted (PID 4242)", 8000).await, "{}", h.screen());
    let calls = fake.calls();
    let stop = calls.iter().position(|c| c == "stop_daemon").expect("stopped");
    let start = calls.iter().position(|c| c == "start_daemon").expect("started");
    assert!(stop < start, "{calls:?}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn auto_start_installs_when_missing() {
    let env = test_env("settingsvc");
    let fake = FakeDaemon::new();
    let mut h = Harness::new(fake.clone(), env, 100, 30);
    assert!(h.wait_for("ACTIVE", 3000).await);
    h.text("7");
    for _ in 0..6 {
        h.key(KeyCode::Down); // to the "Auto-start" row
    }
    assert!(h.wait_for("Auto-start", 1000).await);
    // The harness cannot assume what the developer's home contains: only check the call path when the
    // row says it can install.
    if h.wait_for("not installed", 1500).await {
        h.key(KeyCode::Enter);
        assert!(h.wait_for("Auto-start installed", 3000).await, "{}", h.screen());
        assert!(fake.calls().iter().any(|c| c == "install_service"));
    }
}
```
(`harness(&fake, tag)` is the helper at the top of the file.)

```rust
#[test]
fn every_colour_mode_uses_the_signal_colour_for_the_active_state_and_never_relies_on_colour_alone() {
    use klip_tui::theme::{Theme, TuiEnv};
    use ratatui::backend::TestBackend;
    use ratatui::style::{Color, Modifier};
    use ratatui::Terminal;
    let a = up(100, 30, two_peers());
    let cases: [(&[(&str, &str)], Color, bool); 5] = [
        (&[("COLORTERM", "truecolor")], Color::Rgb(200, 242, 90), false),
        (&[("COLORTERM", "truecolor"), ("KLIP_THEME", "light")], Color::Rgb(77, 122, 0), false),
        (&[("TERM", "xterm-256color")], Color::Indexed(192), false),
        (&[("TERM", "xterm")], Color::Green, false),
        (&[("NO_COLOR", "1"), ("COLORTERM", "truecolor")], Color::Reset, true),
    ];
    for (vars, want, plain) in cases {
        let env = TuiEnv::from_vars(klip_core::KlipPaths::from_dir(std::env::temp_dir().join("klip-tui-theme-snap")), |k| {
            vars.iter().find(|(n, _)| *n == k).map(|(_, v)| v.to_string())
        });
        let theme = Theme::from_env(&env);
        let mut term = Terminal::new(TestBackend::new(100, 30)).unwrap();
        term.draw(|f| klip_tui::view::view(&a, &theme, f)).unwrap();
        let buf = term.backend().buffer();
        // Find the "ACTIVE" word in the status box and look at its first cell.
        let mut found = None;
        for y in 0..buf.area.height {
            let row: String = (0..buf.area.width).map(|x| buf[(x, y)].symbol().to_string()).collect();
            if let Some(pos) = row.find("● ACTIVE").filter(|_| y > 0) {
                found = Some((row[..pos].chars().count() as u16, y));
                break;
            }
        }
        let (x, y) = found.expect("the ACTIVE marker is on screen");
        let cell = &buf[(x, y)];
        if plain {
            assert_eq!(cell.fg, Color::Reset);
            assert!(cell.modifier.contains(Modifier::BOLD), "plain mode emphasises with bold");
        } else {
            assert_eq!(cell.fg, want);
        }
        // The state is always also written in words.
        assert!(buf[(x + 2, y)].symbol() == "A");
    }
}

        }
    }
}
```

```rust
        Reply::Hello(res) => {
            app.inflight.hello = false;
            match res {
                Ok(h) => {
                    let mine = env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION");
                    if h.daemon_version != mine && !app.told_version {
                        app.told_version = true;
                        app.toast(
                            ToastKind::Warn,
                            format!("Klip daemon {} differs from this program {mine}: restart it (klip stop, klip start)", h.daemon_version),
                        );
                    }
                    app.hello = HelloState::New(h);
                }
                Err(ApiError::Unsupported) | Err(ApiError::Refused(_)) => {
                    if !app.told_old {
                        app.told_old = true;
                        app.toast(
                            ToastKind::Info,
                            "The running Klip is older than this program: restart it to unlock copy-back and clear history",
                        );
                    }
                    app.hello = HelloState::Old;
                }
                Err(_) => {} // no answer says nothing about the protocol; asked again after reconnecting
            }
        }
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Add the idle-cost measurement** — append to `tui_pty.rs`:

```rust
#[test]
#[ignore = "manual measurement: run in release mode, takes KLIP_IDLE_SECS (default 300)"]
fn tui_idle_cost() {
    let secs: u64 = std::env::var("KLIP_IDLE_SECS").ok().and_then(|v| v.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(300);
    let (_d, mut p) = running();
    assert!(p.wait_for("ACTIVE", 15));
    let pid = p.child.process_id().expect("pid");
    let read = |pid: u32| -> (u64, u64) {
        let status = std::fs::read_to_string(format!("/proc/{pid}/status")).unwrap();
        let rss_kib = status.lines().find(|l| l.starts_with("VmRSS:")).and_then(|l| l.split_whitespace().nth(1)).and_then(|v| v.parse().ok()).unwrap_or(0);
        let stat = std::fs::read_to_string(format!("/proc/{pid}/stat")).unwrap();
        let after = stat.rsplit(')').next().unwrap().split_whitespace().collect::<Vec<_>>();
        let ticks: u64 = after[11].parse::<u64>().unwrap_or(0) + after[12].parse::<u64>().unwrap_or(0); // utime + stime
        (rss_kib, ticks)
    };
    let (rss0, t0) = read(pid);
    std::thread::sleep(std::time::Duration::from_secs(secs));
    let (rss1, t1) = read(pid);
    let hz = 100.0; // USER_HZ on Linux
    println!("TUI idle for {secs} s: RSS {rss0} KiB -> {rss1} KiB, CPU {:.2} s ({:.3} % of one core)", (t1 - t0) as f64 / hz, (t1 - t0) as f64 / hz / secs as f64 * 100.0);
    p.send(b"q");
    assert_eq!(p.wait_exit(5), Some(0));
}
```

1. **Spec coverage** — §1 goals: tasks 1, 5, 14 (CLI unchanged: goldens), 18 (real daemons), 13/14 (terminal restore), 11/14 (v0.3.6 compatibility via Hello/Old), 7 (sanitize). §4 architecture: 6, 10, 11, 13. §4.3 terminal safety: 13, 14. §4.4 data safety: 7, 12, 16, 19. §5 screens: 12 (Home, Devices), 15 (Pair), 16 (History), 17 (Send), 19 (Log), 20 (Settings); §5.1 keys and the text-focus rule: 11, 17. §6 timing: 11, 15, 16, 19. §7 protocol: 2, 3, 4, 5. §8 security: 7, 9, 15, 20. §9 compatibility matrix: 2 (golden bytes), 14 and 22 (installed v0.3.6 daemon), 5 (stub). §10 verification levels: unit (every task), key fuzz (14 + extended in 15–20), snapshots (12, 15–17, 19, 20), daemon e2e (4, 18), protocol (2, 5), PTY (14–19), manual (14, 22), regression (every gate). §11 milestones: Phase A–D (reordered, stated at the top). §12 risks: each row has a task. §13 deps: 6.
2. **Placeholder scan** — the only fill-ins are the `<paste …>` verification lines of commit messages (to be written from real output, as the repository's ledger rule requires) and the `{{…}}` README fields of Task 22 Step 4, which Step 4 forbids from remaining (`grep`). No step says "TBD", "handle errors appropriately" or "similar to Task N".
3. **Type consistency** — names used across tasks: `DaemonApi` methods (Task 10 = Task 14 impl = FakeDaemon); `Effect`/`Reply` variants are added in the task that first needs them and mirrored in `run_effect` and `failed_reply` every time; `Modal::PairApproval` (15), `ConfirmAction::{ClearHistory (16), RestartDaemon, ResetConfig (20)}`; `AppState` fields added: `devices` (12), `pair`, `invert_qr` (15), `history` (16), `send` (17), `log`, `log_path` (19), `settings` (20), `told_old`, `told_version` (21); `Msg::Tick { mono_ms, wall_ms }` everywhere; `tab_layout(width, active)` (11) used by drawing and the mouse.
4. **Review Focus** — mapped in the header to the tasks that pin them; each of those tasks contains the named test (hostile text: 7, 12, 16 (incl. PTY), 19; daemon dies mid-modal: 11, 15, 18; broken files: 9, 16, 19, 20; odd terminals: 12, 13, 14; foreign or stale pairing: 15, 18).
